# B2 Core (Tenancy) Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (- [ ]) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Identity gets tenants, memberships, invitations and an append-only audit log. Registration creates a
personal workspace. Every session and every access token belongs to exactly one active tenant (`tid`) and carries
that tenant's roles and the staff marker. Users switch tenants; platform staff switch in (audited, short-lived) and
out. The request principal sits in CLS at every edge. `libs/auth` keeps only the RBAC mechanism; each domain
declares its own permissions and default grants.

**Architecture:** Three waves, six lanes (section "Lane and files"). Wave 0/1: Lane P (principal kernel in
`@platform/common`, open RBAC vocabulary in `@platform/auth` and the four domains) ∥ Lane S (identity tables,
repositories, register/login/refresh learn the tenant; sole owner of the identity migration set). Wave 2: Lane T
alone (tokens switch to `tid` + tenant roles + `staff`, users lose global roles, the user directory becomes
tenant-scoped, `@Roles`/`@StaffOnly`). Wave 3: Lane F (switch tenant, staff switch-in and switch-out) ∥ Lane C (CLS
principal at every edge, token docs) ∥ Lane O (`staff:grant`). RLS is not enforced (B3). Every new table is
Citus-ready (`// citus: <kind>`, distribution column first in the PK and every unique) but is not distributed
(SH-5 does that right after Task 11).

**Tech Stack:** NestJS 12 (catalog `@nestjs/core` 12.1.2), Fastify 5, Drizzle ORM 0.45.4 + drizzle-kit 0.31.11, nestjs-cls 7.0.1, passport-jwt,
`@nestjs/cqrs`, Apollo/`@nestjs/graphql`, gRPC (ts-proto, buf), zod 4, Vitest 5, Testcontainers Citus
(`startTestPostgres()` from `@platform/testing/postgres`).

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-12-domains-tenancy-rls-program-design.md` (§4, §5.1-§5.3, §6, §7, §9, §13,
§16); `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-09-sharding-citus-pgbouncer-design.md` (§2.1, §2.3);
`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-09-stripe-ledger-immudb-design.md` (`PLATFORM_TENANT_ID`). Inventory:
`docs/superpowers/plans/program-work/inventory.md` (B2-1..B2-5, B2-7..B2-11, B2-15, B2-18..B2-22, AUDIT-F14, F16,
F26, FU-7). Conventions: `docs/superpowers/plans/program-work/conventions.md`.

## Executive summary

- 19 tasks, six lanes, four waves; one signed commit per task. Interfaces I-1..I-12 are the B2 contract that the
  edge plan (`2026-11-09-b2-edge.md`), B3, B4 and C wave 2 consume; this plan did not change them.
- Wave 0/1: Lane P (1 principal kernel in `@platform/common`; 2 open RBAC vocabulary, `RbacModule.forFeature`, the
  libs vocabulary guard loses its `libs/auth/` exemption) ∥ Lane S (3-9: `tenants`, `memberships` + `tenant_role`,
  `invitations`, append-only `audit_events` + `TenantAuditLog`, personal workspace on register, tenant-bound
  sessions with refresh keeping the tenant, login with an optional `tenantId` on REST/GraphQL/gRPC). Lane S is
  the sole writer of identity migrations `0001`-`0005`.
- Wave 2, Lane T alone (10-13): access tokens carry `tid` (required, never the nil UUID), tenant `roles` and
  `staff`; `req.user.tenantId`/`staff`; `GET /v1/auth/me` shows them; `billing:read-all` and `files:manage` are
  granted to nobody until their data is tenant-scoped (B2-26, B4-3). Task 11 drops global roles (`0006`
  `is_platform_staff`, `0007` drops `roles` + `user_role`), `PATCH /v1/users/:id/roles`, `updateUserRoles`,
  `users:manage-roles` and `Role`; the `UpdateUserRoles` RPC stays (FAILED_PRECONDITION `GLOBAL_ROLES_REMOVED`).
  Task 12 scopes the user directory to the active tenant (404 outside it; `GetUsersByIds` stays unscoped until
  B2-25). Task 13: `@Roles` applies owner ⊇ admin ⊇ member and is documented for owner-only rules (F14);
  `@StaffOnly`/`StaffOnlyGuard` (403 `STAFF_ONLY`). Merge Lane T only after Task 12 (owners would otherwise list
  every user). SH-5 may distribute tables once Lane T has merged.
- Wave 3: Lane F (14 switch tenant: refresh rotation into another membership + old access token denylisted, staff
  tokens refused; 15 staff switch-in: fresh `is_platform_staff` read, admin + staff access token for
  `STAFF_SWITCH_IN_TTL_SEC`, no refresh token, `staff.switch_in` audit row; 16 switch-out behind `@StaffOnly`) ∥
  Lane C (17 principal in CLS: `JwtAuthGuard`, `PrincipalContextInterceptor` for Socket.IO/graphql-ws, login as a
  user-scoped principal, `SessionTokensService.open`; 18 SECURITY.md/API.md token docs pinned by a spec, F26) ∥
  Lane O (19 `staff:grant <email> [--revoke]` over `DATABASE_MIGRATION_URL`, else `DATABASE_URL`).
- Review Focus (tenant selection abuse, hostile claims, slug inputs, RBAC vocabulary drift, staff switch-in abuse)
  each has named tests in Tasks 2, 3, 7, 8, 9, 10, 14, 15, 16.
- Every task: TDD steps with full code, exact commands, `bun run check`, proto breaking check after proto edits,
  `bun run db:check` + a no-op `drizzle-kit generate` after migrations, commitlint-checked message.
- Dependencies: `nestjs-cls` → `libs/common` (Task 1) and `libs/auth` (Task 17); `postgres` → `domains/identity`
  (Task 19); catalog versions only; the controller runs `bun install` (Tasks 17/19 lock conflict: rerun it).
- Must precede B2 execution: G Tasks 1-4 (G-11 export map), C wave 1 (C1-C7), S0 (SH-1..SH-4), E Tasks 1-2.
- Open decisions recorded in Self-review: staff:grant URL fallback; nil tenant on switch-in is 404; scoped user
  reads bypass the cache; payment-user e2e queries own payments while `billing:read-all` is withheld.
- Reviewed 2026-11-10 (part 1: header to Task 9): fixes in place, findings in "Plan review, part 1" at the end;
  Interfaces unchanged.

## Global Constraints

- **Node.** Prefix every command (tooling, tests, `git commit`) with
  `export NVM_DIR="$HOME/.nvm"; . "$NVM_DIR/nvm.sh" >/dev/null; nvm use 24.4.0 >/dev/null;` and run it from the
  worktree root. Under Node 20 the pre-commit hook cannot load `lint-staged.config.ts`.
- **Upstream state.** Write and run every step against `feat/domains-tenancy` after these merges: G Tasks 1-4
  (G-11: `src/api/index.ts` entries, `IdentityApiModule.forLocal()` without `IdentityCoreModule`, `TokenIssuer` in
  `@domain/identity/testing`, `makeAuthUser` only in `@platform/auth/testing`, `check-api-subpaths --require-all`),
  C wave 1 (C1-C7: `@RateLimit(IDENTITY_RATE_LIMITS.CREDENTIALS)` on register/login, `IpThrottlerModule` before
  `AuthModule`), S0 (SH-1..SH-4: `startTestPostgres()` on `citusdata/citus:14.2.0`, platform migration `0001`), E
  Tasks 1-2 (`bun run db:check`, `scripts/libs-vocabulary.spec.ts`). Line numbers in this plan are hints from
  `9600510`; match every edit on the quoted text.
- **Worktrees and merges.** One git worktree per lane under the session scratchpad (never inside the repo), cut
  from `feat/domains-tenancy`. Branches: `b2/platform` (Lane P), `b2/schema` (Lane S), `b2/tokens` (Lane T),
  `b2/flows` (Lane F), `b2/context` (Lane C), `b2/ops` (Lane O). The controller previews each merge with
  `git merge-tree --write-tree feat/domains-tenancy <branch>`, merges one lane at a time with
  `git merge --no-ff -S`, subject `chore(repo): merge <branch> — <summary>`, and runs `bun run check`, `bun run test`
  and `bun run db:check` on the result. Never push, never open a PR, never merge to `master`.
- **Commits.** One GPG-signed commit per task (`git commit -S`), never `--no-verify`. **No attribution lines** (no
  `Co-Authored-By`, no other trailer); the program decision of 2026-11-09 overrides `conventions.md` §1.2/§4.2.
  Header `<type>(<scope>[,<scope>]): <subject>`, at most 100 characters, subject starting lower-case, no trailing
  period; body lines at most 100 characters. Types: `build chore ci docs feat fix perf refactor revert style test`.
  Scopes used here: `common`, `auth`, `identity`, `identity-contracts`, `billing`, `files`, `notifications`,
  `monolith`, `gateway`, `identity-service`, `bootstrap`, `observability`, `graphql`, `docs`, `repo`. `b2`,
  `tenancy`, `rbac`, `scripts` are NOT scopes. Every commit step writes the message to `$TMPDIR/b2-msg.txt`, checks
  it with `bunx commitlint < "$TMPDIR/b2-msg.txt"` (no `✖`), then runs `git commit -S -F "$TMPDIR/b2-msg.txt"`.
- **Search.** `rg` and `fd`; `jaq` for JSON. `grep`, `find` and `jq` are blocked by a hook.
- **Tests.** Unit: `npx vitest run --project <pkg> <path filter>` (projects are folder names: `common`, `auth`,
  `identity`, `identity-contracts`, `billing`, `files`, `notifications`, `observability`, `bootstrap`, `scripts`).
  Integration: `INTEGRATION=1 npx vitest run --project identity:int <filter>` (Docker; Citus through
  `startTestPostgres()`; sandbox off). App e2e: `npx vitest run --project monolith:e2e` / `gateway:e2e` /
  `identity-service:e2e` (sandbox off: they bind loopback ports). Full: `bun run test` (sandbox off).
- **Gates before every commit.** `bun run check` (biome, eslint `--max-warnings=0`, prettier, tsc, boundaries,
  api-subpaths). After a migration task: `bun run db:check` passes and `bun run db:generate` leaves
  `git status --porcelain -- domains/identity/src/infrastructure/persistence/migrations` empty. After a proto task:
  `bun run proto:lint && bun run proto:gen` leave no diff in `domains/identity/contracts/src/generated` after the
  commit's own regeneration, and identity.v1 stays additive (add fields and RPCs; retire with a comment; never
  delete, renumber or retype; no `deprecated` options, see I-9): when E Task 5 has merged, run
  `node scripts/check-proto-breaking.mjs --against feat/domains-tenancy` → exit 0; otherwise run E-5's verified
  `git archive` form (buf's own `.git#branch=…` input cannot read a worktree, whose `.git` is a file):
  `rm -rf "$TMPDIR/proto-base" && mkdir -p "$TMPDIR/proto-base" && git archive feat/domains-tenancy domains/identity/contracts/src/proto | tar -x -C "$TMPDIR/proto-base" && (cd domains/identity/contracts && npx buf breaking --config ../../../libs/contracts/buf.yaml --against "$TMPDIR/proto-base/domains/identity/contracts" --against-config ../../../libs/contracts/buf.yaml)`
  → exit 0. After an env change: `node scripts/check-env-example.mjs`.
- **Formatting.** After editing TypeScript run `npx biome check --write <files>`; after Markdown/YAML run
  `npx prettier --write <files>`.
- **Layering.** `apps → domains → contracts → libs` (`scripts/check-boundaries.mjs`); `libs/` names no domain, also
  in specs (`scripts/libs-vocabulary.spec.ts`: use `orders`, `catalog`, `alerts`, `shipments`, never `identity`,
  `billing`, `notifications`, `payment(s)`, `stripe`, `checkout`). Export placement follows the G-11 rule: ports,
  presentation, adapters, edge constants and permission vocabularies → `src/api/index.ts`; commands, handlers,
  domain types and errors, persistence, Core modules, domain config namespaces → root `src/index.ts` only; fakes
  and test signers → `src/testing/index.ts`. A file reachable from `src/api/index.ts` never imports a root-only
  file.
- **Migrations (identity set, Lane S then Lane T only).** Stacked numbered files: `0001_tenants` (Task 3),
  `0002_memberships` (Task 4), `0003_invitations` (Task 5), `0004_audit_events` (Task 6), `0005_tenant_sessions`
  (Task 8), `0006_platform_staff` and `0007_drop_user_roles` (Task 11). Generate with
  `(cd domains/identity && npx drizzle-kit generate --name <name>)` (offline: no database needed); commit the SQL and
  `meta/`. Never edit a committed migration; a freshly generated, uncommitted file may be hand-edited only where a
  task says so. If drizzle-kit asks an interactive question, stop: the schema edit is bigger than the task says.
  Each table carries a `// citus: reference|tenant|user` comment; PK and every unique start with the distribution
  column; no FK crosses a domain schema; no distribution call (SH-5 adds them after Task 11). Next free identity
  index after this plan: `0008`.
- **Dependencies.** Task 1 adds `"nestjs-cls": "catalog:"` (7.0.1) to `libs/common`; Task 17 adds the same to
  `libs/auth`; Task 19 adds `"postgres": "catalog:"` (3.4.9) to `domains/identity`. The task stops after editing
  `package.json`; the controller runs `bun install` and the task commits `bun.lock` with its change. No new catalog
  versions. Tasks 17 and 19 run in parallel lanes: when the second of `b2/context` / `b2/ops` merges, resolve a
  `bun.lock` conflict by keeping both `package.json` changes and rerunning `bun install` (never hand-merge the lock).
- **Code style.** Relative imports end in `.js`; no `any`, no `console.log` (Nest `Logger`; `console.info`/`error`
  only in the CLI); `override` on subclass members; `T[]` arrays; ids from `generateId()` (uuidv7); every import
  declared in that package's `package.json`; injected classes imported as values.
- **Docker.** Never touch the developer's containers or the `boilerplate` compose project. Testcontainers pick
  random ports.
- **C contract (kept by every task).** `req.user.id` / `socket.data.user.id` stay the user id; `req.user.tenantId`
  is the active tenant (Task 10); credential endpoints keep `@RateLimit(IDENTITY_RATE_LIMITS.CREDENTIALS)` with the
  identifier in `email`; credential handlers throw on failure (a 403 for a non-member `tenantId` is a failure); the
  global guard order stays `IpThrottlerModule` → `AuthModule` → `AppThrottlerModule`.
- **E contract.** The smoke journey `POST /v1/auth/register` → 201 with `accessToken`, then `GET /v1/auth/me` → 200
  with the same `email` keeps working after every task.

## Review Focus

Five input classes that would bite users and that no happy-path test exercises. Each has a named test in its
owning task.

1. **Tenant selection with a tenant the caller may not use.** Login or switch with a `tenantId` the user is not a
   member of, the nil UUID (`PLATFORM_TENANT_ID`), an unknown UUID, a malformed id, and a refresh whose membership
   was removed after login. Expected: 403 `TENANT_ACCESS_DENIED` (identical for "unknown tenant" and "not a
   member"), 400 for a malformed id, 401 `MEMBERSHIP_REVOKED` on refresh, and never a token for that tenant.
   Tests: Task 8 (`tenant-selection.service.spec.ts`, refresh), Task 9 (REST, GraphQL, gRPC), Task 14 (switch).
2. **Hostile or legacy access-token claims.** `tid` missing, not a UUID or the nil UUID; `staff: 'true'` or `1`;
   `roles` not an array or with unknown values; a pre-B2 token without `tid`. Expected: 401 `INVALID_TOKEN` with a
   `reason`, unknown roles dropped, `staff` true only for the JSON boolean. Test: Task 10 `token-claims.spec.ts`.
3. **Slug derivation inputs.** Emails whose local part slugs to nothing (`+++@x.io`), non-ASCII (`Zoë.Ünal@x.io`),
   upper case, 200 characters, and two users with the same local part on different domains (`ada@a.io`,
   `ada@b.io`). Expected: valid, distinct slugs that match `TENANT_SLUG_PATTERN`; register never answers 409 for
   them. Tests: Task 3 `tenant-slug.spec.ts`, Task 7 int spec and monolith e2e.
4. **Drift in the open RBAC vocabulary.** `@RequirePermissions('orders:raed')` (undeclared), a grant of an
   undeclared permission, two features declaring the same permission, a malformed name, and the same feature
   registered twice (Api module plus a test module). Expected: boot fails with the offending names; identical
   re-registration is a no-op. Tests: Task 2 `rbac.registry.spec.ts`, `rbac-vocabulary.check.spec.ts`.
5. **Staff switch-in abuse.** A non-staff caller, a staff user whose flag was revoked after login, an unknown tenant,
   the nil UUID, a blank or 1 001-character reason, a switch-in token used for `POST /v1/auth/refresh` or
   `switch-tenant`, and `switch-out` with a normal token. Expected: 403 `STAFF_ONLY`, 404, 400, 401/403 as
   specified, and an `audit_events` row for every successful switch-in and switch-out. Tests: Task 15, Task 16.

## Interfaces (B2 contract)

Authoritative for the B2 edge plan (`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-09-b2-edge.md`), B3, B4 and C wave 2. Final as
of the "Self-review" section. "Task N" names the core task that ships the symbol.

### I-1. Tenant roles, platform tenant, principal (`@platform/common` root; Task 1)

```ts
// libs/common/src/tenancy/tenant-role.ts
export const TENANT_ROLES = ['owner', 'admin', 'member'] as const; // canonical order
export type TenantRole = (typeof TENANT_ROLES)[number];
export function isTenantRole(value: unknown): value is TenantRole;
/** owner ⊇ admin ⊇ member: the roles `role` implies, itself first. */
export function impliedTenantRoles(role: TenantRole): readonly TenantRole[];
/** True when any of `held` implies `required` (owner satisfies admin and member). */
export function satisfiesTenantRole(held: readonly TenantRole[], required: TenantRole): boolean;
/** Unknown values dropped, duplicates removed, canonical order. */
export function normalizeTenantRoles(values: readonly unknown[]): TenantRole[];

// libs/common/src/tenancy/platform-tenant.ts
/** The nil UUID: tenant_id of platform-level rows. Never a tenant, never a runtime principal's tenant. */
export const PLATFORM_TENANT_ID = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000';
export function isPlatformTenantId(value: unknown): boolean;

// libs/common/src/tenancy/principal.ts
export interface Principal {
  readonly userId: string;
  /** null only in user-scoped units of work (login before the tenant is chosen). */
  readonly tenantId: string | null;
  /** Roles in `tenantId`; always [] when tenantId is null. */
  readonly roles: readonly TenantRole[];
  /** true only for a staff switch-in token (always with a tenant). */
  readonly staff: boolean;
}
export interface PrincipalInput {
  readonly userId: string;
  readonly tenantId: string | null;
  readonly roles?: readonly unknown[];
  readonly staff?: boolean;
}
export const TenancyErrorCode = {
  INVALID_PRINCIPAL: 'INVALID_PRINCIPAL',
  PRINCIPAL_MISSING: 'PRINCIPAL_MISSING',
} as const;
export type TenancyErrorCode = (typeof TenancyErrorCode)[keyof typeof TenancyErrorCode];
/**
 * Validated, frozen principal. userId: UUID, not nil. tenantId: UUID or null, never PLATFORM_TENANT_ID.
 * roles: normalizeTenantRoles. tenantId null ⇒ roles [] and staff false.
 * @throws UnauthenticatedException code INVALID_PRINCIPAL, details.reason
 */
export function createPrincipal(input: PrincipalInput): Principal;
export function isPrincipal(value: unknown): value is Principal;

// libs/common/src/tenancy/principal-context.ts (nestjs-cls ClsServiceManager: the same store as DI ClsService)
export const CLS_PRINCIPAL: unique symbol; // Symbol('app:principal')
export function getPrincipal(): Principal | undefined; // undefined outside a CLS context or when unset
/** @throws UnauthenticatedException code PRINCIPAL_MISSING */
export function requirePrincipal(): Principal;
/** @throws Error when no CLS context is active (the caller must run inside one). */
export function setPrincipal(principal: Principal): void;
/** Runs fn in a child CLS context (inherits the parent store) with `principal` set. */
export function runWithPrincipal<T>(principal: Principal, fn: () => T): T;
```

### I-2. Who writes `CLS_PRINCIPAL`

| Edge                                                        | Writer                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | Task       |
| ----------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ---------- |
| HTTP REST and GraphQL over HTTP                             | `JwtAuthGuard`, after authentication, **only when a CLS context is active** (the `ClsMiddleware` opened it). Later root fields of the same GraphQL operation skip passport (`isAlreadyAuthenticated`) and share the same HTTP store, so the first field's write stands               | 17         |
| Socket.IO messages, graphql-ws operations                   | `PrincipalContextInterceptor` (an `APP_INTERCEPTOR` of `AuthModule` when `globalGuards` is on): observability's `RequestContextInterceptor` opens their CLS context only after the guards ran. Apps import `ObservabilityModule` before `AuthModule`, so it is the outer interceptor | 17         |
| Login (after the password check)                            | `runWithPrincipal({ userId, tenantId: null })` in `LoginHandler` around tenant selection                                                                                                                                                                                             | 17         |
| Register, login, refresh, switch (once a session is opened) | `SessionTokensService.open()` sets `{ userId, tenantId, roles, staff: false }` when a context is active                                                                                                                                                                              | 17         |
| gRPC services                                               | `GrpcContextInterceptor` from `x-user-id`, `x-tenant-id`, `x-user-roles`, `x-staff`                                                                                                                                                                                                  | edge B2-23 |
| Kafka consumers                                             | envelope `actorId` + `tenantId`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | edge B2-24 |
| Crons, CLIs, relays                                         | `SystemContext.run(fn)`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | B3-12      |

Writer rules (every writer, core and edge): write only inside an active CLS context (`ClsServiceManager
.getClsService().isActive()`), never open one at the edge (that would hide the request id that
`RequestContextInterceptor` sets); `PrincipalContextInterceptor` writes only when `getPrincipal()` is still
`undefined` (idempotent); a writer never replaces a principal with one of another user. `setPrincipal()` throws
outside a context, so edge writers call the internal `writePrincipal(user)` helper (Task 17), which checks
`isActive()` first.

`RequestContextService.userId` (observability) returns `getPrincipal()?.userId` first, then the legacy
`CLS_USER_ID` (still set by the gRPC interceptor until B2-23 retires it) (Task 17).
`principalOf(user: AuthUser): Principal` is exported by `@platform/auth` (Task 17).

### I-3. Access-token claims and `AuthUser` (`@platform/auth` root; Task 10)

```ts
export interface AccessTokenClaims {
  sub: string; // user id
  email: string; // documented in Task 18 (F26)
  tid: string; // active tenant: UUID, never PLATFORM_TENANT_ID; required (a token without it is INVALID_TOKEN)
  roles: TenantRole[]; // roles in `tid`; unknown values dropped
  staff: boolean; // wire: the claim `staff: true` exists only on switch-in tokens; anything but `true` absent → false
  jti: string;
  typ: 'access';
  iat: number;
  exp: number;
  iss: string;
  aud: string;
}
/** `req.user` (HTTP/GraphQL) and `socket.data.user` (Socket.IO). */
export interface AuthUser {
  id: string;
  email: string;
  tenantId: string; // C wave 2 reads exactly this field
  roles: TenantRole[];
  staff: boolean;
  /** Resolved at verification by RbacRegistry.resolve(roles); never in the token. */
  permissions: string[];
  jti: string;
  exp: number;
}
export interface AccessTokenSubject {
  id: string;
  email: string;
  tenantId: string;
  roles: readonly TenantRole[];
  staff?: boolean;
}
export function parseAccessTokenClaims(payload: unknown): AccessTokenClaims; // @throws UnauthenticatedException INVALID_TOKEN
export function toAuthUser(claims: AccessTokenClaims, permissions: readonly string[]): AuthUser; // Task 2 signature
export function isAuthUser(value: unknown): value is AuthUser;
// TokenService keeps its one-argument method (Task 2): it resolves permissions through RbacRegistry, so
// socket-auth.ts and @platform/graphql's subscription-auth.ts need no change.
class TokenService {
  toAuthUser(claims: AccessTokenClaims): AuthUser;
}
// @platform/auth/testing (Task 10)
export const TEST_TENANT_ID = '0190f3b2-0000-7000-8000-00000000c0de';
export type TestAccessTokenSubject = Omit<AccessTokenSubject, 'tenantId'> & { tenantId?: string }; // default TEST_TENANT_ID
export function signTestAccessToken(
  subject: TestAccessTokenSubject,
  options?: SignTestAccessTokenOptions,
): Promise<IssuedAccessToken>;
export function makeAuthUser(overrides?: Partial<AuthUser>, rbac?: RbacRegistry): AuthUser; // roles ['member'], tenantId TEST_TENANT_ID, staff false
```

The refresh token is unchanged (`sub`, `jti` = session id, `typ: 'refresh'`); its tenant is the session row's
`tenant_id`. Staff switch-in tokens: `roles: ['admin']`, `staff: true`, TTL `STAFF_SWITCH_IN_TTL_SEC`, no refresh
token, no session row. Until Task 10 merges, tokens carry the legacy global roles and Task 2's internal
`legacyTenantRoles()` maps them for permission resolution: `user` → `member`, `moderator` → `member`, `admin` →
`admin`. (`moderator` → `member` is least privilege for the transition: `admin` would hand moderators
`users:manage-roles`, a self-promotion path. No membership data is migrated, so spec §6's "moderator folds into
admin" has nothing left to fold once Task 11 drops global roles.) Task 10 deletes `legacyTenantRoles`; Task 11
deletes `Role`, `ROLE_VALUES` and `isRole` together with identity's global roles (identity's presentation types its
global `roles` with them until then).

### I-4. RBAC mechanism (`@platform/auth` root; Task 2, Task 13)

```ts
// libs/auth/src/rbac/rbac-feature.ts
export const PERMISSION_PATTERN: RegExp; // /^[a-z][a-z0-9-]*:[a-z][a-z0-9-]*$/
export function isPermissionName(value: unknown): value is string;
export interface RbacFeature {
  /** Owner of the vocabulary (a domain's name); used in error messages only. */
  readonly name: string;
  readonly permissions: readonly string[];
  /** Default grants; cumulative through owner ⊇ admin ⊇ member. */
  readonly grants: Readonly<Partial<Record<TenantRole, readonly string[]>>>;
}
export function defineRbacFeature<const TPermission extends string>(feature: {
  name: string;
  permissions: readonly TPermission[];
  grants: Partial<Record<TenantRole, readonly NoInfer<TPermission>[]>>;
}): RbacFeature; // frozen copy; validates names (throws Error)

// libs/auth/src/rbac/rbac.registry.ts
@Injectable()
export class RbacRegistry {
  /** Idempotent for a deep-equal feature; throws on a malformed or undeclared permission, a permission declared by another feature, or a redefinition of the same name. */
  register(feature: RbacFeature): void;
  isDeclared(permission: string): boolean;
  /** Every declared permission, sorted. */
  permissions(): string[];
  /** Union of the grants of every implied role, sorted; unknown roles grant nothing. */
  resolve(roles: readonly TenantRole[]): string[];
}

// libs/auth/src/rbac/rbac.module.ts
export class RbacModule {
  /** Global: RbacRegistry + RbacVocabularyCheck. Imported and re-exported by AuthModule.forRootAsync(). */
  static forRoot(): DynamicModule;
  /** Registers one domain vocabulary into the global registry at instantiation. */
  static forFeature(feature: RbacFeature): DynamicModule;
}
/** OnApplicationBootstrap: every @RequirePermissions/@RequireAnyPermission name on a controller or provider must be declared, else boot fails listing them. */
export class RbacVocabularyCheck {}

// decorators and guards (strings instead of the deleted Permission enum)
export const RequirePermissions: (...permissions: string[]) => CustomDecorator<string>;
export const RequireAnyPermission: (...permissions: string[]) => CustomDecorator<string>;
export function hasPermissions(
  granted: readonly string[],
  required: readonly string[],
  mode?: PermissionMatchMode,
): boolean;
// Task 13
export const Roles: (...roles: TenantRole[]) => CustomDecorator<string>; // any listed role, hierarchy applied
export const STAFF_ONLY_KEY = 'auth:staff-only';
export const StaffOnly: () => MethodDecorator & ClassDecorator; // SetMetadata + UseGuards(StaffOnlyGuard)
export class StaffOnlyGuard {} // 403 STAFF_ONLY unless req.user.staff === true
export const RbacErrorCode = { STAFF_ONLY: 'STAFF_ONLY' } as const;
// @platform/auth/testing (Task 2)
export function createTestRbacRegistry(...features: readonly RbacFeature[]): RbacRegistry;
```

Registration mechanics: `RbacModule.forFeature(feature)` returns `{ module: RbacModule, providers: [{ provide:
RBAC_FEATURE_REGISTRATION (module-local symbol), inject: [RbacRegistry], useFactory: (r) => (r.register(feature),
feature) }] }`. Nest 12 identifies dynamic modules by reference, so the same feature imported by two modules
registers twice: `register` is a no-op for a deep-equal feature. Every module that imports `forFeature` needs the
global `RbacModule.forRoot()` (it comes with `AuthModule.forRootAsync()`); a test module that declares a
controller or resolver carrying `@RequirePermissions` must import the owning feature or boot fails in
`RbacVocabularyCheck` with `RBAC vocabulary check failed: undeclared permission(s) <names> (<Class>.<method>)`.

Deleted from `@platform/auth`: `Permission`, `PERMISSION_VALUES`, `isPermission`, `ROLE_PERMISSIONS`,
`resolvePermissions` (Task 2); `legacyTenantRoles` (Task 10); `Role`, `ROLE_VALUES`, `isRole` (Task 11).
`scripts/libs-vocabulary.spec.ts` loses its `libs/auth/` exemption in Task 2.

### I-5. Permission vocabularies and default grants (Task 2; `users:manage-roles` removed in Task 11)

Each domain exports `<X>Permission` (a `const` object) and `<X>_RBAC = defineRbacFeature(...)` from
`domains/<x>/src/<x>.permissions.ts` through its `./api` entry, and imports `RbacModule.forFeature(<X>_RBAC)` in
its Api module (`IdentityApiModule`, `BillingApiModule`, `NotificationsApiModule`: both `forLocal()` and
`forRemote()`; `FilesModule`). B2-23 (edge) adds the same import to the Grpc modules and `RbacModule.forRoot()` to
the gRPC-only services.

| Domain        | Constant                              | Permission                                             | member | admin                      | owner     |
| ------------- | ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------ | ------ | -------------------------- | --------- |
| identity      | `IdentityPermission.UsersRead`        | `users:read`                                           |        | ✓                          | (implied) |
| identity      | `IdentityPermission.UsersManageRoles` | `users:manage-roles` (Task 2 only; Task 11 deletes it) |        | ✓                          | (implied) |
| identity      | `IdentityPermission.MembersManage`    | `members:manage`                                       |        | ✓                          | (implied) |
| identity      | `IdentityPermission.TenantManage`     | `tenant:manage`                                        |        |                            | ✓         |
| billing       | `BillingPermission.Checkout`          | `billing:checkout`                                     | ✓      | (implied)                  | (implied) |
| billing       | `BillingPermission.ReadAll`           | `billing:read-all`                                     |        | Tasks 2-9 only (see below) | (implied) |
| notifications | `NotificationsPermission.Read`        | `notifications:read`                                   | ✓      | (implied)                  | (implied) |
| notifications | `NotificationsPermission.Write`       | `notifications:write`                                  |        | ✓                          | (implied) |
| files         | `FilesPermission.Read`                | `files:read`                                           | ✓      | (implied)                  | (implied) |
| files         | `FilesPermission.Write`               | `files:write`                                          | ✓      | (implied)                  | (implied) |
| files         | `FilesPermission.Manage`              | `files:manage`                                         |        | Tasks 2-9 only (see below) | (implied) |

Staff switch-in tokens carry `admin`, so staff never get `tenant:manage`. Rules about who may change an owner's
roles belong to B2-13 (edge).

**Cross-tenant permissions are withheld from Task 10 on.** Today `billing:read-all` lists every user's payments
(`resolvePaymentsOwner` returns no owner) and `files:manage` opens every user's file (`resolveFileAccess` →
`manager`). Until Task 10 only legacy global admins hold them (status quo). From Task 10 every registrant is the
owner of a personal workspace, so Task 10 removes both from `BILLING_RBAC.grants.admin` and
`FILES_RBAC.grants.admin` (declared, granted to nobody). They come back with tenant-scoped data:
**B2-26 (edge) adds `BillingPermission.ReadAll` to `BILLING_RBAC.grants.admin` in the commit that scopes payment
listing to the active tenant; B4-3 adds `FilesPermission.Manage` to `FILES_RBAC.grants.admin` in the commit that
moves storage keys under `tenants/<tid>/`.** A spec in each `*.permissions.spec.ts` pins the withheld state.

### I-6. Identity tables (Drizzle; `domains/identity/src/infrastructure/persistence/identity.schema.ts`)

The schema file imports only `drizzle-orm` and dependency-free relative files. Role values come from
`domains/identity/src/domain/tenant-role.ts`
(`export const TENANT_ROLE_VALUES = ['owner', 'admin', 'member'] as const satisfies readonly TenantRole[];` with
`import type { TenantRole } from '@platform/common'`; a spec asserts it equals `TENANT_ROLES`). The nil-UUID literal
in the CHECK is asserted equal to `PLATFORM_TENANT_ID` by a spec.

```ts
export const tenantRole = identityPgSchema.enum('tenant_role', TENANT_ROLE_VALUES); // Task 4

// citus: reference (Task 3)
export const tenants = identityPgSchema.table(
  'tenants',
  {
    id: uuid()
      .primaryKey()
      .default(sql`uuidv7()`),
    slug: text().notNull().unique('tenants_slug_unique'),
    name: text().notNull(),
    personal: boolean().notNull().default(false),
    createdAt: timestamptz().notNull().defaultNow(),
  },
  () => [
    check('tenants_id_not_platform', sql`"id" <> '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000'::uuid`),
  ],
);

// citus: reference (Task 4)
export const memberships = identityPgSchema.table(
  'memberships',
  {
    tenantId: uuid()
      .notNull()
      .references(() => tenants.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
    userId: uuid()
      .notNull()
      .references(() => users.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
    roles: tenantRole().array().notNull(),
    lastActiveAt: timestamptz(),
    createdAt: timestamptz().notNull().defaultNow(),
  },
  (t) => [
    primaryKey({ name: 'memberships_tenant_id_user_id_pk', columns: [t.tenantId, t.userId] }),
    index('memberships_user_id_last_active_at_idx').on(t.userId, t.lastActiveAt.desc().nullsLast()),
    check('memberships_roles_not_empty', sql`cardinality("roles") > 0`),
  ],
);

// citus: tenant (Task 5)
export const invitations = identityPgSchema.table(
  'invitations',
  {
    tenantId: uuid()
      .notNull()
      .references(() => tenants.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
    id: uuid()
      .notNull()
      .default(sql`uuidv7()`),
    email: text().notNull(),
    roles: tenantRole().array().notNull(),
    tokenHash: text().notNull(),
    invitedBy: uuid()
      .notNull()
      .references(() => users.id),
    expiresAt: timestamptz().notNull(),
    acceptedAt: timestamptz(),
    createdAt: timestamptz().notNull().defaultNow(),
  },
  (t) => [
    primaryKey({ name: 'invitations_tenant_id_id_pk', columns: [t.tenantId, t.id] }),
    uniqueIndex('invitations_tenant_id_token_hash_uq').on(t.tenantId, t.tokenHash),
    index('invitations_token_hash_idx').on(t.tokenHash),
  ],
);

// citus: tenant (Task 6); append-only: no UPDATE/DELETE path in code (B3 revokes the grants)
export const auditEvents = identityPgSchema.table(
  'audit_events',
  {
    tenantId: uuid()
      .notNull()
      .references(() => tenants.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
    id: uuid()
      .notNull()
      .default(sql`uuidv7()`),
    actorId: uuid(), // no FK: the history outlives users
    type: text().notNull(),
    data: jsonb()
      .$type<Record<string, unknown>>()
      .notNull()
      .default(sql`'{}'::jsonb`),
    createdAt: timestamptz().notNull().defaultNow(),
  },
  (t) => [
    primaryKey({ name: 'audit_events_tenant_id_id_pk', columns: [t.tenantId, t.id] }),
    index('audit_events_tenant_id_created_at_idx').on(t.tenantId, t.createdAt.desc()),
  ],
);

// citus: user (Task 8): PK (user_id, id), tenant_id NOT NULL, sessions_user_id_idx dropped (the PK leads with user_id)
export const sessions = identityPgSchema.table(
  'sessions',
  {
    id: uuid().notNull(), // = the refresh token's jti, no default
    userId: uuid()
      .notNull()
      .references(() => users.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
    tenantId: uuid()
      .notNull()
      .references(() => tenants.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
    refreshTokenHash: text().notNull(),
    userAgent: text(),
    ip: text(),
    expiresAt: timestamptz().notNull(),
    revokedAt: timestamptz(),
    replacedById: uuid(),
    createdAt: timestamptz().notNull().defaultNow(),
  },
  (t) => [
    primaryKey({ name: 'sessions_user_id_id_pk', columns: [t.userId, t.id] }),
    index('sessions_expires_at_idx').on(t.expiresAt),
  ],
);

// citus: reference (Task 11): `is_platform_staff` added (0006), then `roles` and the `user_role` enum dropped (0007)
export const users = identityPgSchema.table(
  'users',
  {
    id: uuid()
      .primaryKey()
      .default(sql`uuidv7()`),
    email: text().notNull().unique('users_email_unique'),
    passwordHash: text().notNull(),
    displayName: text().notNull(),
    isPlatformStaff: boolean().notNull().default(false),
    createdAt: timestamptz().notNull().defaultNow(),
    updatedAt: timestamptz()
      .notNull()
      .defaultNow()
      .$onUpdate(() => new Date()),
  },
  (t) => [
    index('users_search_trgm_idx').using(
      'gin',
      t.email.op('gin_trgm_ops'),
      t.displayName.op('gin_trgm_ops'),
    ),
  ],
);

export const identitySchema = {
  tenantRole,
  users,
  sessions,
  tenants,
  memberships,
  invitations,
  auditEvents,
  usersRelations,
  sessionsRelations,
}; // after Task 11 (userRole removed)
```

`0005_tenant_sessions` starts with the hand-added statement `DELETE FROM "identity"."sessions";` (sessions are
disposable: everyone logs in again) so it applies to a database that already has sessions, and its
`DROP CONSTRAINT "sessions_pkey"` is written by hand (drizzle-kit 0.31 emits a commented placeholder for a dropped
column-level primary key). `scripts/check-drizzle-history.spec.ts` and `bun run db:check` compare snapshots only, so
hand edits to the SQL are safe. Users created before B2 have no membership; their next login creates a personal
workspace (I-8 `TenantSelection`). Task 11 is split in two generated files because drizzle-kit asks "created or
renamed?" (interactively) when one run drops `roles` and adds `is_platform_staff` on the same table.

Repository rules on these tables (Citus-ready, sharding design §2.1): every query on `invitations` and
`audit_events` carries `tenant_id = $tenantId` except the named system read `findByTokenHash`; every query on
`sessions` carries `user_id = $userId` except `deleteExpired` (system cron); no `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` on them; locks
are `pg_advisory_xact_lock` only.

### I-7. Identity repository ports (`domains/identity/src/application/persistence/`, root exports)

```ts
// tenants.repository.ts (Task 3)
export interface TenantRecord {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly slug: string;
  readonly name: string;
  readonly personal: boolean;
  readonly createdAt: Date;
}
export interface NewTenant {
  id: string;
  slug: string;
  name: string;
  personal: boolean;
}
export abstract class TenantsRepository {
  /** @throws TenantSlugTakenException on tenants_slug_unique */
  abstract insert(tenant: NewTenant): Promise<void>;
  abstract findById(id: string): Promise<TenantRecord | null>;
  /** One query; unknown ids omitted; order unspecified. */
  abstract findByIds(ids: readonly string[]): Promise<TenantRecord[]>;
  abstract existsBySlug(slug: string): Promise<boolean>;
  /** false when no row matched. */
  abstract rename(id: string, name: string): Promise<boolean>;
}

// memberships.repository.ts (Task 4)
export interface MembershipRecord {
  readonly tenantId: string;
  readonly userId: string;
  readonly roles: readonly TenantRole[];
  readonly lastActiveAt: Date | null;
  readonly createdAt: Date;
}
export interface NewMembership {
  tenantId: string;
  userId: string;
  roles: readonly TenantRole[];
  lastActiveAt: Date | null;
}
export interface TenantMembership extends MembershipRecord {
  readonly tenant: TenantRecord;
}
export abstract class MembershipsRepository {
  /** @throws MembershipAlreadyExistsException on the PK; InvalidRolesException when roles is empty */
  abstract insert(membership: NewMembership): Promise<void>;
  abstract find(tenantId: string, userId: string): Promise<MembershipRecord | null>;
  /** Latest last_active_at first (NULLs last), then newest created_at; null when the user has none. */
  abstract findMostRecentForUser(userId: string): Promise<MembershipRecord | null>;
  /** Every membership with its tenant, in the findMostRecentForUser order. */
  abstract listForUser(userId: string): Promise<TenantMembership[]>;
  /** Sets last_active_at (a replicated write on Citus: register, login and switch only, never refresh). */
  abstract touch(tenantId: string, userId: string, at: Date): Promise<void>;
}
// Edge additions (B2-13 implements them in the same files, Drizzle + in-memory + int spec):
//   abstract updateRoles(tenantId: string, userId: string, roles: readonly TenantRole[]): Promise<boolean>;
//   abstract delete(tenantId: string, userId: string): Promise<boolean>;
//   /** pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext('identity:tenant-owners:' || tenantId)), then count owners. */
//   abstract countOwnersLocked(tenantId: string): Promise<number>;
//   abstract listMembers(tenantId: string, criteria: { limit: number; cursor?: string }): Promise<CursorPage<MemberRecord>>;

// invitations.repository.ts (Task 5)
export interface InvitationRecord {
  readonly tenantId: string;
  readonly id: string;
  readonly email: string;
  readonly roles: readonly TenantRole[];
  readonly tokenHash: string;
  readonly invitedBy: string;
  readonly expiresAt: Date;
  readonly acceptedAt: Date | null;
  readonly createdAt: Date;
}
export interface NewInvitation {
  tenantId: string;
  id: string;
  email: string;
  roles: readonly TenantRole[];
  tokenHash: string;
  invitedBy: string;
  expiresAt: Date;
}
export abstract class InvitationsRepository {
  abstract insert(invitation: NewInvitation): Promise<void>;
  /** System method 3 (multi-shard read on Citus); B3-11 moves it into a *.system-repository.ts. */
  abstract findByTokenHash(tokenHash: string): Promise<InvitationRecord | null>;
  /** Not accepted and expires_at > now, newest first. */
  abstract listPending(tenantId: string, now: Date): Promise<InvitationRecord[]>;
  /** Conditional (accepted_at IS NULL AND expires_at > now); true when this call accepted it. */
  abstract markAccepted(tenantId: string, id: string, now: Date): Promise<boolean>;
}
// domain/invitation-token.ts (Task 5)
export const INVITATION_TTL_MS = 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1_000;
export const INVITATION_TOKEN_BYTES = 32;
export function generateInvitationToken(): { token: string; tokenHash: string }; // base64url(32 random bytes), sha256 hex
export function hashInvitationToken(token: string): string; // sha256 hex

// audit-events.repository.ts (Task 6)
export const AUDIT_EVENT_TYPES = {
  STAFF_SWITCH_IN: 'staff.switch_in', // Task 15
  STAFF_SWITCH_OUT: 'staff.switch_out', // Task 16
  MEMBER_ROLES_CHANGED: 'member.roles_changed', // edge B2-13
  MEMBER_REMOVED: 'member.removed', // edge B2-13
  MEMBER_LEFT: 'member.left', // edge B2-13
  INVITATION_CREATED: 'invitation.created', // edge B2-14
  INVITATION_ACCEPTED: 'invitation.accepted', // edge B2-14
  TENANT_RENAMED: 'tenant.renamed', // edge B2-12
} as const;
export type AuditEventType = (typeof AUDIT_EVENT_TYPES)[keyof typeof AUDIT_EVENT_TYPES];
export interface AuditEventRecord {
  readonly tenantId: string;
  readonly id: string;
  readonly actorId: string | null;
  readonly type: string;
  readonly data: Readonly<Record<string, unknown>>;
  readonly createdAt: Date;
}
export interface NewAuditEvent {
  tenantId: string;
  id: string;
  actorId: string | null;
  type: AuditEventType;
  data: Record<string, unknown>;
  createdAt: Date;
}
export abstract class AuditEventsRepository {
  abstract append(event: NewAuditEvent): Promise<void>;
  /** Keyset page on id within the tenant, newest first. */
  abstract listForTenant(
    tenantId: string,
    criteria: { limit: number; cursor?: string },
  ): Promise<CursorPage<AuditEventRecord>>;
}

// users.repository.ts changes
//   Task 11: UserSnapshot/UserRecord lose `roles`, gain `isPlatformStaff: boolean`; removed: findAggregate,
//            countAdmins, updateRoles; added: abstract isPlatformStaff(id: string): Promise<boolean>;
//   Task 12: ListUsersCriteria gains `tenantId: string` (list joins memberships);
//            added: abstract findByIdInTenant(id: string, tenantId: string): Promise<UserRecord | null>;
// sessions.repository.ts changes (Task 8): NewSession and SessionRecord gain `tenantId: string`;
//   findById(id) → findById(userId: string, id: string) (router query on Citus)
```

// application/services/tenant-audit-log.service.ts (Task 6)

### I-9. identity.v1 proto and edge surfaces (core owns everything below; the edge plan must not re-add it)

```proto
message AuthTokens { /* 1-5 unchanged */ string tenant_id = 6; repeated string tenant_roles = 7; } // Task 8
message LoginRequest { /* 1-3 unchanged */ optional string tenant_id = 4; } // Task 9
message User { /* … */ repeated string roles = 4; /* comment only: "Always empty since B2" (Task 11) */ }
rpc UpdateUserRoles(UpdateUserRolesRequest) returns (User); // Task 11: comment only; always FAILED_PRECONDITION GLOBAL_ROLES_REMOVED
// No `[deprecated = true]` / `option deprecated = true`: ts-proto turns them into `@deprecated` JSDoc, and
// `@typescript-eslint/no-deprecated` (warn) fails `bun run check` (`--max-warnings=0`) at every remaining use.
message GetUserRequest { string id = 1; optional string tenant_id = 2; } // Task 12
message ListUsersRequest { /* 1-3 unchanged */ optional string tenant_id = 4; } // Task 12 (required by the zod schema)
rpc SwitchTenant(SwitchTenantRequest) returns (AuthTokens); // Task 14 (AuthService)
message SwitchTenantRequest {
  string user_id = 1; string tenant_id = 2; string refresh_token = 3;
  string access_token_jti = 4; int64 access_token_exp = 5; ClientInfo client = 6;
}
rpc StaffSwitchIn(StaffSwitchInRequest) returns (StaffAccessToken); // Task 15 (AuthService)
message StaffSwitchInRequest { string staff_user_id = 1; string tenant_id = 2; string reason = 3; ClientInfo client = 4; }
message StaffAccessToken { string access_token = 1; int32 expires_in = 2; string token_type = 3; string tenant_id = 4; string audit_event_id = 5; }
rpc StaffSwitchOut(StaffSwitchOutRequest) returns (google.protobuf.Empty); // Task 16 (AuthService)
message StaffSwitchOutRequest { string staff_user_id = 1; string tenant_id = 2; string access_token_jti = 3; int64 access_token_exp = 4; }
```

Next free field numbers for the edge plan: `AuthTokens` 8, `LoginRequest` 5, `User` 7, `GetUserRequest` 3,
`ListUsersRequest` 5. B2-17 (edge) puts tenant, member and invitation RPCs in a new `service TenantsService` in the
same `identity.v1` package.

REST (core): `POST /v1/auth/register|login|refresh` responses gain `tenantId`, `tenantRoles` (Task 8);
`POST /v1/auth/login` body gains optional `tenantId` (Task 9); `GET /v1/auth/me` gains `tenantId`, `tenantRoles`,
`staff` (Task 10); `PATCH /v1/users/:id/roles` and `UserResponse.roles` removed (Task 11); `GET /v1/users` and
`GET /v1/users/:id` scoped to the active tenant (Task 12); `POST /v1/auth/switch-tenant { tenantId, refreshToken }`
→ 200 tokens (Task 14); `POST /v1/staff/tenants/:tenantId/switch-in { reason }` → 201 `{ accessToken, expiresIn,
tokenType, tenantId, auditEventId }` (Task 15); `POST /v1/staff/switch-out` → 204, `@StaffOnly()` (Task 16).
GraphQL (core): `enum TenantRole { OWNER ADMIN MEMBER }` and `AuthPayload.tenantId: ID!`,
`AuthPayload.tenantRoles: [TenantRole!]!` (Task 8); `LoginInput.tenantId: ID` (Task 9); `User.roles`, `enum Role`
and `updateUserRoles` removed (Task 11); `users`/`user(id)` scoped (Task 12);
`switchTenant(input: SwitchTenantInput!): AuthPayload!` (Task 14). Edge B2-16 adds `me` tenant fields, `myTenants`,
`tenantMembers` and the other tenancy mutations.

### I-10. Error codes

| Code                          | Class (package)                                                                              | HTTP                             | Raised by                                                                                              |
| ----------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `INVALID_PRINCIPAL`           | `UnauthenticatedException` (common)                                                          | 401                              | `createPrincipal` (Task 1)                                                                             |
| `PRINCIPAL_MISSING`           | `UnauthenticatedException` (common)                                                          | 401                              | `requirePrincipal` (Task 1)                                                                            |
| `STAFF_ONLY`                  | `PermissionDeniedException` (`RbacErrorCode`, auth) / `NotPlatformStaffException` (identity) | 403                              | `StaffOnlyGuard` (Task 13); switch-in by a non-staff user (Task 15)                                    |
| `TENANT_SLUG_TAKEN`           | `TenantSlugTakenException` (identity)                                                        | 409                              | `tenants_slug_unique` (Task 3)                                                                         |
| `MEMBERSHIP_EXISTS`           | `MembershipAlreadyExistsException` (identity)                                                | 409                              | memberships PK (Task 4)                                                                                |
| `TENANT_ACCESS_DENIED`        | `TenantAccessDeniedException` (identity)                                                     | 403                              | login/switch with a tenant the user is not a member of, unknown and nil tenants included (Tasks 8, 14) |
| `MEMBERSHIP_REVOKED`          | `MembershipRevokedException` (identity)                                                      | 401                              | refresh of a session whose membership is gone; the session is revoked (Task 8)                         |
| `GLOBAL_ROLES_REMOVED`        | `GlobalRolesRemovedException` (identity, `BusinessRuleViolationException`)                   | 422 (gRPC `FAILED_PRECONDITION`) | the retired `UpdateUserRoles` RPC (Task 11)                                                            |
| `STAFF_TOKEN_NOT_ALLOWED`     | `StaffTokenNotAllowedException` (identity, `PermissionDeniedException`)                      | 403                              | `switch-tenant` / `switchTenant` called with a staff switch-in token (Task 14)                         |
| `NOT_FOUND` (entity `Tenant`) | `EntityNotFoundException`                                                                    | 404                              | staff switch-in to an unknown tenant (Task 15)                                                         |

Removed: `CANNOT_REVOKE_OWN_ADMIN`, `CANNOT_REMOVE_LAST_ADMIN` and their exceptions (Task 11; B2-13's per-tenant
last-owner rule replaces them). `INVALID_ROLES` / `InvalidRolesException` stay for tenant roles.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests.**

`libs/common/src/tenancy/tenant-role.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
  impliedTenantRoles,
  isTenantRole,
  normalizeTenantRoles,
  satisfiesTenantRole,
  TENANT_ROLES,
} from './tenant-role.js';

describe('tenant roles', () => {
  it('lists owner, admin and member in canonical order', () => {
    expect(TENANT_ROLES).toEqual(['owner', 'admin', 'member']);
  });

  it('narrows untrusted values (legacy global roles are not tenant roles)', () => {
    expect(TENANT_ROLES.every(isTenantRole)).toBe(true);
    for (const value of ['user', 'moderator', 'Owner', '', null, undefined, 1, {}]) {
      expect(isTenantRole(value)).toBe(false);
    }
  });

  it('implies owner ⊇ admin ⊇ member, the role itself first', () => {
    expect(impliedTenantRoles('owner')).toEqual(['owner', 'admin', 'member']);
    expect(impliedTenantRoles('admin')).toEqual(['admin', 'member']);
    expect(impliedTenantRoles('member')).toEqual(['member']);
  });

  it('checks a requirement through the hierarchy', () => {
    expect(satisfiesTenantRole(['owner'], 'admin')).toBe(true);
    expect(satisfiesTenantRole(['admin'], 'member')).toBe(true);
    expect(satisfiesTenantRole(['member', 'admin'], 'admin')).toBe(true);
    expect(satisfiesTenantRole(['member'], 'admin')).toBe(false);
    expect(satisfiesTenantRole(['admin'], 'owner')).toBe(false);
    expect(satisfiesTenantRole([], 'member')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('normalizes: unknown values dropped, duplicates removed, canonical order', () => {
    expect(normalizeTenantRoles(['member', 'root', 'owner', 'member', 7])).toEqual([
      'owner',
      'member',
    ]);
    expect(normalizeTenantRoles([])).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

`libs/common/src/tenancy/principal.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { UnauthenticatedException } from '../errors/domain.exception.js';
import { isPlatformTenantId, PLATFORM_TENANT_ID } from './platform-tenant.js';
import { createPrincipal, isPrincipal, TenancyErrorCode } from './principal.js';

const USER = '0190f3b2-0000-7000-8000-000000000001';
const TENANT = '0190f3b2-0000-7000-8000-0000000000aa';

const errorOf = (fn: () => unknown): unknown => {
  try {
    fn();
  } catch (error) {
    return error;
  }
  return undefined;
};

describe('PLATFORM_TENANT_ID', () => {
  it('is the nil UUID', () => {
    expect(PLATFORM_TENANT_ID).toBe('00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000');
    expect(isPlatformTenantId(PLATFORM_TENANT_ID)).toBe(true);
    expect(isPlatformTenantId(TENANT)).toBe(false);
    expect(isPlatformTenantId(undefined)).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('createPrincipal', () => {
  it('builds a frozen tenant principal with normalized roles', () => {
    const principal = createPrincipal({
      userId: USER,
      tenantId: TENANT,
      roles: ['member', 'owner', 'ghost', 'member'],
    });
    expect(principal).toEqual({
      userId: USER,
      tenantId: TENANT,
      roles: ['owner', 'member'],
      staff: false,
    });
    expect(Object.isFrozen(principal)).toBe(true);
    expect(Object.isFrozen(principal.roles)).toBe(true);
    expect(isPrincipal(principal)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('builds a user-scoped principal (no tenant, no roles, never staff)', () => {
    expect(createPrincipal({ userId: USER, tenantId: null })).toEqual({
      userId: USER,
      tenantId: null,
      roles: [],
      staff: false,
    });
  });

  it('keeps staff only as the boolean true, and only with a tenant', () => {
    expect(
      createPrincipal({ userId: USER, tenantId: TENANT, roles: ['admin'], staff: true }).staff,
    ).toBe(true);
    expect(
      errorOf(() => createPrincipal({ userId: USER, tenantId: null, staff: true })),
    ).toMatchObject({
      code: TenancyErrorCode.INVALID_PRINCIPAL,
      details: { reason: 'roles and staff need a tenant' },
    });
  });

  it.each([
    ['a malformed user id', { userId: 'u-1', tenantId: TENANT }, 'userId is not a UUID'],
    ['the nil user id', { userId: PLATFORM_TENANT_ID, tenantId: TENANT }, 'userId is not a UUID'],
    [
      'a malformed tenant id',
      { userId: USER, tenantId: "x'; drop table t; --" },
      'tenantId is not a UUID',
    ],
    [
      'the platform tenant',
      { userId: USER, tenantId: PLATFORM_TENANT_ID },
      'tenantId is the platform tenant',
    ],
    [
      'roles without a tenant',
      { userId: USER, tenantId: null, roles: ['owner'] },
      'roles and staff need a tenant',
    ],
  ])('refuses %s with 401 INVALID_PRINCIPAL', (_name, input, reason) => {
    const error = errorOf(() => createPrincipal(input));
    expect(error).toBeInstanceOf(UnauthenticatedException);
    expect(error).toMatchObject({ code: 'INVALID_PRINCIPAL', details: { reason } });
  });
});

describe('isPrincipal', () => {
  it('rejects anything that createPrincipal would not produce', () => {
    expect(isPrincipal(undefined)).toBe(false);
    expect(isPrincipal({ userId: USER, tenantId: TENANT, roles: ['root'], staff: false })).toBe(
      false,
    );
    expect(isPrincipal({ userId: USER, tenantId: TENANT, roles: [], staff: 'yes' })).toBe(false);
    expect(isPrincipal({ userId: 'u', tenantId: null, roles: [], staff: false })).toBe(false);
    expect(
      isPrincipal({ userId: PLATFORM_TENANT_ID, tenantId: TENANT, roles: [], staff: false }),
    ).toBe(false);
    expect(
      isPrincipal({ userId: USER, tenantId: PLATFORM_TENANT_ID, roles: [], staff: false }),
    ).toBe(false);
    expect(isPrincipal({ userId: USER, tenantId: null, roles: ['owner'], staff: false })).toBe(
      false,
    );
    expect(isPrincipal({ userId: USER, tenantId: null, roles: [], staff: true })).toBe(false);
    expect(
      isPrincipal({ userId: USER, tenantId: TENANT, roles: ['member', 'owner'], staff: false }),
    ).toBe(false);
    expect(isPrincipal(createPrincipal({ userId: USER, tenantId: null }))).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

`libs/common/src/tenancy/principal-context.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { ClsServiceManager } from 'nestjs-cls';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { UnauthenticatedException } from '../errors/domain.exception.js';
import { createPrincipal } from './principal.js';
import {
  CLS_PRINCIPAL,
  getPrincipal,
  requirePrincipal,
  runWithPrincipal,
  setPrincipal,
} from './principal-context.js';

const USER = '0190f3b2-0000-7000-8000-000000000001';
const TENANT = '0190f3b2-0000-7000-8000-0000000000aa';
const REQUEST = Symbol('request');
const cls = ClsServiceManager.getClsService();

const errorOf = (fn: () => unknown): unknown => {
  try {
    fn();
  } catch (error) {
    return error;
  }
  return undefined;
};

describe('principal context', () => {
  it('is empty outside a CLS context, and setPrincipal refuses to run there', () => {
    expect(getPrincipal()).toBeUndefined();
    expect(() => requirePrincipal()).toThrow(UnauthenticatedException);
    expect(() => setPrincipal(createPrincipal({ userId: USER, tenantId: null }))).toThrow(
      /active CLS context/,
    );
  });

  it('stores the principal under CLS_PRINCIPAL in the active context', () => {
    const principal = createPrincipal({ userId: USER, tenantId: TENANT, roles: ['member'] });
    cls.run(() => {
      setPrincipal(principal);
      expect(getPrincipal()).toBe(principal);
      expect(requirePrincipal()).toBe(principal);
      expect(cls.get(CLS_PRINCIPAL)).toBe(principal);
    });
  });

  it('answers 401 PRINCIPAL_MISSING inside a context that has none', () => {
    cls.run(() => {
      expect(errorOf(() => requirePrincipal())).toMatchObject({ code: 'PRINCIPAL_MISSING' });
    });
  });

  it('runWithPrincipal scopes the principal to a child context that keeps the parent values', async () => {
    const outer = createPrincipal({ userId: USER, tenantId: null });
    const inner = createPrincipal({ userId: USER, tenantId: TENANT, roles: ['owner'] });
    await cls.run(async () => {
      cls.set(REQUEST, 'r-1');
      setPrincipal(outer);
      const seen = await runWithPrincipal(inner, async () => {
        await Promise.resolve();
        return { principal: getPrincipal(), request: cls.get<string>(REQUEST) };
      });
      expect(seen).toEqual({ principal: inner, request: 'r-1' });
      expect(getPrincipal()).toBe(outer);
    });
  });

  it('runWithPrincipal opens a context when none is active', () => {
    const principal = createPrincipal({ userId: USER, tenantId: TENANT });
    expect(runWithPrincipal(principal, () => getPrincipal())).toBe(principal);
    expect(getPrincipal()).toBeUndefined();
  });
});
```

`libs/common/src/tenancy/tenant-role.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * Roles a user holds in ONE tenant (spec §6): owner ⊇ admin ⊇ member. Stored in
 * `identity.memberships.roles`, carried by access tokens (`roles` = the roles in `tid`) and by the
 * request principal. The values are persisted and sent over gRPC: never rename them.
 */
export const TENANT_ROLES = ['owner', 'admin', 'member'] as const;

export type TenantRole = (typeof TENANT_ROLES)[number];

const TENANT_ROLE_SET: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set(TENANT_ROLES);

const IMPLIED: Readonly<Record<TenantRole, readonly TenantRole[]>> = Object.freeze({
  owner: Object.freeze(['owner', 'admin', 'member'] as const),
  admin: Object.freeze(['admin', 'member'] as const),
  member: Object.freeze(['member'] as const),
});

/** Narrows untrusted input (token claims, gRPC metadata, database rows). */
export function isTenantRole(value: unknown): value is TenantRole {
  return typeof value === 'string' && TENANT_ROLE_SET.has(value);
}

/** The roles `role` implies, itself first. */
export function impliedTenantRoles(role: TenantRole): readonly TenantRole[] {
  return IMPLIED[role];
}

/** True when any of `held` implies `required`. */
export function satisfiesTenantRole(held: readonly TenantRole[], required: TenantRole): boolean {
  return held.some((role) => isTenantRole(role) && IMPLIED[role].includes(required));
}

/** Unknown values dropped, duplicates removed, canonical order (owner, admin, member). */
export function normalizeTenantRoles(values: readonly unknown[]): TenantRole[] {
  const present = new Set(values.filter(isTenantRole));
  return TENANT_ROLES.filter((role) => present.has(role));
}
```

`libs/common/src/tenancy/platform-tenant.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * The nil UUID: the `tenant_id` of platform-level rows (platform Stripe events, platform ledger
 * accounts). It is never issued as a tenant (`identity.tenants` has a CHECK against it) and never
 * accepted as a runtime principal's tenant (`createPrincipal` refuses it).
 */
export const PLATFORM_TENANT_ID = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000';

export function isPlatformTenantId(value: unknown): boolean {
  return typeof value === 'string' && value.toLowerCase() === PLATFORM_TENANT_ID;
}
```

`libs/common/src/tenancy/principal.ts`:

```ts
import { UnauthenticatedException } from '../errors/domain.exception.js';
import { isUuid } from '../utils/id.util.js';
import { isPlatformTenantId } from './platform-tenant.js';
import { normalizeTenantRoles, type TenantRole } from './tenant-role.js';

/** Who a unit of work runs as (spec §7). Written into CLS at every edge. */
export interface Principal {
  readonly userId: string;
  /** null only in user-scoped units of work (login before the tenant is chosen). */
  readonly tenantId: string | null;
  /** Roles in `tenantId`; always [] when tenantId is null. */
  readonly roles: readonly TenantRole[];
  /** true only for a staff switch-in token (always with a tenant). */
  readonly staff: boolean;
}

export interface PrincipalInput {
  readonly userId: string;
  readonly tenantId: string | null;
  readonly roles?: readonly unknown[];
  readonly staff?: boolean;
}

export const TenancyErrorCode = {
  INVALID_PRINCIPAL: 'INVALID_PRINCIPAL',
  PRINCIPAL_MISSING: 'PRINCIPAL_MISSING',
} as const;
export type TenancyErrorCode = (typeof TenancyErrorCode)[keyof typeof TenancyErrorCode];

const invalid = (reason: string): UnauthenticatedException =>
  new UnauthenticatedException('Invalid principal', {
    code: TenancyErrorCode.INVALID_PRINCIPAL,
    details: { reason },
  });

/**
 * The only way to build a principal. B3's transaction adapter writes these values into `SET LOCAL`
 * strings, so ids must be UUIDs and the platform tenant is refused here, once.
 */
export function createPrincipal(input: PrincipalInput): Principal {
  if (!isUuid(input.userId) || isPlatformTenantId(input.userId))
    throw invalid('userId is not a UUID');
  const { tenantId } = input;
  if (tenantId !== null) {
    if (!isUuid(tenantId)) throw invalid('tenantId is not a UUID');
    if (isPlatformTenantId(tenantId)) throw invalid('tenantId is the platform tenant');
  }
  const roles = normalizeTenantRoles(input.roles ?? []);
  const staff = input.staff === true;
  if (tenantId === null && (roles.length > 0 || staff))
    throw invalid('roles and staff need a tenant');
  return Object.freeze({ userId: input.userId, tenantId, roles: Object.freeze(roles), staff });
}

/** True only for a value `createPrincipal` could have returned (same id, tenant and role rules). */
export function isPrincipal(value: unknown): value is Principal {
  if (typeof value !== 'object' || value === null) return false;
  const candidate = value as Record<string, unknown>;
  const { roles, staff, tenantId, userId } = candidate;
  if (!isUuid(userId) || isPlatformTenantId(userId) || typeof staff !== 'boolean') return false;
  if (!Array.isArray(roles)) return false;
  const normalized = normalizeTenantRoles(roles);
  // Canonical order and no unknown or duplicate value: exactly what createPrincipal stores.
  if (normalized.length !== roles.length || normalized.some((role, i) => role !== roles[i]))
    return false;
  if (tenantId === null) return roles.length === 0 && !staff;
  return isUuid(tenantId) && !isPlatformTenantId(tenantId);
}
```

`libs/common/src/tenancy/principal-context.ts`:

```ts
import { ClsServiceManager } from 'nestjs-cls';
import { UnauthenticatedException } from '../errors/domain.exception.js';
import { type Principal, TenancyErrorCode } from './principal.js';

/** CLS key of the request principal (spec §7). */
export const CLS_PRINCIPAL: unique symbol = Symbol('app:principal');

/**
 * nestjs-cls keeps ONE ClsService per process: `ClsServiceManager.getClsService()` is the same
 * instance DI hands out (`ClsModule` provides it with `useValue`), so these helpers need no module
 * and work in guards, interceptors, handlers and B3's transaction adapter alike.
 */
const cls = () => ClsServiceManager.getClsService();

export function getPrincipal(): Principal | undefined {
  const store = cls();
  return store.isActive() ? store.get<Principal | undefined>(CLS_PRINCIPAL) : undefined;
}

export function requirePrincipal(): Principal {
  const principal = getPrincipal();
  if (principal === undefined) {
    throw new UnauthenticatedException('Authentication required', {
      code: TenancyErrorCode.PRINCIPAL_MISSING,
    });
  }
  return principal;
}

export function setPrincipal(principal: Principal): void {
  const store = cls();
  if (!store.isActive()) throw new Error('setPrincipal() needs an active CLS context');
  store.set(CLS_PRINCIPAL, principal);
}

/** Runs `fn` in a child context that inherits the parent store, with `principal` set. */
export function runWithPrincipal<T>(principal: Principal, fn: () => T): T {
  const store = cls();
  return store.run({ ifNested: 'inherit' }, () => {
    store.set(CLS_PRINCIPAL, principal);
    return fn();
  });
}
```

- `TENANT_ROLES` (`owner` ⊇ `admin` ⊇ `member`), `isTenantRole`, `impliedTenantRoles`, `satisfiesTenantRole`,

`libs/auth/src/rbac/rbac.registry.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { defineRbacFeature, isPermissionName, PERMISSION_PATTERN } from './rbac-feature.js';
import { RbacRegistry } from './rbac.registry.js';

const ORDERS = defineRbacFeature({
  name: 'orders',
  permissions: ['orders:read', 'orders:approve'],
  grants: { member: ['orders:read'], admin: ['orders:approve'] },
});
const ALERTS = defineRbacFeature({
  name: 'alerts',
  permissions: ['alerts:read', 'alerts:manage'],
  grants: { member: ['alerts:read'], owner: ['alerts:manage'] },
});

describe('defineRbacFeature', () => {
  it('freezes a sorted, validated copy', () => {
    expect(ORDERS).toEqual({
      name: 'orders',
      permissions: ['orders:approve', 'orders:read'],
      grants: { member: ['orders:read'], admin: ['orders:approve'] },
    });
    expect(Object.isFrozen(ORDERS)).toBe(true);
    expect(Object.isFrozen(ORDERS.permissions)).toBe(true);
  });

  it.each([
    [
      'a malformed name',
      { permissions: ['Orders:Read'], grants: {} },
      /malformed permission "Orders:Read"/,
    ],
    ['a wildcard', { permissions: ['orders:*'], grants: {} }, /malformed permission "orders:\*"/],
    [
      'a duplicate',
      { permissions: ['orders:read', 'orders:read'], grants: {} },
      /duplicate permission/,
    ],
    [
      'an undeclared grant',
      { permissions: ['orders:read'], grants: { member: ['orders:raed'] } },
      /undeclared permission "orders:raed"/,
    ],
  ])('rejects %s', (_name, feature, message) => {
    expect(() => defineRbacFeature({ name: 'orders', ...feature } as never)).toThrow(message);
  });

  it('accepts only <resource>:<action> names', () => {
    expect(PERMISSION_PATTERN.source).toBe('^[a-z][a-z0-9-]*:[a-z][a-z0-9-]*$');
    expect(isPermissionName('members:manage')).toBe(true);
    for (const bad of ['members', 'members:', ':manage', 'a:b:c', 'files:*', 7, undefined]) {
      expect(isPermissionName(bad)).toBe(false);
    }
  });
});

describe('RbacRegistry', () => {
  const registry = (): RbacRegistry => {
    const value = new RbacRegistry();
    value.register(ORDERS);
    value.register(ALERTS);
    return value;
  };

  it('resolves grants through owner ⊇ admin ⊇ member, sorted and deduplicated', () => {
    const rbac = registry();
    expect(rbac.resolve(['member'])).toEqual(['alerts:read', 'orders:read']);
    expect(rbac.resolve(['admin'])).toEqual(['alerts:read', 'orders:approve', 'orders:read']);
    expect(rbac.resolve(['owner', 'member'])).toEqual([
      'alerts:manage',
      'alerts:read',
      'orders:approve',
      'orders:read',
    ]);
    expect(rbac.resolve([])).toEqual([]);
    expect(rbac.resolve(['root' as never])).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('knows every declared permission', () => {
    const rbac = registry();
    expect(rbac.permissions()).toEqual([
      'alerts:manage',
      'alerts:read',
      'orders:approve',
      'orders:read',
    ]);
    expect(rbac.isDeclared('orders:read')).toBe(true);
    expect(rbac.isDeclared('orders:raed')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('treats the same feature registered twice (an Api module and a test module) as a no-op', () => {
    const rbac = registry();
    rbac.register(
      defineRbacFeature({
        name: 'orders',
        permissions: ['orders:read', 'orders:approve'],
        grants: { member: ['orders:read'], admin: ['orders:approve'] },
      }),
    );
    expect(rbac.resolve(['admin'])).toEqual(['alerts:read', 'orders:approve', 'orders:read']);
  });

  it('refuses a redefinition and a permission owned by another feature', () => {
    const rbac = registry();
    expect(() =>
      rbac.register(
        defineRbacFeature({ name: 'orders', permissions: ['orders:read'], grants: {} }),
      ),
    ).toThrow(/"orders" is already registered with a different vocabulary/);
    expect(() =>
      rbac.register(
        defineRbacFeature({ name: 'shipments', permissions: ['orders:read'], grants: {} }),
      ),
    ).toThrow(/owned by another feature: orders:read \(orders\)/);
  });

  it('validates hand-built features too', () => {
    expect(() =>
      new RbacRegistry().register({
        name: 'x',
        permissions: ['x:read'],
        grants: { member: ['y:read'] },
      }),
    ).toThrow(/undeclared permission "y:read"/);
  });
});
```

`libs/auth/src/rbac/rbac-vocabulary.check.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { Controller, Get, type INestApplicationContext, Injectable, Module } from '@nestjs/common';
import { NestFactory } from '@nestjs/core';
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
  RequireAnyPermission,
  RequirePermissions,
} from '../decorators/require-permissions.decorator.js';
import { defineRbacFeature } from './rbac-feature.js';
import { RbacModule } from './rbac.module.js';
import { RbacRegistry } from './rbac.registry.js';

const ORDERS = defineRbacFeature({
  name: 'orders',
  permissions: ['orders:read', 'orders:approve'],
  grants: { member: ['orders:read'], admin: ['orders:approve'] },
});

@Controller('orders')
class OrdersController {
  @Get()
  @RequirePermissions('orders:read')
  list(): void {}

  @Get('raed')
  @RequireAnyPermission('orders:approve', 'orders:raed')
  typo(): void {}
}

@Injectable()
@RequirePermissions('shipments:track')
class ShipmentsResolver {}

const boot = async (module: new () => unknown): Promise<INestApplicationContext> =>
  NestFactory.createApplicationContext(module, { logger: false, abortOnError: false });

describe('RbacVocabularyCheck (boot-time drift check)', () => {
  let app: INestApplicationContext | undefined;
  afterEach(async () => {
    await app?.close().catch(() => undefined);
    app = undefined;
  });

  it('boots when every required permission is declared, and features registered twice stay one', async () => {
    @Controller('ok')
    class OkController {
      @Get()
      @RequirePermissions('orders:read')
      list(): void {}
    }
    @Module({ imports: [RbacModule.forFeature(ORDERS)] })
    class OrdersApi {}
    @Module({
      imports: [RbacModule.forRoot(), OrdersApi, RbacModule.forFeature(ORDERS)],
      controllers: [OkController],
    })
    class AppModule {}
    // createApplicationContext() runs init(): OnApplicationBootstrap hooks included.
    app = await boot(AppModule);
    expect(app.get(RbacRegistry).resolve(['admin'])).toEqual(['orders:approve', 'orders:read']);
  });

  it('fails the boot naming every undeclared permission and where it is required', async () => {
    @Module({
      imports: [RbacModule.forRoot(), RbacModule.forFeature(ORDERS)],
      controllers: [OrdersController],
      providers: [ShipmentsResolver],
    })
    class AppModule {}
    await expect(boot(AppModule)).rejects.toThrow(
      'RBAC vocabulary check failed: undeclared permission(s) orders:raed (OrdersController.typo); shipments:track (ShipmentsResolver)',
    );
  });

  it('registers two different features from two forFeature imports', async () => {
    const ALERTS = defineRbacFeature({
      name: 'alerts',
      permissions: ['alerts:read'],
      grants: { member: ['alerts:read'] },
    });
    @Module({
      imports: [RbacModule.forRoot(), RbacModule.forFeature(ORDERS), RbacModule.forFeature(ALERTS)],
    })
    class AppModule {}
    app = await boot(AppModule);
    expect(app.get(RbacRegistry).permissions()).toEqual([
      'alerts:read',
      'orders:approve',
      'orders:read',
    ]);
  });
});
```

describe('BILLING_RBAC', () => {
