`libs/auth/src/rbac/rbac-feature.ts`:

```ts
import { isTenantRole, type TenantRole } from '@platform/common';

/** `<resource>:<action>`, lower-case kebab segments: `orders:read`, `members:manage`. */
export const PERMISSION_PATTERN = /^[a-z][a-z0-9-]*:[a-z][a-z0-9-]*$/;

export function isPermissionName(value: unknown): value is string {
  return typeof value === 'string' && PERMISSION_PATTERN.test(value);
}

/**
 * One domain's RBAC vocabulary (spec §6): the permissions it owns and the tenant roles that get each
 * by default. Grants are cumulative through owner ⊇ admin ⊇ member. Registered at boot with
 * `RbacModule.forFeature(feature)`; no platform file names a domain permission.
 */
export interface RbacFeature {
  /** The owner of the vocabulary (a domain's name); used in error messages. */
  readonly name: string;
  readonly permissions: readonly string[];
  readonly grants: Readonly<Partial<Record<TenantRole, readonly string[]>>>;
}

/** Every problem of `feature`, empty when it is valid. */
export function rbacFeatureProblems(feature: RbacFeature): string[] {
  const problems: string[] = [];
  if (feature.name.trim() === '') problems.push('name is empty');
  const declared = new Set<string>();
  for (const permission of feature.permissions) {
    if (!isPermissionName(permission)) problems.push(`malformed permission "${permission}"`);
    else if (declared.has(permission)) problems.push(`duplicate permission "${permission}"`);
    declared.add(permission);
  }
  for (const [role, granted] of Object.entries(feature.grants)) {
    if (!isTenantRole(role)) problems.push(`unknown role "${role}"`);
    for (const permission of granted ?? []) {
      if (!declared.has(permission)) {
        problems.push(`grant of undeclared permission "${permission}" to ${role}`);
      }
    }
  }
  return problems;
}

/** Validates and freezes a feature (throws `Error` listing every problem). */
export function defineRbacFeature<const TPermission extends string>(feature: {
  name: string;
  permissions: readonly TPermission[];
  grants: Partial<Record<TenantRole, readonly NoInfer<TPermission>[]>>;
}): RbacFeature {
  const problems = rbacFeatureProblems(feature);
  if (problems.length > 0) {
    throw new Error(`Invalid RBAC feature "${feature.name}": ${problems.join('; ')}`);
  }
  const grants: Partial<Record<TenantRole, readonly string[]>> = {};
  for (const [role, granted] of Object.entries(feature.grants)) {
    if (isTenantRole(role)) grants[role] = Object.freeze([...new Set(granted ?? [])].sort());
  }
  return Object.freeze({
    name: feature.name,
    permissions: Object.freeze([...feature.permissions].sort()),
    grants: Object.freeze(grants),
  });
}
```

`libs/auth/src/rbac/rbac.registry.ts`:

```ts
import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { impliedTenantRoles, isTenantRole, type TenantRole } from '@platform/common';
import { isEqual } from 'lodash-es';
import { type RbacFeature, rbacFeatureProblems } from './rbac-feature.js';

/**
 * The role → permission map, assembled at boot from every domain's `RbacModule.forFeature(…)`
 * (spec §6). Global (`RbacModule.forRoot()`, imported by `AuthModule.forRootAsync()`); permissions are
 * resolved once per request when an access token is verified.
 */
@Injectable()
export class RbacRegistry {
  private readonly features = new Map<string, RbacFeature>();
  /** permission → name of the feature that declared it. */
  private readonly owners = new Map<string, string>();
  /** role → permissions granted to it directly (not through the hierarchy). */
  private readonly direct = new Map<TenantRole, Set<string>>();

  /**
   * Adds a feature. A deep-equal re-registration is a no-op (Nest identifies dynamic modules by
   * reference, so a feature imported by two modules registers twice).
   * @throws Error on an invalid feature, a different feature under a known name, or a permission
   *   another feature already declared
   */
  register(feature: RbacFeature): void {
    const known = this.features.get(feature.name);
    if (known !== undefined) {
      if (isEqual(known, feature)) return;
      throw new Error(
        `RBAC feature "${feature.name}" is already registered with a different vocabulary`,
      );
    }
    const problems = rbacFeatureProblems(feature);
    if (problems.length > 0) {
      throw new Error(`Invalid RBAC feature "${feature.name}": ${problems.join('; ')}`);
    }
    const owned = feature.permissions.filter((permission) => this.owners.has(permission));
    if (owned.length > 0) {
      const list = owned.map((permission) => `${permission} (${this.owners.get(permission)})`);
      throw new Error(
        `RBAC feature "${feature.name}" declares permissions owned by another feature: ${list.join(', ')}`,
      );
    }
    this.features.set(feature.name, feature);
    for (const permission of feature.permissions) this.owners.set(permission, feature.name);
    for (const [role, granted] of Object.entries(feature.grants)) {
      if (!isTenantRole(role)) continue;
      const set = this.direct.get(role) ?? new Set<string>();
      for (const permission of granted ?? []) set.add(permission);
      this.direct.set(role, set);
    }
  }

  isDeclared(permission: string): boolean {
    return this.owners.has(permission);
  }

  /** Every declared permission, sorted. */
  permissions(): string[] {
    return [...this.owners.keys()].sort();
  }

  /** Union of the grants of every role `roles` implies, sorted; unknown roles grant nothing. */
  resolve(roles: readonly TenantRole[]): string[] {
    const granted = new Set<string>();
    for (const role of roles) {
      if (!isTenantRole(role)) continue;
      for (const implied of impliedTenantRoles(role)) {
        for (const permission of this.direct.get(implied) ?? []) granted.add(permission);
      }
    }
    return [...granted].sort();
  }
}
```

`libs/auth/src/rbac/rbac-vocabulary.check.ts`:

```ts
import { Injectable, type OnApplicationBootstrap } from '@nestjs/common';
import { DiscoveryService, MetadataScanner } from '@nestjs/core';
import { PERMISSIONS_KEY } from '../auth.constants.js';
import type { PermissionRequirement } from '../decorators/require-permissions.decorator.js';
import { RbacRegistry } from './rbac.registry.js';

const requirementOf = (target: unknown): PermissionRequirement | undefined =>
  typeof target === 'function'
    ? (Reflect.getMetadata(PERMISSIONS_KEY, target) as PermissionRequirement | undefined)
    : undefined;

/**
 * Boot-time drift check: every `@RequirePermissions` / `@RequireAnyPermission` name on a controller
 * or provider (resolvers, WebSocket gateways) must be declared by some `RbacModule.forFeature`. A
 * typo or a forgotten feature fails the boot instead of silently denying every caller.
 */
@Injectable()
export class RbacVocabularyCheck implements OnApplicationBootstrap {
  constructor(
    private readonly discovery: DiscoveryService,
    private readonly scanner: MetadataScanner,
    private readonly registry: RbacRegistry,
  ) {}

  onApplicationBootstrap(): void {
    const undeclared = this.undeclared();
    if (undeclared.length > 0) {
      throw new Error(
        `RBAC vocabulary check failed: undeclared permission(s) ${undeclared.join('; ')}`,
      );
    }
  }

  /** `<names> (<Class>[.<method>])` for each class or method requiring an undeclared permission. */
  undeclared(): string[] {
    const found = new Set<string>();
    const wrappers = [...this.discovery.getControllers(), ...this.discovery.getProviders()];
    for (const { metatype } of wrappers) {
      if (typeof metatype !== 'function') continue;
      const prototype = (metatype as { prototype?: object }).prototype ?? null;
      this.collect(found, requirementOf(metatype), metatype.name);
      for (const method of this.scanner.getAllMethodNames(prototype)) {
        const handler: unknown = prototype === null ? undefined : Reflect.get(prototype, method);
        this.collect(found, requirementOf(handler), `${metatype.name}.${method}`);
      }
    }
    return [...found];
  }

  private collect(
    found: Set<string>,
    requirement: PermissionRequirement | undefined,
    where: string,
  ) {
    const missing = (requirement?.permissions ?? []).filter(
      (name) => !this.registry.isDeclared(name),
    );
    if (missing.length > 0) found.add(`${missing.join(', ')} (${where})`);
  }
}
```

`libs/auth/src/rbac/rbac.module.ts`:

```ts
import { type DynamicModule, Module } from '@nestjs/common';
import { DiscoveryModule } from '@nestjs/core';
import type { RbacFeature } from './rbac-feature.js';
import { RbacRegistry } from './rbac.registry.js';
import { RbacVocabularyCheck } from './rbac-vocabulary.check.js';

const RBAC_FEATURE = Symbol('auth:rbac-feature');
const RBAC_FEATURE_REGISTRATION = Symbol('auth:rbac-feature-registration');

const registerFeature = (registry: RbacRegistry, feature: RbacFeature): RbacFeature => {
  registry.register(feature);
  return feature;
};

/**
 * Open RBAC vocabulary (spec §6). `forRoot()` (global; `AuthModule.forRootAsync()` imports it) holds
 * the registry and the boot-time vocabulary check; each domain's Api module imports
 * `forFeature(<X>_RBAC)`. The feature itself is a `useValue` provider so that two features never
 * share a module id, whatever Nest's module-id algorithm (deep hash or reference).
 */
@Module({})
export class RbacModule {
  static forRoot(): DynamicModule {
    return {
      module: RbacModule,
      global: true,
      imports: [DiscoveryModule],
      providers: [RbacRegistry, RbacVocabularyCheck],
      exports: [RbacRegistry],
    };
  }

  static forFeature(feature: RbacFeature): DynamicModule {
    return {
      module: RbacModule,
      providers: [
        { provide: RBAC_FEATURE, useValue: feature },
        {
          provide: RBAC_FEATURE_REGISTRATION,
          inject: [RbacRegistry, RBAC_FEATURE],
          useFactory: registerFeature,
        },
      ],
    };
  }
}
```

`libs/auth/src/rbac/permission-match.ts` (moved out of `role-permissions.ts`, now on strings):

```ts
/** How several required permissions combine: every one of them, or at least one. */
export type PermissionMatchMode = 'all' | 'any';

/** `true` when `granted` satisfies `required` (`'all'` = every one, `'any'` = at least one). */
export function hasPermissions(
  granted: readonly string[],
  required: readonly string[],
  mode: PermissionMatchMode = 'all',
): boolean {
  if (required.length === 0) return true;
  const set = new Set(granted);
  return mode === 'all'
    ? required.every((permission) => set.has(permission))
    : required.some((permission) => set.has(permission));
}
```

`libs/auth/src/rbac/legacy-tenant-roles.ts` (deleted again by Task 10):

```ts
import { normalizeTenantRoles, type TenantRole } from '@platform/common';
import { isRole, Role } from './role.enum.js';

const LEGACY: Readonly<Record<Role, TenantRole>> = Object.freeze({
  [Role.Admin]: 'admin',
  // Least privilege for the transition (I-3): `admin` would grant users:manage-roles.
  [Role.Moderator]: 'member',
  [Role.User]: 'member',
});

/** Global roles of a pre-tenancy token → tenant roles, for permission resolution only (until Task 10). */
export function legacyTenantRoles(roles: readonly unknown[]): TenantRole[] {
  return normalizeTenantRoles(roles.filter(isRole).map((role) => LEGACY[role]));
}
```

```ts
import { defineRbacFeature, type RbacFeature } from '../rbac/rbac-feature.js';
import { RbacRegistry } from '../rbac/rbac.registry.js';

/** A registry holding `features` (specs that verify tokens or build an `AuthUser`). */
export function createTestRbacRegistry(...features: readonly RbacFeature[]): RbacRegistry {
  const registry = new RbacRegistry();
  for (const feature of features) registry.register(feature);
  return registry;
}

/** Neutral vocabulary for platform specs (libs name no domain). */
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { IDENTITY_LIMITS } from '../identity.constants.js';
import {
  deriveTenantSlug,
  personalWorkspaceName,
  slugify,
  TENANT_SLUG_PATTERN,
} from './tenant-slug.js';

const TENANT_A = '0190f3b2-0000-7000-8000-0000000000aa';
const TENANT_B = '0190f3b2-0000-7000-8000-0000deadbeef';

describe('tenant slugs', () => {
  it.each([
    ['Ada.Lovelace', 'ada-lovelace'],
    ['ADA', 'ada'],
    ['Zoë.Ünal', 'zoe-unal'],
    ['+++', 'workspace'],
    ['---ada---', 'ada'],
    ['', 'workspace'],
  ])('slugify(%j) = %j', (input, expected) => {
    expect(slugify(input)).toBe(expected);
  });

  it('caps the base at 40 characters without a trailing dash', () => {
    const slug = slugify(`${'a'.repeat(39)}.${'b'.repeat(160)}`);
    expect(slug).toBe('a'.repeat(39));
    expect(slugify('x'.repeat(200))).toHaveLength(40);
  });

  it('suffixes the last 8 hex digits of the tenant id: two users with the same local part get two slugs', () => {
    expect(deriveTenantSlug('ada', TENANT_A)).toBe('ada-000000aa');
    expect(deriveTenantSlug('ada', TENANT_B)).toBe('ada-deadbeef');
  });

  it('always yields a valid slug, whatever the email local part', () => {
    for (const base of ['+++', 'Zoë.Ünal', 'ADA', 'x'.repeat(200), '🙂', 'a_b c']) {
      expect(deriveTenantSlug(base, TENANT_B)).toMatch(TENANT_SLUG_PATTERN);
    }
    expect('-ada').not.toMatch(TENANT_SLUG_PATTERN);
    expect('ada-').not.toMatch(TENANT_SLUG_PATTERN);
    expect('Ada').not.toMatch(TENANT_SLUG_PATTERN);
  });

  it("names a personal workspace `<display name>'s workspace` within the name limit", () => {
    expect(personalWorkspaceName('  Ada  ')).toBe("Ada's workspace");
    const long = personalWorkspaceName('é'.repeat(IDENTITY_LIMITS.DISPLAY_NAME_MAX_LENGTH));
    expect(long.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(IDENTITY_LIMITS.TENANT_NAME_MAX_LENGTH);
    expect(long.endsWith("'s workspace")).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the slug helpers.** In `domains/identity/src/identity.constants.ts`, inside
      `IDENTITY_LIMITS` after `IP_MAX_LENGTH: 64,` add
      `  /** identity.tenants.name (personal workspace names are cut to fit). */` +
      `  TENANT_NAME_MAX_LENGTH: 100,`. Create `domains/identity/src/domain/tenant-slug.ts`:

```ts
import { IDENTITY_LIMITS } from '../identity.constants.js';

/** DNS-label shape: lower-case alphanumerics and inner dashes, 1-63 characters. */
export const TENANT_SLUG_PATTERN = /^[a-z0-9](?:[a-z0-9-]{0,61}[a-z0-9])?$/;

const SLUG_BASE_MAX_LENGTH = 40;
const FALLBACK_SLUG = 'workspace';
const POSSESSIVE = "'s workspace";

/** NFKD, accents stripped, lower case, every run of other characters → '-', ≤ 40 chars; '' → 'workspace'. */
export function slugify(value: string): string {
  const slug = value
    .normalize('NFKD')
    .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '')
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-')
    .replace(/^-+/, '')
    .slice(0, SLUG_BASE_MAX_LENGTH)
    .replace(/-+$/, '');
  return slug === '' ? FALLBACK_SLUG : slug;
}

/**
 * `<slugify(base)>-<last 8 hex digits of tenantId>` (≤ 49 chars). The suffix is random in a uuidv7, so two
 * tenants with the same base (`ada@a.io`, `ada@b.io`) get different slugs; `tenants_slug_unique` still
 * decides (409 `TENANT_SLUG_TAKEN` on the astronomically rare collision).
 */
export function deriveTenantSlug(base: string, tenantId: string): string {
  return `${slugify(base)}-${tenantId.replace(/-/g, '').slice(-8).toLowerCase()}`;
}

/** `<displayName>'s workspace`, cut by code points to `IDENTITY_LIMITS.TENANT_NAME_MAX_LENGTH`. */
export function personalWorkspaceName(displayName: string): string {
  const room = IDENTITY_LIMITS.TENANT_NAME_MAX_LENGTH - POSSESSIVE.length;
  return `${Array.from(displayName.trim()).slice(0, room).join('')}${POSSESSIVE}`;
}
```

`domains/identity/test/identity-database.ts`:

```ts
import { type INestApplicationContext, Module, type Provider } from '@nestjs/common';
import { NestFactory } from '@nestjs/core';
import { AppConfigModule } from '@platform/config';
import { DatabaseModule, DRIZZLE, type DrizzleDB } from '@platform/database';
import { startTestPostgres } from '@platform/testing/postgres';
import { ClsModule } from 'nestjs-cls';
import { vi } from 'vitest';
import { identityMigrations } from '../src/infrastructure/persistence/identity.migrations.js';
import {
  type IdentitySchema,
  identitySchema,
} from '../src/infrastructure/persistence/identity.schema.js';

export interface IdentityDatabase {
  readonly app: INestApplicationContext;
  readonly db: DrizzleDB<IdentitySchema>;
  close(): Promise<void>;
}

/**
 * A fresh Citus 14.2 database (`startTestPostgres`) with the platform set and identity's history
 * applied by the real `DatabaseModule`, and `providers` (repositories under test) on top. Int specs only.
 */
export async function startIdentityDatabase(providers: Provider[]): Promise<IdentityDatabase> {
  const pg = await startTestPostgres();
  vi.stubEnv('DATABASE_URL', pg.url);
  vi.stubEnv('SERVICE_NAME', 'identity-int');

  @Module({
    imports: [
      AppConfigModule.forRoot(),
      ClsModule.forRoot({ global: true }),
      DatabaseModule.forRootAsync({
        schema: identitySchema,
        migrations: [identityMigrations],
        runMigrations: true,
      }),
    ],
    providers,
  })
  class IdentityIntModule {}

  try {
    const app = await NestFactory.createApplicationContext(IdentityIntModule, {
      logger: false,
      abortOnError: false,
    });
    return {
      app,
      db: app.get<DrizzleDB<IdentitySchema>>(DRIZZLE),
      close: async () => {
        await app.close();
        await pg.stop();
        vi.unstubAllEnvs();
      },
    };
  } catch (error) {
    await pg.stop();
    vi.unstubAllEnvs();
    throw error;
  }
}
```

`domains/identity/src/infrastructure/persistence/tenancy.repositories.int-spec.ts`:

```ts
import { generateId, isUuidV7, PLATFORM_TENANT_ID } from '@platform/common';
import { sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { type IdentityDatabase, startIdentityDatabase } from '../../../test/identity-database.js';
import { TenantSlugTakenException } from '../../domain/identity.errors.js';
import { DrizzleTenantsRepository } from './drizzle-tenants.repository.js';

/*
 * identity's tenancy repositories against Citus 14.2 (single node, @platform/testing/postgres) with
 * identity's real migration history. Opt-in: INTEGRATION=1 npx vitest run --project identity:int tenancy
 * The tables are not distributed here (SH-5 adds the distribution calls); every query already carries
 * its distribution column (sharding design §2.1).
 */

const constraintOf = (error: unknown): unknown =>
  (error as { cause?: { constraint_name?: unknown } } | undefined)?.cause?.constraint_name;

describe.skipIf(process.env['INTEGRATION'] !== '1')(
  'identity tenancy repositories on Citus',
  () => {
    let database: IdentityDatabase | undefined;
    let tenants: DrizzleTenantsRepository;
    const run = generateId().slice(-8);
    const slug = (name: string): string => `${name}-${run}`;

    beforeAll(async () => {
      database = await startIdentityDatabase([DrizzleTenantsRepository]);
      tenants = database.app.get(DrizzleTenantsRepository);
    });

    afterAll(async () => {
      await database?.close();
    });

    const newTenant = async (name: string, personal = false): Promise<string> => {
      const id = generateId();
      await tenants.insert({ id, slug: slug(name), name: `${name} workspace`, personal });
      return id;
    };

    describe('tenants', () => {
      it('insert → findById / findByIds / existsBySlug / rename', async () => {
        const id = await newTenant('acme', true);
        await expect(tenants.findById(id)).resolves.toMatchObject({
          id,
          slug: slug('acme'),
          name: 'acme workspace',
          personal: true,
          createdAt: expect.any(Date),
        });
        const other = await newTenant('globex');
        const found = await tenants.findByIds([id, other, generateId()]);
        expect(found.map((tenant) => tenant.id).sort()).toEqual([id, other].sort());
        await expect(tenants.findByIds([])).resolves.toEqual([]);
        await expect(tenants.existsBySlug(slug('acme'))).resolves.toBe(true);
        await expect(tenants.existsBySlug(slug('nope'))).resolves.toBe(false);
        await expect(tenants.rename(id, 'Acme Inc.')).resolves.toBe(true);
        await expect(tenants.findById(id)).resolves.toMatchObject({ name: 'Acme Inc.' });
        await expect(tenants.rename(generateId(), 'x')).resolves.toBe(false);
      });

      it('maps tenants_slug_unique to TenantSlugTakenException', async () => {
        await newTenant('dupe');
        await expect(
          tenants.insert({ id: generateId(), slug: slug('dupe'), name: 'x', personal: false }),
        ).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(TenantSlugTakenException);
      });

      it('refuses the platform tenant id (tenants_id_not_platform)', async () => {
        const failure: unknown = await tenants
          .insert({
            id: PLATFORM_TENANT_ID,
            slug: slug('platform'),
            name: 'Platform',
            personal: false,
          })
          .then(
            () => undefined,
            (error: unknown) => error,
          );
        expect(constraintOf(failure)).toBe('tenants_id_not_platform');
      });

      it('defaults id (uuidv7) and personal = false for raw SQL inserts', async () => {
        const [row] = await (database as IdentityDatabase).db.execute<{
          id: string;
          personal: boolean;
        }>(
          sql`insert into identity.tenants (slug, name) values (${slug('raw')}, 'Raw') returning id, personal`,
        );
        expect(isUuidV7(row?.id)).toBe(true);
        expect(row?.personal).toBe(false);
      });
    });
  },
);
```

```ts
export interface TenantRecord {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly slug: string;
  readonly name: string;
  readonly personal: boolean;
  readonly createdAt: Date;
}

export interface NewTenant {
  id: string;
  slug: string;
  name: string;
  personal: boolean;
}

/** Tenants persistence port (`identity.tenants`, a reference table on Citus). */
export abstract class TenantsRepository {
  /** @throws TenantSlugTakenException when `tenants_slug_unique` rejects the row */
  abstract insert(tenant: NewTenant): Promise<void>;

  abstract findById(id: string): Promise<TenantRecord | null>;

  /** One query; unknown ids are omitted; order is unspecified. */
  abstract findByIds(ids: readonly string[]): Promise<TenantRecord[]>;

  abstract existsBySlug(slug: string): Promise<boolean>;

  /** `false` when no row matched. */
  abstract rename(id: string, name: string): Promise<boolean>;
}
```

`domains/identity/src/infrastructure/persistence/drizzle-tenants.repository.ts`:

```ts
import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { type DrizzleTransactionalAdapter, TransactionHost } from '@platform/database';
import { eq, sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import type {
  NewTenant,
  TenantRecord,
  TenantsRepository,
} from '../../application/persistence/tenants.repository.js';
import { TenantSlugTakenException } from '../../domain/identity.errors.js';
import { type IdentitySchema, tenants } from './identity.schema.js';
import { isUniqueViolation } from './postgres-errors.js';

/** Tenants over Drizzle; every statement joins the active `TransactionRunner` transaction. */
@Injectable()
export class DrizzleTenantsRepository implements TenantsRepository {
  constructor(
    private readonly txHost: TransactionHost<DrizzleTransactionalAdapter<IdentitySchema>>,
  ) {}

  async insert(tenant: NewTenant): Promise<void> {
    try {
      await this.txHost.tx.insert(tenants).values(tenant);
    } catch (error) {
      if (isUniqueViolation(error, 'tenants_slug_unique')) {
        throw new TenantSlugTakenException({ cause: error });
      }
      throw error;
    }
  }

  async findById(id: string): Promise<TenantRecord | null> {
    const [row] = await this.txHost.tx.select().from(tenants).where(eq(tenants.id, id)).limit(1);
    return row ?? null;
  }

  async findByIds(ids: readonly string[]): Promise<TenantRecord[]> {
    if (ids.length === 0) return [];
    return this.txHost.tx
      .select()
      .from(tenants)
      .where(sql`${tenants.id} = any(${sql.param([...ids])}::uuid[])`);
  }

  async existsBySlug(slug: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const rows = await this.txHost.tx
      .select({ id: tenants.id })
      .from(tenants)
      .where(eq(tenants.slug, slug))
      .limit(1);
    return rows.length > 0;
  }

  async rename(id: string, name: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const rows = await this.txHost.tx
      .update(tenants)
      .set({ name })
      .where(eq(tenants.id, id))
      .returning({ id: tenants.id });
    return rows.length > 0;
  }
}
```

```ts
/** A user row by raw SQL (no dependency on the users repository's shape, which Task 11 changes). */
const newUser = async (name: string): Promise<string> => {
  const [row] = await (database as IdentityDatabase).db.execute<{ id: string }>(sql`
      insert into identity.users (email, password_hash, display_name)
      values (${`${name}.${run}@example.com`}, 'x', ${name}) returning id`);
  if (row === undefined) throw new Error('user insert returned nothing');
  return row.id;
};

describe('memberships', () => {
  it('insert → find, roles normalized; empty roles refused before any SQL', async () => {
    const tenantId = await newTenant('m-insert');
    const userId = await newUser('m-insert');
    await memberships.insert({
      tenantId,
      userId,
      roles: ['member', 'owner', 'member'],
      lastActiveAt: null,
    });
    await expect(memberships.find(tenantId, userId)).resolves.toEqual({
      tenantId,
      userId,
      roles: ['owner', 'member'],
      lastActiveAt: null,
      createdAt: expect.any(Date),
    });
    await expect(memberships.find(tenantId, generateId())).resolves.toBeNull();
    await expect(
      memberships.insert({
        tenantId,
        userId: await newUser('m-empty'),
        roles: [],
        lastActiveAt: null,
      }),
    ).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(InvalidRolesException);
  });

  it('maps the primary key to MembershipAlreadyExistsException', async () => {
    const tenantId = await newTenant('m-dupe');
    const userId = await newUser('m-dupe');
    await memberships.insert({ tenantId, userId, roles: ['member'], lastActiveAt: null });
    await expect(
      memberships.insert({ tenantId, userId, roles: ['admin'], lastActiveAt: null }),
    ).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(MembershipAlreadyExistsException);
  });

  it('orders by last_active_at desc (NULLs last), then newest first; touch moves one to the front', async () => {
    const userId = await newUser('m-order');
    const never = await newTenant('m-never');
    const old = await newTenant('m-old');
    const recent = await newTenant('m-recent');
    await memberships.insert({ tenantId: never, userId, roles: ['member'], lastActiveAt: null });
    await memberships.insert({
      tenantId: old,
      userId,
      roles: ['member'],
      lastActiveAt: new Date('2026-01-01T00:00:00Z'),
    });
    await memberships.insert({
      tenantId: recent,
      userId,
      roles: ['admin'],
      lastActiveAt: new Date('2026-02-01T00:00:00Z'),
    });
    await expect(memberships.findMostRecentForUser(userId)).resolves.toMatchObject({
      tenantId: recent,
    });
    const listed = await memberships.listForUser(userId);
    expect(listed.map((membership) => membership.tenantId)).toEqual([recent, old, never]);
    expect(listed[0]?.tenant).toMatchObject({ id: recent, slug: slug('m-recent') });
    await memberships.touch(old, userId, new Date('2026-03-01T00:00:00Z'));
    await expect(memberships.findMostRecentForUser(userId)).resolves.toMatchObject({
      tenantId: old,
    });
    await expect(memberships.findMostRecentForUser(generateId())).resolves.toBeNull();
  });

  it('enforces memberships_roles_not_empty in SQL and cascades tenant and user deletes', async () => {
    const db = (database as IdentityDatabase).db;
    const tenantId = await newTenant('m-cascade');
    const userId = await newUser('m-cascade');
    const failure: unknown = await db
      .execute(
        sql`insert into identity.memberships (tenant_id, user_id, roles) values (${tenantId}, ${userId}, '{}')`,
      )
      .then(
        () => undefined,
        (error: unknown) => error,
      );
    expect(constraintOf(failure)).toBe('memberships_roles_not_empty');
    await memberships.insert({ tenantId, userId, roles: ['owner'], lastActiveAt: null });
    await db.execute(sql`delete from identity.tenants where id = ${tenantId}`);
    await expect(memberships.find(tenantId, userId)).resolves.toBeNull();
    const other = await newTenant('m-cascade-2');
    await memberships.insert({ tenantId: other, userId, roles: ['owner'], lastActiveAt: null });
    await db.execute(sql`delete from identity.users where id = ${userId}`);
    await expect(memberships.find(other, userId)).resolves.toBeNull();
  });
});
```

In `identity.schema.ts`: add `primaryKey` to the pg-core import and
`import { TENANT_ROLE_VALUES } from '../../domain/tenant-role.js';`; after `userRole` add
`/** Tenant roles (values = @platform/common \`TENANT_ROLES\`). */`+`export const tenantRole = identityPgSchema.enum('tenant_role', TENANT_ROLE_VALUES);`; after `tenants` add:

```ts
// citus: reference (a STABLE policy helper reads it on every node: sharding design §2.2)
export const memberships = identityPgSchema.table(
  'memberships',
  {
    tenantId: uuid()
      .notNull()
      .references(() => tenants.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
    userId: uuid()
      .notNull()
      .references(() => users.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
    /** Canonical order (owner, admin, member), deduplicated, never empty. */
    roles: tenantRole().array().notNull(),
    /** Set by register, login and switch (a replicated write on Citus), never by refresh. */
    lastActiveAt: timestamptz(),
    createdAt: timestamptz().notNull().defaultNow(),
  },
  (t) => [
    primaryKey({ name: 'memberships_tenant_id_user_id_pk', columns: [t.tenantId, t.userId] }),
    // "most recently active membership" at login (MembershipsRepository.findMostRecentForUser).
    index('memberships_user_id_last_active_at_idx').on(t.userId, t.lastActiveAt.desc().nullsLast()),
    check('memberships_roles_not_empty', sql`cardinality("roles") > 0`),
  ],
);
```

`domains/identity/src/application/persistence/memberships.repository.ts`:

```ts
import type { TenantRole } from '@platform/common';
import type { TenantRecord } from './tenants.repository.js';

export interface MembershipRecord {
  readonly tenantId: string;
  readonly userId: string;
  readonly roles: readonly TenantRole[];
  readonly lastActiveAt: Date | null;
  readonly createdAt: Date;
}

export interface NewMembership {
  tenantId: string;
  userId: string;
  roles: readonly TenantRole[];
  lastActiveAt: Date | null;
}

export interface TenantMembership extends MembershipRecord {
  readonly tenant: TenantRecord;
}

/** Memberships persistence port (`identity.memberships`, a reference table on Citus). */
export abstract class MembershipsRepository {
  /**
   * Roles are normalized (canonical order, deduplicated).
   * @throws InvalidRolesException when no valid role is left
   * @throws MembershipAlreadyExistsException on the (tenant_id, user_id) primary key
   */
  abstract insert(membership: NewMembership): Promise<void>;

  abstract find(tenantId: string, userId: string): Promise<MembershipRecord | null>;

  /** Latest `last_active_at` first (NULLs last), then newest `created_at`; `null` when the user has none. */
  abstract findMostRecentForUser(userId: string): Promise<MembershipRecord | null>;

  /** Every membership with its tenant, in the `findMostRecentForUser` order. */
  abstract listForUser(userId: string): Promise<TenantMembership[]>;

  /** Sets `last_active_at` (register, login and switch only; refresh never writes it). */
  abstract touch(tenantId: string, userId: string, at: Date): Promise<void>;
}
```

`domains/identity/src/infrastructure/persistence/drizzle-memberships.repository.ts`:

```ts
import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { normalizeTenantRoles } from '@platform/common';
import { type DrizzleTransactionalAdapter, TransactionHost } from '@platform/database';
import { and, desc, eq, sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import type {
  MembershipRecord,
  MembershipsRepository,
  NewMembership,
  TenantMembership,
} from '../../application/persistence/memberships.repository.js';
import {
  InvalidRolesException,
  MembershipAlreadyExistsException,
} from '../../domain/identity.errors.js';
import { type IdentitySchema, memberships, tenants } from './identity.schema.js';
import { isUniqueViolation } from './postgres-errors.js';

const RECENT_FIRST = [
  sql`${memberships.lastActiveAt} desc nulls last`,
  desc(memberships.createdAt),
] as const;

const membershipOf = (tenantId: string, userId: string) =>
  and(eq(memberships.tenantId, tenantId), eq(memberships.userId, userId));

/** Memberships over Drizzle; every statement joins the active `TransactionRunner` transaction. */
@Injectable()
export class DrizzleMembershipsRepository implements MembershipsRepository {
  constructor(
    private readonly txHost: TransactionHost<DrizzleTransactionalAdapter<IdentitySchema>>,
  ) {}

  async insert(membership: NewMembership): Promise<void> {
    const roles = normalizeTenantRoles(membership.roles);
    if (roles.length === 0) {
      throw new InvalidRolesException([
        { path: 'roles', message: 'At least one role is required' },
      ]);
    }
    try {
      await this.txHost.tx.insert(memberships).values({ ...membership, roles });
    } catch (error) {
      if (isUniqueViolation(error, 'memberships_tenant_id_user_id_pk')) {
        throw new MembershipAlreadyExistsException({ cause: error });
      }
      throw error;
    }
  }

  async find(tenantId: string, userId: string): Promise<MembershipRecord | null> {
    const [row] = await this.txHost.tx
      .select()
      .from(memberships)
      .where(membershipOf(tenantId, userId))
      .limit(1);
    return row ?? null;
  }

  async findMostRecentForUser(userId: string): Promise<MembershipRecord | null> {
    const [row] = await this.txHost.tx
      .select()
      .from(memberships)
      .where(eq(memberships.userId, userId))
      .orderBy(...RECENT_FIRST)
      .limit(1);
    return row ?? null;
  }

  async listForUser(userId: string): Promise<TenantMembership[]> {
    const rows = await this.txHost.tx
      .select({ membership: memberships, tenant: tenants })
      .from(memberships)
      .innerJoin(tenants, eq(tenants.id, memberships.tenantId))
      .where(eq(memberships.userId, userId))
      .orderBy(...RECENT_FIRST);
    return rows.map(({ membership, tenant }) => ({ ...membership, tenant }));
  }

  async touch(tenantId: string, userId: string, at: Date): Promise<void> {
    await this.txHost.tx
      .update(memberships)
      .set({ lastActiveAt: at })
      .where(membershipOf(tenantId, userId));
  }
}
```

```ts
describe('invitations (tenant-distributed: every query carries tenant_id except the system read)', () => {
  const invite = async (tenantId: string, invitedBy: string, expiresAt: Date) => {
    const { token, tokenHash } = generateInvitationToken();
    const id = generateId();
    await invitations.insert({
      tenantId,
      id,
      email: `guest.${generateId().slice(-6)}@example.com`,
      roles: ['member'],
      tokenHash,
      invitedBy,
      expiresAt,
    });
    return { id, token, tokenHash };
  };

  it('findByTokenHash (system read) → listPending → markAccepted once', async () => {
    const tenantId = await newTenant('i-flow');
    const inviter = await newUser('i-flow');
    const now = new Date();
    const live = await invite(tenantId, inviter, new Date(now.getTime() + INVITATION_TTL_MS));
    const expired = await invite(tenantId, inviter, new Date(now.getTime() - 1_000));
    await expect(
      invitations.findByTokenHash(hashInvitationToken(live.token)),
    ).resolves.toMatchObject({
      tenantId,
      id: live.id,
      roles: ['member'],
      acceptedAt: null,
    });
    await expect(invitations.findByTokenHash(sha256Hex('unknown'))).resolves.toBeNull();
    const pending = await invitations.listPending(tenantId, now);
    expect(pending.map((invitation) => invitation.id)).toEqual([live.id]);
    await expect(invitations.markAccepted(tenantId, live.id, now)).resolves.toBe(true);
    await expect(invitations.markAccepted(tenantId, live.id, now)).resolves.toBe(false);
    await expect(invitations.markAccepted(tenantId, expired.id, now)).resolves.toBe(false);
    await expect(invitations.listPending(tenantId, now)).resolves.toEqual([]);
  });

  it('is keyed (tenant_id, id) with a tenant-leading unique token hash', async () => {
    const tenantId = await newTenant('i-keys');
    const inviter = await newUser('i-keys');
    const first = await invite(tenantId, inviter, new Date(Date.now() + 60_000));
    const failure: unknown = await invitations
      .insert({
        tenantId,
        id: generateId(),
        email: 'x@example.com',
        roles: ['admin'],
        tokenHash: first.tokenHash,
        invitedBy: inviter,
        expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + 60_000),
      })
      .then(
        () => undefined,
        (error: unknown) => error,
      );
    expect(constraintOf(failure)).toBe('invitations_tenant_id_token_hash_uq');
    const [pk] = await (database as IdentityDatabase).db.execute<{ columns: string }>(sql`
        select string_agg(a.attname, ',' order by k.ord) as columns
        from pg_constraint c
        cross join lateral unnest(c.conkey) with ordinality as k(attnum, ord)
        join pg_attribute a on a.attrelid = c.conrelid and a.attnum = k.attnum
        where c.conname = 'invitations_tenant_id_id_pk'`);
    expect(pk?.columns).toBe('tenant_id,id');
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.** `domains/identity/src/domain/invitation-token.ts`:

```ts
import { randomBytes } from 'node:crypto';
import { sha256Hex } from '@platform/common';

/** Invitations expire 7 days after they are sent (spec §5.1). */
export const INVITATION_TTL_MS = 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1_000;
export const INVITATION_TOKEN_BYTES = 32;

/** What `identity.invitations.token_hash` stores for a raw token (the raw token is never stored). */
export function hashInvitationToken(token: string): string {
  return sha256Hex(token);
}

/** A fresh raw token (base64url of 32 random bytes, mailed once) and its hash (stored). */
export function generateInvitationToken(): { token: string; tokenHash: string } {
  const token = randomBytes(INVITATION_TOKEN_BYTES).toString('base64url');
  return { token, tokenHash: hashInvitationToken(token) };
}
```

```ts
// citus: tenant (PK and every unique lead with tenant_id; FKs to the reference tables only)
export const invitations = identityPgSchema.table(
  'invitations',
  {
    tenantId: uuid()
      .notNull()
      .references(() => tenants.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
    id: uuid()
      .notNull()
      .default(sql`uuidv7()`),
    /** Normalised like users.email; acceptance requires the logged-in user's email to match. */
    email: text().notNull(),
    roles: tenantRole().array().notNull(),
    /** sha256 hex of the mailed token. */
    tokenHash: text().notNull(),
    invitedBy: uuid()
      .notNull()
      .references(() => users.id),
    expiresAt: timestamptz().notNull(),
    acceptedAt: timestamptz(),
    createdAt: timestamptz().notNull().defaultNow(),
  },
  (t) => [
    primaryKey({ name: 'invitations_tenant_id_id_pk', columns: [t.tenantId, t.id] }),
    uniqueIndex('invitations_tenant_id_token_hash_uq').on(t.tenantId, t.tokenHash),
    // findByTokenHash: a multi-shard system read (one index probe per shard once distributed).
    index('invitations_token_hash_idx').on(t.tokenHash),
  ],
);
```

`domains/identity/src/application/persistence/invitations.repository.ts`:

```ts
import type { TenantRole } from '@platform/common';

export interface InvitationRecord {
  readonly tenantId: string;
  readonly id: string;
  readonly email: string;
  readonly roles: readonly TenantRole[];
  readonly tokenHash: string;
  readonly invitedBy: string;
  readonly expiresAt: Date;
  readonly acceptedAt: Date | null;
  readonly createdAt: Date;
}

export interface NewInvitation {
  tenantId: string;
  id: string;
  email: string;
  roles: readonly TenantRole[];
  tokenHash: string;
  invitedBy: string;
  expiresAt: Date;
}

/** Invitations persistence port (`identity.invitations`, distributed by tenant_id). */
export abstract class InvitationsRepository {
  abstract insert(invitation: NewInvitation): Promise<void>;

  /**
   * System method 3 (spec §9): the accept flow knows only the token. A multi-shard read on Citus;
   * B3-11 moves it into a `*.system-repository.ts` on the BYPASSRLS pool.
   */
  abstract findByTokenHash(tokenHash: string): Promise<InvitationRecord | null>;

  /** Not accepted and `expires_at > now`, newest first. */
  abstract listPending(tenantId: string, now: Date): Promise<InvitationRecord[]>;

  /** Conditional (`accepted_at IS NULL AND expires_at > now`); `true` when this call accepted it. */
  abstract markAccepted(tenantId: string, id: string, now: Date): Promise<boolean>;
}
```

`domains/identity/src/infrastructure/persistence/drizzle-invitations.repository.ts`:

```ts
import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { type DrizzleTransactionalAdapter, TransactionHost } from '@platform/database';
import { and, desc, eq, gt, isNull } from 'drizzle-orm';
import type {
  InvitationRecord,
  InvitationsRepository,
  NewInvitation,
} from '../../application/persistence/invitations.repository.js';
import { type IdentitySchema, invitations } from './identity.schema.js';

@Injectable()
export class DrizzleInvitationsRepository implements InvitationsRepository {
  constructor(
    private readonly txHost: TransactionHost<DrizzleTransactionalAdapter<IdentitySchema>>,
  ) {}

  async insert(invitation: NewInvitation): Promise<void> {
    await this.txHost.tx
      .insert(invitations)
      .values({ ...invitation, roles: [...invitation.roles] });
  }

  async findByTokenHash(tokenHash: string): Promise<InvitationRecord | null> {
    const [row] = await this.txHost.tx
      .select()
      .from(invitations)
      .where(eq(invitations.tokenHash, tokenHash))
      .limit(1);
    return row ?? null;
  }

  async listPending(tenantId: string, now: Date): Promise<InvitationRecord[]> {
    return this.txHost.tx
      .select()
      .from(invitations)
      .where(
        and(
          eq(invitations.tenantId, tenantId),
          isNull(invitations.acceptedAt),
          gt(invitations.expiresAt, now),
        ),
      )
      .orderBy(desc(invitations.id));
  }

  async markAccepted(tenantId: string, id: string, now: Date): Promise<boolean> {
    const rows = await this.txHost.tx
      .update(invitations)
      .set({ acceptedAt: now })
      .where(
        and(
          eq(invitations.tenantId, tenantId),
          eq(invitations.id, id),
          isNull(invitations.acceptedAt),
          gt(invitations.expiresAt, now),
        ),
      )
      .returning({ id: invitations.id });
    return rows.length > 0;
  }
}
```

```ts
import { Logger } from '@nestjs/common';
import { isUuidV7 } from '@platform/common';
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import {
  AUDIT_EVENT_TYPES,
  type AuditEventsRepository,
  type NewAuditEvent,
} from '../persistence/audit-events.repository.js';
import { TenantAuditLog } from './tenant-audit-log.service.js';

const TENANT = '0190f3b2-0000-7000-8000-0000000000aa';
const ACTOR = '0190f3b2-0000-7000-8000-000000000001';

describe('TenantAuditLog', () => {
  afterEach(() => vi.restoreAllMocks());

  it('appends one row in the target tenant and writes the audit log line (AUDIT-F16)', async () => {
    const appended: NewAuditEvent[] = [];
    const repository = {
      append: vi.fn(async (event: NewAuditEvent) => {
        appended.push(event);
      }),
      listForTenant: vi.fn(),
    } as unknown as AuditEventsRepository;
    const log = vi.spyOn(Logger.prototype, 'log').mockImplementation(() => undefined);
    const now = new Date('2026-11-09T10:00:00Z');

    const id = await new TenantAuditLog(repository).record({
      tenantId: TENANT,
      actorId: ACTOR,
      type: AUDIT_EVENT_TYPES.STAFF_SWITCH_IN,
      data: { reason: 'ticket 42' },
      now,
    });

    expect(isUuidV7(id)).toBe(true);
    expect(appended).toEqual([
      {
        tenantId: TENANT,
        id,
        actorId: ACTOR,
        type: 'staff.switch_in',
        data: { reason: 'ticket 42' },
        createdAt: now,
      },
    ]);
    expect(log).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      {
        audit: 'staff.switch_in',
        auditEventId: id,
        tenantId: TENANT,
        actorId: ACTOR,
        data: { reason: 'ticket 42' },
      },
      'Audit event recorded',
    );
  });

  it('defaults data to {} and a missing actor to null; a failing append writes no log line', async () => {
    const repository = {
      append: vi.fn(async () => {
        throw new Error('db down');
      }),
      listForTenant: vi.fn(),
    } as unknown as AuditEventsRepository;
    const log = vi.spyOn(Logger.prototype, 'log').mockImplementation(() => undefined);
    await expect(
      new TenantAuditLog(repository).record({
        tenantId: TENANT,
        actorId: null,
        type: AUDIT_EVENT_TYPES.MEMBER_LEFT,
      }),
    ).rejects.toThrow('db down');
    expect(repository.append).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      expect.objectContaining({ actorId: null, data: {}, type: 'member.left' }),
    );
    expect(log).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});
```

`domains/identity/src/infrastructure/persistence/audit-events.append-only.spec.ts` (append-only, code side: no
trigger, because Citus refuses to distribute a table that has triggers unless `citus.enable_unsafe_triggers` is on,
and no `REVOKE` yet, because until B3 the runtime role owns the table; B3's policies grant only SELECT and INSERT
on it, spec §8.2):

```ts
import { readdirSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join, relative } from 'node:path';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { DrizzleAuditEventsRepository } from './drizzle-audit-events.repository.js';

const SRC = join(import.meta.dirname, '..', '..');
/** Drizzle `.update(auditEvents)` / `.delete(auditEvents)`, or raw `UPDATE` / `DELETE FROM` on the table. */
const MUTATION =
  /\.(?:update|delete)\(\s*auditEvents\b|\b(?:update|delete\s+from)\s+"?identity"?\s*\.\s*"?audit_events\b/i;

/** Every non-spec TypeScript source under domains/identity/src. */
function sources(dir: string): string[] {
  return readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true }).flatMap((entry) => {
    const path = join(dir, entry.name);
    if (entry.isDirectory()) return sources(path);
    return entry.name.endsWith('.ts') && !/\.(?:spec|int-spec|e2e-spec)\.ts$/.test(entry.name)
      ? [path]
      : [];
  });
}

describe('identity.audit_events is append-only (code side; B3 adds the database side)', () => {
  it('the Drizzle repository only appends and lists', () => {
    const methods = Object.getOwnPropertyNames(DrizzleAuditEventsRepository.prototype).filter(
      (name) => name !== 'constructor',
    );
    expect(methods.sort()).toEqual(['append', 'listForTenant']);
  });

  it('no identity source updates or deletes audit rows', () => {
    expect(MUTATION.test('await this.txHost.tx.delete(auditEvents).where(x)')).toBe(true);
    expect(MUTATION.test('UPDATE "identity"."audit_events" SET data = $1')).toBe(true);
    expect(MUTATION.test('await this.txHost.tx.insert(auditEvents).values(event)')).toBe(false);
    const offenders = sources(SRC)
      .filter((file) => MUTATION.test(readFileSync(file, 'utf8')))
      .map((file) => relative(SRC, file));
    expect(offenders).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

```ts
describe('audit_events (append-only, tenant-distributed)', () => {
  it('appends and pages newest first within one tenant only', async () => {
    const tenantId = await newTenant('a-page');
    const otherTenant = await newTenant('a-other');
    const ids: string[] = [];
    for (const type of ['member.left', 'member.removed', 'staff.switch_in'] as const) {
      const id = generateId();
      ids.push(id);
      await auditEvents.append({
        tenantId,
        id,
        actorId: null,
        type,
        data: { n: ids.length },
        createdAt: new Date(),
      });
    }
    await auditEvents.append({
      tenantId: otherTenant,
      id: generateId(),
      actorId: null,
      type: 'member.left',
      data: {},
      createdAt: new Date(),
    });
    const first = await auditEvents.listForTenant(tenantId, { limit: 2 });
    expect(first.items.map((event) => event.id)).toEqual([ids[2], ids[1]]);
    expect(first.items[0]).toMatchObject({
      tenantId,
      type: 'staff.switch_in',
      data: { n: 3 },
      actorId: null,
    });
    expect(first.nextCursor).not.toBeNull();
    const second = await auditEvents.listForTenant(tenantId, {
      limit: 2,
      cursor: first.nextCursor ?? undefined,
    });
    expect(second.items.map((event) => event.id)).toEqual([ids[0]]);
    expect(second.nextCursor).toBeNull();
  });

  it('keeps actor_id without a foreign key (history outlives users) and defaults data to {}', async () => {
    const tenantId = await newTenant('a-actor');
    const [row] = await (database as IdentityDatabase).db.execute<{ data: unknown }>(sql`
        insert into identity.audit_events (tenant_id, actor_id, type)
        values (${tenantId}, ${generateId()}, 'member.left') returning data`);
    expect(row?.data).toEqual({});
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.** `identity.schema.ts` gains `jsonb` in the pg-core import and, after `invitations`:

```ts
// citus: tenant. Append-only: no code path updates or deletes a row (audit-events.append-only.spec.ts); B3's
// policies allow only SELECT and INSERT (spec §8.2). No trigger: Citus will not distribute a table with triggers.
export const auditEvents = identityPgSchema.table(
  'audit_events',
  {
    tenantId: uuid()
      .notNull()
      .references(() => tenants.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
    id: uuid()
      .notNull()
      .default(sql`uuidv7()`),
    /** The user who acted; no FK: the history outlives users. `null` for system actions. */
    actorId: uuid(),
    /** `AUDIT_EVENT_TYPES` value (`<subject>.<verb>`). */
    type: text().notNull(),
    data: jsonb()
      .$type<Record<string, unknown>>()
      .notNull()
      .default(sql`'{}'::jsonb`),
    createdAt: timestamptz().notNull().defaultNow(),
  },
  (t) => [
    primaryKey({ name: 'audit_events_tenant_id_id_pk', columns: [t.tenantId, t.id] }),
    index('audit_events_tenant_id_created_at_idx').on(t.tenantId, t.createdAt.desc()),
  ],
);
```

```ts
import type { CursorPage } from '@platform/common';

/** Audit event types written in a tenant's log (`<subject>.<verb>`); the edge tasks named are the writers. */
export const AUDIT_EVENT_TYPES = {
  STAFF_SWITCH_IN: 'staff.switch_in',
  STAFF_SWITCH_OUT: 'staff.switch_out',
  MEMBER_ROLES_CHANGED: 'member.roles_changed', // edge B2-13
  MEMBER_REMOVED: 'member.removed', // edge B2-13
  MEMBER_LEFT: 'member.left', // edge B2-13
  INVITATION_CREATED: 'invitation.created', // edge B2-14
  INVITATION_ACCEPTED: 'invitation.accepted', // edge B2-14
  TENANT_RENAMED: 'tenant.renamed', // edge B2-12
} as const;
export type AuditEventType = (typeof AUDIT_EVENT_TYPES)[keyof typeof AUDIT_EVENT_TYPES];

export interface AuditEventRecord {
  readonly tenantId: string;
  readonly id: string;
  readonly actorId: string | null;
  readonly type: string;
  readonly data: Readonly<Record<string, unknown>>;
  readonly createdAt: Date;
}

export interface NewAuditEvent {
  tenantId: string;
  id: string;
  actorId: string | null;
  type: AuditEventType;
  data: Record<string, unknown>;
  createdAt: Date;
}

/** Append-only tenant audit log (`identity.audit_events`, distributed by tenant_id). */
export abstract class AuditEventsRepository {
  abstract append(event: NewAuditEvent): Promise<void>;

  /** Keyset page on the uuidv7 id within the tenant, newest first. */
  abstract listForTenant(
    tenantId: string,
    criteria: { limit: number; cursor?: string | undefined },
  ): Promise<CursorPage<AuditEventRecord>>;
}
```

`domains/identity/src/infrastructure/persistence/drizzle-audit-events.repository.ts`:

```ts
import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import type { CursorPage } from '@platform/common';
import {
  type DrizzleTransactionalAdapter,
  keysetFetchLimit,
  keysetOrder,
  keysetPage,
  keysetWhere,
  TransactionHost,
} from '@platform/database';
import { and, eq } from 'drizzle-orm';
import type {
  AuditEventRecord,
  AuditEventsRepository,
  NewAuditEvent,
} from '../../application/persistence/audit-events.repository.js';
import { auditEvents, type IdentitySchema } from './identity.schema.js';

@Injectable()
export class DrizzleAuditEventsRepository implements AuditEventsRepository {
  constructor(
    private readonly txHost: TransactionHost<DrizzleTransactionalAdapter<IdentitySchema>>,
  ) {}

  async append(event: NewAuditEvent): Promise<void> {
    await this.txHost.tx.insert(auditEvents).values(event);
  }

  async listForTenant(
    tenantId: string,
    criteria: { limit: number; cursor?: string | undefined },
  ): Promise<CursorPage<AuditEventRecord>> {
    const rows = await this.txHost.tx
      .select()
      .from(auditEvents)
      .where(and(eq(auditEvents.tenantId, tenantId), keysetWhere(auditEvents.id, criteria.cursor)))
      .orderBy(keysetOrder(auditEvents.id))
      .limit(keysetFetchLimit(criteria.limit));
    return keysetPage(rows, criteria.limit);
  }
}
```

`domains/identity/src/application/services/tenant-audit-log.service.ts`:

```ts
import { Injectable, Logger } from '@nestjs/common';
import { generateId } from '@platform/common';
import {
  type AuditEventType,
  AuditEventsRepository,
} from '../persistence/audit-events.repository.js';

export interface TenantAuditEntry {
  tenantId: string;
  actorId: string | null;
  type: AuditEventType;
  data?: Record<string, unknown>;
  now?: Date;
}

/**
 * The tenant-visible audit trail (spec §5.1): one `identity.audit_events` row in the caller's unit of
 * work, then one structured log line (logger `IdentityAudit`, `audit: <type>`) for the operators'
 * pipeline. `data` must never hold secrets or tokens. Replaces the log-only role audit (AUDIT-F16).
 */
@Injectable()
export class TenantAuditLog {
  private readonly logger = new Logger('IdentityAudit');

  constructor(private readonly events: AuditEventsRepository) {}

  /** @returns the id of the appended row */
  async record(entry: TenantAuditEntry): Promise<string> {
    const id = generateId();
    const data = entry.data ?? {};
    await this.events.append({
      tenantId: entry.tenantId,
      id,
      actorId: entry.actorId,
      type: entry.type,
      data,
      createdAt: entry.now ?? new Date(),
    });
    this.logger.log(
      {
        audit: entry.type,
        auditEventId: id,
        tenantId: entry.tenantId,
        actorId: entry.actorId,
        data,
      },
      'Audit event recorded',
    );
    return id;
  }
}
```

```ts
import { isUuidV7 } from '@platform/common';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { TENANT_SLUG_PATTERN } from '../../domain/tenant-slug.js';
import { createInMemoryIdentityPersistence } from '../../testing/in-memory-identity.js';
import { PersonalWorkspaces } from './personal-workspaces.service.js';

const NOW = new Date('2026-11-09T10:00:00.000Z');
const ada = {
  id: '0190f3b2-0000-7000-8000-000000000001',
  email: 'ada.lovelace+test@example.com',
  displayName: 'Ada',
};

describe('PersonalWorkspaces', () => {
  it('creates a personal tenant slugged from the email and an owner membership active now', async () => {
    const persistence = createInMemoryIdentityPersistence();
    const membership = await new PersonalWorkspaces(
      persistence.tenants,
      persistence.memberships,
    ).create(ada, NOW);

    expect(membership).toEqual({
      tenantId: expect.any(String),
      userId: ada.id,
      roles: ['owner'],
      lastActiveAt: NOW,
      createdAt: NOW,
    });
    expect(isUuidV7(membership.tenantId)).toBe(true);
    const tenant = persistence.store.tenants.get(membership.tenantId);
    expect(tenant).toMatchObject({ name: "Ada's workspace", personal: true });
    expect(tenant?.slug).toMatch(/^ada-lovelace-test-[0-9a-f]{8}$/);
    await expect(persistence.memberships.find(membership.tenantId, ada.id)).resolves.toMatchObject({
      roles: ['owner'],
    });
  });

  it('gives the same local part on two domains two slugs, and slug-less local parts a valid one', async () => {
    const persistence = createInMemoryIdentityPersistence();
    const workspaces = new PersonalWorkspaces(persistence.tenants, persistence.memberships);
    const first = await workspaces.create({ ...ada, email: 'ada@a.io' }, NOW);
    const second = await workspaces.create(
      { ...ada, id: '0190f3b2-0000-7000-8000-000000000002', email: 'ada@b.io' },
      NOW,
    );
    const third = await workspaces.create(
      { ...ada, id: '0190f3b2-0000-7000-8000-000000000003', email: '+++@x.io' },
      NOW,
    );
    const slugs = [first, second, third].map(
      (m) => persistence.store.tenants.get(m.tenantId)?.slug,
    );
    expect(new Set(slugs).size).toBe(3);
    for (const slug of slugs) expect(slug).toMatch(TENANT_SLUG_PATTERN);
    expect(slugs[2]).toMatch(/^workspace-[0-9a-f]{8}$/);
  });
});
```

`domains/identity/src/testing/in-memory-tenancy.spec.ts` (the fakes mirror the Drizzle semantics the int spec pins):

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
  InvalidRolesException,
  MembershipAlreadyExistsException,
  TenantSlugTakenException,
} from '../index.js';
import { createInMemoryIdentityPersistence } from './in-memory-identity.js';

const USER = '0190f3b2-0000-7000-8000-000000000001';
const T1 = '0190f3b2-0000-7000-8000-0000000000a1';
const T2 = '0190f3b2-0000-7000-8000-0000000000a2';
const T3 = '0190f3b2-0000-7000-8000-0000000000a3';

describe('in-memory tenancy repositories', () => {
  it('tenants: unique slug, lookups, rename', async () => {
    const { tenants } = createInMemoryIdentityPersistence();
    await tenants.insert({ id: T1, slug: 'acme', name: 'Acme', personal: false });
    await expect(
      tenants.insert({ id: T2, slug: 'acme', name: 'x', personal: false }),
    ).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(TenantSlugTakenException);
    await expect(tenants.findByIds([T1, T2])).resolves.toMatchObject([{ id: T1 }]);
    await expect(tenants.existsBySlug('acme')).resolves.toBe(true);
    await expect(tenants.rename(T1, 'Acme Inc.')).resolves.toBe(true);
    await expect(tenants.rename(T2, 'x')).resolves.toBe(false);
  });

  it('memberships: normalized roles, duplicates refused, most recent first (NULLs last)', async () => {
    const { tenants, memberships } = createInMemoryIdentityPersistence();
    for (const id of [T1, T2, T3])
      await tenants.insert({ id, slug: id, name: id, personal: false });
    await expect(
      memberships.insert({ tenantId: T1, userId: USER, roles: [], lastActiveAt: null }),
    ).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(InvalidRolesException);
    await memberships.insert({
      tenantId: T1,
      userId: USER,
      roles: ['member', 'owner'],
      lastActiveAt: null,
    });
    await memberships.insert({
      tenantId: T2,
      userId: USER,
      roles: ['admin'],
      lastActiveAt: new Date(1_000),
    });
    await memberships.insert({
      tenantId: T3,
      userId: USER,
      roles: ['member'],
      lastActiveAt: new Date(2_000),
    });
    await expect(
      memberships.insert({ tenantId: T1, userId: USER, roles: ['member'], lastActiveAt: null }),
    ).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(MembershipAlreadyExistsException);
    await expect(memberships.find(T1, USER)).resolves.toMatchObject({ roles: ['owner', 'member'] });
    expect((await memberships.listForUser(USER)).map((m) => m.tenant.id)).toEqual([T3, T2, T1]);
    await memberships.touch(T1, USER, new Date(3_000));
    await expect(memberships.findMostRecentForUser(USER)).resolves.toMatchObject({ tenantId: T1 });
  });
});
```

```ts
      slug: expect.stringMatching(/^ada-tenancy-[0-9a-f]{8}$/),

    await register('+++@x.io', 'Plus');
    await register('ada@a.io', 'Ada A');
    await register('ada@b.io', 'Ada B');
    const slugs = [...e2e.identity.store.tenants.values()].map((tenant) => tenant.slug);
    expect(new Set(slugs).size).toBe(slugs.length);
  });
});
```

      `domains/identity/src/application/services/personal-workspaces.service.ts`:

```ts
import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { generateId } from '@platform/common';
import { deriveTenantSlug, personalWorkspaceName } from '../../domain/tenant-slug.js';
import {
  type MembershipRecord,
  MembershipsRepository,
} from '../persistence/memberships.repository.js';
import { TenantsRepository } from '../persistence/tenants.repository.js';

/**
 * The personal workspace every account gets (spec §5.2): a `personal` tenant named
 * `"<display name>'s workspace"`, slugged from the email's local part, and an owner membership marked
 * active now. Runs in the caller's unit of work (register; login of a pre-tenancy account).
 */
@Injectable()
export class PersonalWorkspaces {
  constructor(
    private readonly tenants: TenantsRepository,
    private readonly memberships: MembershipsRepository,
  ) {}

  async create(
    user: { id: string; email: string; displayName: string },
    now: Date,
  ): Promise<MembershipRecord> {
    const tenantId = generateId();
    const localPart = user.email.split('@')[0] ?? '';
    await this.tenants.insert({
      id: tenantId,
      slug: deriveTenantSlug(localPart, tenantId),
      name: personalWorkspaceName(user.displayName),
      personal: true,
    });
    await this.memberships.insert({
      tenantId,
      userId: user.id,
      roles: ['owner'],
      lastActiveAt: now,
    });
    return { tenantId, userId: user.id, roles: ['owner'], lastActiveAt: now, createdAt: now };
  }
}
```

`domains/identity/src/testing/in-memory-tenancy.ts`:

```ts
import { type CursorPage, normalizeTenantRoles } from '@platform/common';
import { decodeIdCursor, keysetPage } from '@platform/database';
import {
  type AuditEventRecord,
  AuditEventsRepository,
  type InvitationRecord,
  InvalidRolesException,
  InvitationsRepository,
  MembershipAlreadyExistsException,
  type MembershipRecord,
  MembershipsRepository,
  type NewAuditEvent,
  type NewInvitation,
  type NewMembership,
  type NewTenant,
  type TenantMembership,
  type TenantRecord,
  TenantSlugTakenException,
  TenantsRepository,
} from '../index.js';
import type { InMemoryIdentityStore } from './in-memory-identity.js';

/** Key of `InMemoryIdentityStore.memberships`. */
export const membershipKey = (tenantId: string, userId: string): string => `${tenantId}:${userId}`;

/** `ORDER BY last_active_at DESC NULLS LAST, created_at DESC`. */
function recentFirst(a: MembershipRecord, b: MembershipRecord): number {
  if (a.lastActiveAt === null || b.lastActiveAt === null) {
    if (a.lastActiveAt !== b.lastActiveAt) return a.lastActiveAt === null ? 1 : -1;
  } else if (a.lastActiveAt.getTime() !== b.lastActiveAt.getTime()) {
    return b.lastActiveAt.getTime() - a.lastActiveAt.getTime();
  }
  return b.createdAt.getTime() - a.createdAt.getTime();
}

export class InMemoryTenantsRepository extends TenantsRepository {
  constructor(private readonly store: InMemoryIdentityStore) {
    super();
  }

  async insert(tenant: NewTenant): Promise<void> {
    if (await this.existsBySlug(tenant.slug)) throw new TenantSlugTakenException();
    this.store.tenants.set(tenant.id, { ...tenant, createdAt: new Date() });
  }

  async findById(id: string): Promise<TenantRecord | null> {
    return this.store.tenants.get(id) ?? null;
  }

  async findByIds(ids: readonly string[]): Promise<TenantRecord[]> {
    return [...new Set(ids)].flatMap((id) => {
      const tenant = this.store.tenants.get(id);
      return tenant ? [tenant] : [];
    });
  }

  async existsBySlug(slug: string): Promise<boolean> {
    return [...this.store.tenants.values()].some((tenant) => tenant.slug === slug);
  }

  async rename(id: string, name: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const tenant = this.store.tenants.get(id);
    if (!tenant) return false;
    this.store.tenants.set(id, { ...tenant, name });
    return true;
  }
}

export class InMemoryMembershipsRepository extends MembershipsRepository {
  constructor(private readonly store: InMemoryIdentityStore) {
    super();
  }

  async insert(membership: NewMembership): Promise<void> {
    const roles = normalizeTenantRoles(membership.roles);
    if (roles.length === 0) {
      throw new InvalidRolesException([
        { path: 'roles', message: 'At least one role is required' },
      ]);
    }
    const key = membershipKey(membership.tenantId, membership.userId);
    if (this.store.memberships.has(key)) throw new MembershipAlreadyExistsException();
    this.store.memberships.set(key, { ...membership, roles, createdAt: new Date() });
  }

  async find(tenantId: string, userId: string): Promise<MembershipRecord | null> {
    return this.store.memberships.get(membershipKey(tenantId, userId)) ?? null;
  }

  async findMostRecentForUser(userId: string): Promise<MembershipRecord | null> {
    return this.ofUser(userId)[0] ?? null;
  }

  async listForUser(userId: string): Promise<TenantMembership[]> {
    return this.ofUser(userId).flatMap((membership) => {
      const tenant = this.store.tenants.get(membership.tenantId);
      return tenant ? [{ ...membership, tenant }] : [];
    });
  }

  async touch(tenantId: string, userId: string, at: Date): Promise<void> {
    const key = membershipKey(tenantId, userId);
    const membership = this.store.memberships.get(key);
    if (membership) this.store.memberships.set(key, { ...membership, lastActiveAt: at });
  }

  private ofUser(userId: string): MembershipRecord[] {
    return [...this.store.memberships.values()]
      .filter((m) => m.userId === userId)
      .sort(recentFirst);
  }
}

export class InMemoryInvitationsRepository extends InvitationsRepository {
  constructor(private readonly store: InMemoryIdentityStore) {
    super();
  }

  async insert(invitation: NewInvitation): Promise<void> {
    this.store.invitations.set(`${invitation.tenantId}:${invitation.id}`, {
      ...invitation,
      roles: [...invitation.roles],
      acceptedAt: null,
      createdAt: new Date(),
    });
  }

  async findByTokenHash(tokenHash: string): Promise<InvitationRecord | null> {
    return [...this.store.invitations.values()].find((i) => i.tokenHash === tokenHash) ?? null;
  }

  async listPending(tenantId: string, now: Date): Promise<InvitationRecord[]> {
    return [...this.store.invitations.values()]
      .filter((i) => i.tenantId === tenantId && i.acceptedAt === null && i.expiresAt > now)
      .sort((a, b) => b.id.localeCompare(a.id));
  }

  async markAccepted(tenantId: string, id: string, now: Date): Promise<boolean> {
    const key = `${tenantId}:${id}`;
    const invitation = this.store.invitations.get(key);
    if (!invitation || invitation.acceptedAt !== null || invitation.expiresAt <= now) return false;
    this.store.invitations.set(key, { ...invitation, acceptedAt: now });
    return true;
  }
}

export class InMemoryAuditEventsRepository extends AuditEventsRepository {
  constructor(private readonly store: InMemoryIdentityStore) {
    super();
  }

  async append(event: NewAuditEvent): Promise<void> {
    this.store.auditEvents.push({ ...event, data: { ...event.data } });
  }

  async listForTenant(
    tenantId: string,
    criteria: { limit: number; cursor?: string | undefined },
  ): Promise<CursorPage<AuditEventRecord>> {
    const after = criteria.cursor === undefined ? undefined : decodeIdCursor(criteria.cursor).id;
    const rows = this.store.auditEvents
      .filter((e) => e.tenantId === tenantId && (after === undefined || e.id < after))
      .sort((a, b) => b.id.localeCompare(a.id))
      .slice(0, criteria.limit + 1);
    return keysetPage(rows, criteria.limit);
  }
}
```

```ts
import { PLATFORM_TENANT_ID } from '@platform/common';
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { makeUserRecord } from '../../../test/fixtures.js';
import { TenantAccessDeniedException } from '../../domain/identity.errors.js';
import { createInMemoryIdentityPersistence } from '../../testing/in-memory-identity.js';
import { PersonalWorkspaces } from './personal-workspaces.service.js';
import { TenantSelection } from './tenant-selection.service.js';

const NOW = new Date('2026-11-09T10:00:00.000Z');
const OTHER = '0190f3b2-0000-7000-8000-0000000000bb';

describe('TenantSelection.forLogin', () => {
  let persistence: ReturnType<typeof createInMemoryIdentityPersistence>;
  let selection: TenantSelection;
  const user = makeUserRecord();

  beforeEach(() => {
    persistence = createInMemoryIdentityPersistence();
    const workspaces = new PersonalWorkspaces(persistence.tenants, persistence.memberships);
    selection = new TenantSelection(persistence.memberships, workspaces);
  });

  const member = async (
    tenantId: string,
    lastActiveAt: Date | null,
    roles: ('owner' | 'admin' | 'member')[] = ['member'],
  ) => {
    await persistence.tenants.insert({
      id: tenantId,
      slug: tenantId,
      name: tenantId,
      personal: false,
    });
    await persistence.memberships.insert({ tenantId, userId: user.id, roles, lastActiveAt });
  };

  it('without a tenantId: the most recently active membership, touched', async () => {
    await member('0190f3b2-0000-7000-8000-0000000000a1', new Date(1_000));
    await member('0190f3b2-0000-7000-8000-0000000000a2', new Date(2_000), ['admin']);
    const picked = await selection.forLogin(user, undefined, NOW);
    expect(picked).toMatchObject({
      tenantId: '0190f3b2-0000-7000-8000-0000000000a2',
      roles: ['admin'],
      lastActiveAt: NOW,
    });
    await expect(persistence.memberships.find(picked.tenantId, user.id)).resolves.toMatchObject({
      lastActiveAt: NOW,
    });
  });

  it('with a tenantId the user belongs to: that membership, touched', async () => {
    await member('0190f3b2-0000-7000-8000-0000000000a1', new Date(1_000), ['owner']);
    await member('0190f3b2-0000-7000-8000-0000000000a2', new Date(2_000));
    await expect(
      selection.forLogin(user, '0190f3b2-0000-7000-8000-0000000000a1', NOW),
    ).resolves.toMatchObject({
      tenantId: '0190f3b2-0000-7000-8000-0000000000a1',
      roles: ['owner'],
      lastActiveAt: NOW,
    });
  });

  it.each([
    ['a tenant the user is not a member of', OTHER],
    ['an unknown tenant', '0190f3b2-0000-7000-8000-00000000dead'],
    ['the platform tenant (nil UUID)', PLATFORM_TENANT_ID],
    ['a malformed id', "x'; drop table identity.users; --"],
  ])(
    'refuses %s with the same 403 TENANT_ACCESS_DENIED (Review Focus 1)',
    async (_name, tenantId) => {
      await member('0190f3b2-0000-7000-8000-0000000000a1', null);
      await persistence.tenants.insert({
        id: OTHER,
        slug: 'other',
        name: 'Other',
        personal: false,
      });
      await expect(selection.forLogin(user, tenantId, NOW)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(
        TenantAccessDeniedException,
      );
    },
  );

  it('a pre-tenancy account without any membership gets its personal workspace', async () => {
    const picked = await selection.forLogin(user, undefined, NOW);
    expect(picked).toMatchObject({ userId: user.id, roles: ['owner'], lastActiveAt: NOW });
    expect(persistence.store.tenants.get(picked.tenantId)).toMatchObject({ personal: true });
  });
});
```

```ts
import { sha256Hex } from '@platform/common';
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { makeUserRecord } from '../../../test/fixtures.js';
import { mockOf } from '../../../test/mocks.js';
import type { TokenIssuer } from '../../infrastructure/security/token-issuer.service.js';
import type { SessionsRepository } from '../persistence/sessions.repository.js';
import { SessionTokensService } from './session-tokens.service.js';

const TENANT = '0190f3b2-0000-7000-8000-0000000000aa';

describe('SessionTokensService', () => {
  const expiresAt = new Date('2026-10-06T12:00:00.000Z');
  let tokens: ReturnType<typeof mockOf<TokenIssuer>>;
  let sessions: ReturnType<typeof mockOf<SessionsRepository>>;
  let service: SessionTokensService;

  beforeEach(() => {
    tokens = mockOf<TokenIssuer>({
      issueRefreshToken: async () => ({ token: 'refresh-jwt', expiresAt }),
      issueAccessToken: async () => ({ token: 'access-jwt', jti: 'jti', expiresIn: 900, exp: 1 }),
    });
    sessions = mockOf<SessionsRepository>({ create: async () => undefined });
    service = new SessionTokensService(tokens, sessions);
  });

  it('opens a tenant-bound session (sha256 of the refresh token only) and returns the tenant with the pair', async () => {
    const user = makeUserRecord({ roles: ['admin', 'user'] });

    const result = await service.open({
      user,
      tenantId: TENANT,
      roles: ['owner'],
      client: { userAgent: ' curl/8 ', ip: '10.0.0.1' },
      sessionId: 'sid-1',
    });

    expect(tokens.issueRefreshToken).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ userId: user.id, sessionId: 'sid-1' });
    expect(sessions.create).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
      id: 'sid-1',
      userId: user.id,
      tenantId: TENANT,
      refreshTokenHash: sha256Hex('refresh-jwt'),
      expiresAt,
      userAgent: 'curl/8',
      ip: '10.0.0.1',
    });
    expect(result).toEqual({
      accessToken: 'access-jwt',
      refreshToken: 'refresh-jwt',
      expiresIn: 900,
      tokenType: 'Bearer',
      user: {
        id: user.id,
        email: user.email,
        displayName: user.displayName,
        roles: ['admin', 'user'],
        createdAt: user.createdAt,
        updatedAt: user.updatedAt,
      },
      tenantId: TENANT,
      tenantRoles: ['owner'],
    });
  });

  it('generates a uuidv7 session id and stores null client info when none is known', async () => {
    await service.open({ user: makeUserRecord(), tenantId: TENANT, roles: ['member'] });
    const [session] = sessions.create.mock.calls[0] ?? [];
    expect(session?.id).toMatch(/^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-7/);
    expect(session).toMatchObject({ tenantId: TENANT, userAgent: null, ip: null });
  });
});
```
