Replace `domains/identity/src/application/commands/login/login.handler.spec.ts` with:

```ts
import { Logger } from '@nestjs/common';
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import {
  makeAuthTokens,
  makeUserSnapshot,
  passThroughTransaction,
} from '../../../../test/fixtures.js';
import { mockOf } from '../../../../test/mocks.js';
import {
  InvalidCredentialsException,
  TenantAccessDeniedException,
} from '../../../domain/identity.errors.js';
import type { PasswordHasher } from '../../../infrastructure/security/password-hasher.service.js';
import type { MembershipRecord } from '../../persistence/memberships.repository.js';
import type { UsersRepository } from '../../persistence/users.repository.js';
import type { SessionTokensService } from '../../services/session-tokens.service.js';
import type { TenantSelection } from '../../services/tenant-selection.service.js';
import { LoginCommand } from './login.command.js';
import { LoginHandler } from './login.handler.js';

const TENANT = '0190f3b2-0000-7000-8000-0000000000aa';

describe('LoginHandler', () => {
  const tokens = makeAuthTokens();
  const stored = makeUserSnapshot();
  const membership: MembershipRecord = {
    tenantId: TENANT,
    userId: stored.id,
    roles: ['admin'],
    lastActiveAt: new Date(),
    createdAt: new Date(),
  };
  let users: ReturnType<typeof mockOf<UsersRepository>>;
  let hasher: ReturnType<typeof mockOf<PasswordHasher>>;
  let sessionTokens: ReturnType<typeof mockOf<SessionTokensService>>;
  let selection: ReturnType<typeof mockOf<TenantSelection>>;
  let transaction: ReturnType<typeof passThroughTransaction>;
  let handler: LoginHandler;

  beforeEach(() => {
    users = mockOf<UsersRepository>({
      findCredentialsByEmail: async () => stored,
      updatePasswordHash: async () => undefined,
    });
    hasher = mockOf<PasswordHasher>({
      verify: async () => true,
      verifyDummy: async () => false,
      needsRehash: () => false,
      hash: async () => '$argon2id$new',
    });
    sessionTokens = mockOf<SessionTokensService>({ open: async () => tokens });
    selection = mockOf<TenantSelection>({ forLogin: async () => membership });
    transaction = passThroughTransaction();
    handler = new LoginHandler(users, hasher, sessionTokens, transaction, selection);
  });

  it('verifies the password, picks the tenant, opens a session there (hash never passed on)', async () => {
    const client = { ip: '10.0.0.9' };
    await expect(
      handler.execute(new LoginCommand(' Ada@Example.com ', 'pw', client, TENANT)),
    ).resolves.toBe(tokens);

    expect(users.findCredentialsByEmail).toHaveBeenCalledWith('ada@example.com');
    expect(hasher.verify).toHaveBeenCalledWith(stored.passwordHash, 'pw');
    const [user, tenantId] = selection.forLogin.mock.calls[0] ?? [];
    expect(user).not.toHaveProperty('passwordHash');
    expect(tenantId).toBe(TENANT);
    const [input] = sessionTokens.open.mock.calls[0] ?? [];
    expect(input).toEqual({ user, tenantId: TENANT, roles: ['admin'], client });
    expect(transaction.runs).toBe(1);
  });

  it('a tenant the user may not use fails AFTER the password check (a thrown failure: C wave-1 contract)', async () => {
    selection.forLogin.mockRejectedValue(new TenantAccessDeniedException());
    await expect(
      handler.execute(new LoginCommand('ada@example.com', 'pw', undefined, TENANT)),
    ).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(TenantAccessDeniedException);
    expect(hasher.verify).toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(sessionTokens.open).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('unknown email: burns a dummy verification, then the same 401 as a wrong password', async () => {
    users.findCredentialsByEmail.mockResolvedValue(null);
    await expect(
      handler.execute(new LoginCommand('nobody@example.com', 'pw')),
    ).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(InvalidCredentialsException);
    expect(hasher.verifyDummy).toHaveBeenCalledWith('pw');
    expect(selection.forLogin).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('wrong password → InvalidCredentialsException, no tenant picked, no session', async () => {
    hasher.verify.mockResolvedValue(false);
    await expect(
      handler.execute(new LoginCommand('ada@example.com', 'bad')),
    ).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(InvalidCredentialsException);
    expect(selection.forLogin).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(sessionTokens.open).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('re-hashes with the current argon2 parameters; a failure there never fails the login', async () => {
    hasher.needsRehash.mockReturnValue(true);
    await handler.execute(new LoginCommand('ada@example.com', 'pw'));
    expect(users.updatePasswordHash).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      stored.id,
      '$argon2id$new',
      expect.any(Date),
    );

    const failure = new Error('db down');
    users.updatePasswordHash.mockRejectedValue(failure);
    const warn = vi.spyOn(Logger.prototype, 'warn').mockImplementation(() => undefined);
    await expect(handler.execute(new LoginCommand('ada@example.com', 'pw'))).resolves.toBe(tokens);
    expect(warn).toHaveBeenCalledExactlyOnceWith(
      { err: failure, userId: stored.id },
      'Password re-hash failed',
    );
  });
});
```

```ts
import { AuthErrorCode, type RefreshTokenClaims } from '@platform/auth';
import { generateId, isUuidV7, sha256Hex, UnauthenticatedException } from '@platform/common';
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import {
  makeAuthTokens,
  makeUserRecord,
  passThroughTransaction,
} from '../../../../test/fixtures.js';
import { mockOf } from '../../../../test/mocks.js';
import {
  InvalidRefreshTokenException,
  MembershipRevokedException,
  RefreshTokenReuseDetectedException,
  SessionExpiredException,
} from '../../../domain/identity.errors.js';
import type { TokenIssuer } from '../../../infrastructure/security/token-issuer.service.js';
import type { MembershipsRepository } from '../../persistence/memberships.repository.js';
import type { SessionRecord, SessionsRepository } from '../../persistence/sessions.repository.js';
import type { UsersRepository } from '../../persistence/users.repository.js';
import type { SessionTokensService } from '../../services/session-tokens.service.js';
import { RefreshSessionCommand } from './refresh-session.command.js';
import { RefreshSessionHandler } from './refresh-session.handler.js';

const REFRESH_TOKEN = 'header.payload.signature';
const TENANT = '0190f3b2-0000-7000-8000-0000000000aa';

describe('RefreshSessionHandler', () => {
  const user = makeUserRecord();
  const sessionId = generateId();
  const claims: RefreshTokenClaims = {
    sub: user.id,
    jti: sessionId,
    typ: 'refresh',
    iat: 1,
    exp: 2,
    iss: 'iss',
    aud: 'aud',
  };
  const storedSession = (overrides: Partial<SessionRecord> = {}): SessionRecord => ({
    id: sessionId,
    userId: user.id,
    tenantId: TENANT,
    refreshTokenHash: sha256Hex(REFRESH_TOKEN),
    userAgent: null,
    ip: null,
    expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + 60_000),
    revokedAt: null,
    replacedById: null,
    createdAt: new Date(),
    ...overrides,
  });
  const tokens = makeAuthTokens();
  const log: string[] = [];

  let tokenIssuer: ReturnType<typeof mockOf<TokenIssuer>>;
  let users: ReturnType<typeof mockOf<UsersRepository>>;
  let sessions: ReturnType<typeof mockOf<SessionsRepository>>;
  let memberships: ReturnType<typeof mockOf<MembershipsRepository>>;
  let sessionTokens: ReturnType<typeof mockOf<SessionTokensService>>;
  let handler: RefreshSessionHandler;

  beforeEach(() => {
    log.length = 0;
    tokenIssuer = mockOf<TokenIssuer>({ verifyRefreshToken: async () => claims });
    users = mockOf<UsersRepository>({ findById: async () => user });
    sessions = mockOf<SessionsRepository>({
      findById: async () => storedSession(),
      revokeForRotation: async () => {
        log.push('revoke-for-rotation');
        return true;
      },
      revoke: async () => {
        log.push('revoke');
        return true;
      },
      revokeAllForUser: async () => {
        log.push('revoke-all');
        return 3;
      },
    });
    memberships = mockOf<MembershipsRepository>({
      find: async () => ({
        tenantId: TENANT,
        userId: user.id,
        roles: ['admin'],
        lastActiveAt: null,
        createdAt: new Date(),
      }),
    });
    sessionTokens = mockOf<SessionTokensService>({
      open: async () => {
        log.push('open-successor');
        return tokens;
      },
    });
    const transaction = {
      ...passThroughTransaction(),
      run: async <T>(work: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> => {
        log.push('begin');
        const result = await work();
        log.push('commit');
        return result;
      },
    };
    handler = new RefreshSessionHandler(
      tokenIssuer,
      users,
      sessions,
      sessionTokens,
      transaction,
      memberships,
    );
  });

  const command = new RefreshSessionCommand(REFRESH_TOKEN, { ip: '1.2.3.4' });

  it("rotates atomically inside the session's tenant, with the membership's current roles", async () => {
    await expect(handler.execute(command)).resolves.toBe(tokens);
    expect(sessions.findById).toHaveBeenCalledWith(user.id, sessionId);
    expect(memberships.find).toHaveBeenCalledWith(TENANT, user.id);
    expect(log).toEqual(['begin', 'revoke-for-rotation', 'open-successor', 'commit']);
    const [input] = sessionTokens.open.mock.calls[0] ?? [];
    expect(input).toMatchObject({
      user,
      tenantId: TENANT,
      roles: ['admin'],
      client: { ip: '1.2.3.4' },
    });
    expect(isUuidV7(input?.sessionId)).toBe(true);
    expect(sessions.revokeForRotation).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
      id: sessionId,
      userId: user.id,
      refreshTokenHash: sha256Hex(REFRESH_TOKEN),
      replacedById: input?.sessionId,
      now: expect.any(Date),
    });
  });

  it('membership removed since login → session revoked, 401 MEMBERSHIP_REVOKED, nothing issued', async () => {
    memberships.find.mockResolvedValue(null);
    await expect(handler.execute(command)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(MembershipRevokedException);
    expect(sessions.revoke).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
      id: sessionId,
      userId: user.id,
      now: expect.any(Date),
    });
    expect(log).toEqual(['revoke']);
  });

  it('REUSE: a genuine but already-rotated token revokes every session, outside any transaction', async () => {
    sessions.findById.mockResolvedValue(storedSession({ revokedAt: new Date() }));
    await expect(handler.execute(command)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(
      RefreshTokenReuseDetectedException,
    );
    expect(sessions.revokeAllForUser).toHaveBeenCalledWith(user.id, expect.any(Date));
    expect(log).toEqual(['revoke-all']);
    expect(memberships.find).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('a concurrent rotation that wins between the read and the update is classified as reuse', async () => {
    sessions.findById
      .mockResolvedValueOnce(storedSession())
      .mockResolvedValueOnce(storedSession({ revokedAt: new Date() }));
    sessions.revokeForRotation.mockResolvedValue(false);
    await expect(handler.execute(command)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(
      RefreshTokenReuseDetectedException,
    );
    expect(sessionTokens.open).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('expired session → SessionExpiredException; unknown session or hash mismatch → InvalidRefreshTokenException', async () => {
    sessions.findById.mockResolvedValue(storedSession({ expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() - 1) }));
    await expect(handler.execute(command)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(SessionExpiredException);
    sessions.findById.mockResolvedValue(null);
    await expect(handler.execute(command)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(InvalidRefreshTokenException);
    sessions.findById.mockResolvedValue(storedSession({ refreshTokenHash: sha256Hex('other') }));
    await expect(handler.execute(command)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(InvalidRefreshTokenException);
    expect(sessions.revokeAllForUser).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('maps JWT failures before touching the database', async () => {
    tokenIssuer.verifyRefreshToken.mockRejectedValue(
      new UnauthenticatedException('expired', { code: AuthErrorCode.TOKEN_EXPIRED }),
    );
    await expect(handler.execute(command)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(SessionExpiredException);
    tokenIssuer.verifyRefreshToken.mockRejectedValue(new Error('bad signature'));
    await expect(handler.execute(command)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(InvalidRefreshTokenException);
    expect(users.findById).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('a deleted user → InvalidRefreshTokenException', async () => {
    users.findById.mockResolvedValue(null);
    await expect(handler.execute(command)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(InvalidRefreshTokenException);
    vi.clearAllMocks();
  });
});
```

      `identity.errors.ts`: add `PermissionDeniedException` to the `@platform/common` import and append:

```ts
/**
 * 403 — login or switch to a tenant the user may not use. One error for "not a member", "unknown
 * tenant" and the platform tenant, so tenant ids cannot be probed.
 */
export class TenantAccessDeniedException extends PermissionDeniedException {
  constructor() {
    super('You are not a member of this workspace', {
      code: IdentityErrorCode.TENANT_ACCESS_DENIED,
    });
  }
}

/** 401 — the session's membership was removed: the session is revoked, the client logs in again. */
export class MembershipRevokedException extends UnauthenticatedException {
  constructor() {
    super('Your access to this workspace was removed; please log in again', {
      code: IdentityErrorCode.MEMBERSHIP_REVOKED,
    });
  }
}
```

`domains/identity/src/application/services/tenant-selection.service.ts`:

```ts
import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { isPlatformTenantId, isUuid } from '@platform/common';
import { TenantAccessDeniedException } from '../../domain/identity.errors.js';
import {
  type MembershipRecord,
  MembershipsRepository,
} from '../persistence/memberships.repository.js';
import type { UserRecord } from '../persistence/users.repository.js';
import { PersonalWorkspaces } from './personal-workspaces.service.js';

/** Which tenant a login opens its session in (spec §5.2). Runs in the login's unit of work. */
@Injectable()
export class TenantSelection {
  constructor(
    private readonly memberships: MembershipsRepository,
    private readonly workspaces: PersonalWorkspaces,
  ) {}

  /**
   * `tenantId` given: that membership, else 403 (also for unknown, malformed and platform ids).
   * Absent: the most recently active membership; an account without any (created before B2) gets its
   * personal workspace. The chosen membership's `last_active_at` becomes `now`.
   * @throws TenantAccessDeniedException
   */
  async forLogin(
    user: UserRecord,
    tenantId: string | undefined,
    now: Date,
  ): Promise<MembershipRecord> {
    let membership: MembershipRecord | null;
    if (tenantId !== undefined) {
      membership =
        isUuid(tenantId) && !isPlatformTenantId(tenantId)
          ? await this.memberships.find(tenantId, user.id)
          : null;
      if (membership === null) throw new TenantAccessDeniedException();
    } else {
      membership = await this.memberships.findMostRecentForUser(user.id);
      if (membership === null) return this.workspaces.create(user, now);
    }
    await this.memberships.touch(membership.tenantId, user.id, now);
    return { ...membership, lastActiveAt: now };
  }
}
```

Replace the body of `session-tokens.service.ts` from the class doc comment to the end with:

```ts
/** Everything a new session needs: the user, its tenant and its roles there. */
export interface OpenSessionInput {
  readonly user: UserRecord;
  readonly tenantId: string;
  readonly roles: readonly TenantRole[];
  readonly client?: ClientInfo | null | undefined;
  /** The refresh token's `jti`; a fresh uuidv7 by default (rotation passes the successor id). */
  readonly sessionId?: string | undefined;
}

/**
 * Opens a session in one tenant and issues its token pair — shared by register, login, refresh and
 * switch: refresh JWT (own secret) with `jti` = session id, only `sha256(token)` stored; access JWT
 * (verified locally at the edge). Runs inside the caller's transaction when there is one.
 */
@Injectable()
export class SessionTokensService {
  constructor(
    private readonly tokens: TokenIssuer,
    private readonly sessions: SessionsRepository,
  ) {}

  async open(input: OpenSessionInput): Promise<AuthTokens> {
    const sessionId = input.sessionId ?? generateId();
    // Signing is CPU-only (HS256): both tokens in parallel, then a single INSERT.
    const [refresh, access] = await Promise.all([
      this.tokens.issueRefreshToken({ userId: input.user.id, sessionId }),
      this.tokens.issueAccessToken(toAccessTokenSubject(input.user)),
    ]);
    await this.sessions.create({
      id: sessionId,
      userId: input.user.id,
      tenantId: input.tenantId,
      refreshTokenHash: sha256Hex(refresh.token),
      expiresAt: refresh.expiresAt,
      ...toSessionClient(input.client),
    });
    return {
      accessToken: access.token,
      refreshToken: refresh.token,
      expiresIn: access.expiresIn,
      tokenType: BEARER_TOKEN_TYPE,
      user: toUserContract(input.user),
      tenantId: input.tenantId,
      tenantRoles: [...input.roles],
    };
  }
}
```

`refresh-session.handler.ts`: import `timingSafeEqualStr` from `@platform/common`, `isSessionActive` from
`../../../domain/session.js`, `MembershipRevokedException`, and `MembershipsRepository`; add the constructor
parameter `    private readonly memberships: MembershipsRepository,` after `transaction`; replace the body of
`execute` with

```ts
const claims = await this.verify(command.refreshToken);
const refreshTokenHash = sha256Hex(command.refreshToken);
const now = new Date();

// Fresh email for the new access token; roles come from the session tenant's membership.
const user = await this.users.findById(claims.sub);
if (!user) throw new InvalidRefreshTokenException();

// The session row decides the tenant (spec §5.2: refresh keeps it). Router query on Citus.
const session = await this.sessions.findById(claims.sub, claims.jti);
if (
  session !== null &&
  isSessionActive(session, now) &&
  timingSafeEqualStr(session.refreshTokenHash, refreshTokenHash)
) {
  const membership = await this.memberships.find(session.tenantId, user.id);
  if (membership === null) {
    await this.sessions.revoke({ id: session.id, userId: user.id, now });
    throw new MembershipRevokedException();
  }
  const successorId = generateId();
  const tokens = await this.transaction.run(async () => {
    const rotated = await this.sessions.revokeForRotation({
      id: claims.jti,
      userId: claims.sub,
      refreshTokenHash,
      replacedById: successorId,
      now,
    });
    return rotated
      ? this.sessionTokens.open({
          user,
          tenantId: session.tenantId,
          roles: membership.roles,
          client: command.client,
          sessionId: successorId,
        })
      : undefined;
  });
  if (tokens) return tokens;
}
throw await this.rejection(claims, refreshTokenHash, now);
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Sessions table and repository.** `identity.schema.ts`: replace the `sessions` table with

```ts
// citus: user (PK leads with user_id; refresh looks up (user_id = sub, id = jti): a router query)
export const sessions = identityPgSchema.table(
  'sessions',
  {
    /** = the refresh token's `jti` (no default: the app generates it before signing). */
    id: uuid().notNull(),
    userId: uuid()
      .notNull()
      .references(() => users.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
    /** A session (and its refresh token) belongs to exactly one tenant. */
    tenantId: uuid()
      .notNull()
      .references(() => tenants.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
    /** sha256 hex of the refresh token (never the token itself). */
    refreshTokenHash: text().notNull(),
    userAgent: text(),
    ip: text(),
    expiresAt: timestamptz().notNull(),
    revokedAt: timestamptz(),
    replacedById: uuid(),
    createdAt: timestamptz().notNull().defaultNow(),
  },
  (t) => [
    // Also serves "revoke all sessions of a user" (leading column) and the FK cascade.
    primaryKey({ name: 'sessions_user_id_id_pk', columns: [t.userId, t.id] }),
    // hourly purge `WHERE expires_at <= now()` (a multi-shard system DELETE once distributed).
    index('sessions_expires_at_idx').on(t.expiresAt),
  ],
);
```

the statements differently; the snapshot it wrote stays untouched):

```sql
-- Hand-added: sessions are disposable and tenant_id cannot be backfilled; everyone logs in again.
DELETE FROM "identity"."sessions";--> statement-breakpoint
DROP INDEX "identity"."sessions_user_id_idx";--> statement-breakpoint
-- Hand-written: drizzle-kit cannot name a column-level primary key (Postgres default name).
ALTER TABLE "identity"."sessions" DROP CONSTRAINT "sessions_pkey";--> statement-breakpoint
ALTER TABLE "identity"."sessions" ADD COLUMN "tenant_id" uuid NOT NULL;--> statement-breakpoint
ALTER TABLE "identity"."sessions" ADD CONSTRAINT "sessions_user_id_id_pk" PRIMARY KEY("user_id","id");--> statement-breakpoint
ALTER TABLE "identity"."sessions" ADD CONSTRAINT "sessions_tenant_id_tenants_id_fk" FOREIGN KEY ("tenant_id") REFERENCES "identity"."tenants"("id") ON DELETE cascade ON UPDATE no action;
```

- [ ] **Step 7: The gateway's fake identity-service learns tenants.** In `testing/fake-identity-ports.ts`: add
      `import { normalizeTenantRoles, type TenantRole } from '@platform/common';` (merge into the existing
      `@platform/common` import) and `MembershipRevokedException` to the `../index.js` import; extend
      `FakeIdentityState` with

```ts
  readonly tenants = new Map<string, { id: string; name: string; personal: boolean }>();
  readonly memberships: { tenantId: string; userId: string; roles: TenantRole[]; lastActiveAt: number }[] = [];
  /** Refresh-token session id → its user and tenant. */
  readonly sessions = new Map<string, { userId: string; tenantId: string }>();

  membership(tenantId: string, userId: string) {
    return this.memberships.find((m) => m.tenantId === tenantId && m.userId === userId);
  }

  /** Most recently active first. */
  membershipsOf(userId: string) {
    return this.memberships.filter((m) => m.userId === userId).sort((a, b) => b.lastActiveAt - a.lastActiveAt);
  }

  addMembership(tenantId: string, userId: string, roles: TenantRole[]): void {
    if (!this.tenants.has(tenantId)) this.tenants.set(tenantId, { id: tenantId, name: tenantId, personal: false });
    this.memberships.push({ tenantId, userId, roles: normalizeTenantRoles(roles), lastActiveAt: 0 });
  }

  removeMembership(tenantId: string, userId: string): void {
    const index = this.memberships.findIndex((m) => m.tenantId === tenantId && m.userId === userId);
    if (index >= 0) this.memberships.splice(index, 1);
  }
```

`refreshTokens` replaces `return this.issue(entry.user);` with

```ts
const session = this.state.sessions.get(claims.jti);
if (!session || session.userId !== entry.user.id) throw new InvalidRefreshTokenException();
this.state.sessions.delete(claims.jti);
if (!this.state.membership(session.tenantId, entry.user.id)) throw new MembershipRevokedException();
return this.issue(entry.user, session.tenantId);
```

and `issue(user: User, tenantId: string)` stores the refresh session and returns the tenant:

```ts
  private async issue(user: User, tenantId: string): Promise<AuthTokens> {
    const access = await this.tokens.issueAccessToken({
      id: user.id,
      email: user.email,
      roles: user.roles.filter(isRole),
    });
    const sessionId = generateId();
    this.state.sessions.set(sessionId, { userId: user.id, tenantId });
    const refresh = await this.tokens.issueRefreshToken({ userId: user.id, sessionId });
    return {
      accessToken: access.token,
      refreshToken: refresh.token,
      expiresIn: access.expiresIn,
      tokenType: 'Bearer',
      user,
      tenantId,
      tenantRoles: [...(this.state.membership(tenantId, user.id)?.roles ?? [])],
    };
  }
```

```ts
  const alan = await register('alan.sessions@example.com', 'Alan');

    method: 'POST',
    url: '/v1/auth/refresh',
    payload: { refreshToken: alan.refreshToken },
  });
  expect(refreshed.statusCode).toBe(401);
  expect(refreshed.json<{ code: string }>().code).toBe('MEMBERSHIP_REVOKED');

  const linus = await register('linus.sessions@example.com', 'Linus');
  for (const key of [...e2e.identity.store.memberships.keys()]) {
    if (key.endsWith(`:${linus.user.id}`)) e2e.identity.store.memberships.delete(key);
  }
  const login = await inject({
    method: 'POST',
    url: '/v1/auth/login',
    payload: { email: 'linus.sessions@example.com', password: PASSWORD },
  });
  expect(login.statusCode, login.body).toBe(200);
  const body = login.json<AuthTokensBody>();
  expect(body.tenantId).not.toBe(linus.tenantId);
  expect(e2e.identity.store.tenants.get(body.tenantId)).toMatchObject({ personal: true });
});
```

`apps/gateway/test/tenancy-sessions.e2e-spec.ts` (new; same header pattern as `apps/gateway/test/app.e2e-spec.ts`:
its `vi.hoisted` env block, `createGatewayE2eApp`):

```ts
await vi.hoisted(async () => {
  const { GATEWAY_E2E_ENV } = await import('./support/e2e-env.js');
  Object.assign(process.env, GATEWAY_E2E_ENV);
});

import { isUuidV7 } from '@platform/common';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { graphql } from './support/http.js';
import { createGatewayE2eApp, type GatewayE2e } from './support/gateway-e2e-app.js';

interface AuthTokensBody {
  accessToken: string;
  refreshToken: string;
  tenantId: string;
  tenantRoles: string[];
  user: { id: string };
}
const PASSWORD = 'correct horse battery staple';

describe('tenancy through the gateway (remote ports over the fake identity-service)', () => {
  let e2e: GatewayE2e;
  const inject = (...args: Parameters<GatewayE2e['app']['inject']>) => e2e.app.inject(...args);

  beforeAll(async () => {
    e2e = await createGatewayE2eApp();
  });

  afterAll(async () => {
    await e2e?.app.close();
  });

  it('REST register/refresh carry the tenant; GraphQL AuthPayload exposes tenantId and tenantRoles', async () => {
    const registered = await inject({
      method: 'POST',
      url: '/v1/auth/register',
      payload: { email: 'ada.gw@example.com', password: PASSWORD, displayName: 'Ada' },
    });
    expect(registered.statusCode, registered.body).toBe(201);
    const ada = registered.json<AuthTokensBody>();
    expect(isUuidV7(ada.tenantId)).toBe(true);
    expect(ada.tenantRoles).toEqual(['owner']);

    const refreshed = await inject({
      method: 'POST',
      url: '/v1/auth/refresh',
      payload: { refreshToken: ada.refreshToken },
    });
    expect(refreshed.json<AuthTokensBody>().tenantId).toBe(ada.tenantId);

    const login = await inject(
      graphql('mutation ($input: LoginInput!) { login(input: $input) { tenantId tenantRoles } }', {
        input: { email: 'ada.gw@example.com', password: PASSWORD },
      }),
    );
    expect(login.json()).toEqual({
      data: { login: { tenantId: ada.tenantId, tenantRoles: ['OWNER'] } },
    });
  });
});
```

### Task 9: Login with an optional `tenantId` on REST, GraphQL and gRPC (B2-8 edges)

**Files:**

- Modify: `domains/identity/contracts/src/proto/identity/v1/identity.proto` + `contracts/src/generated/**`
  (regenerated), `domains/identity/src/presentation/http/dto/login.dto.ts`,
  `domains/identity/src/presentation/http/strategies/local.strategy.ts`,
  `domains/identity/src/presentation/graphql/inputs/login.input.ts`,
  `domains/identity/src/presentation/graphql/auth.resolver.ts`,
  `domains/identity/src/presentation/grpc/identity-rpc.payloads.ts`,
  `domains/identity/src/application/mappers/identity-request.mapper.ts`,
  `domains/identity/src/application/ports/auth.port.ts` (doc comment),
  `domains/identity/src/testing/fake-identity-ports.ts` (`FakeAuthPort.login`), `docs/API.md` (auth rows)
- Test: `domains/identity/src/presentation/http/identity-http.spec.ts`,
  `domains/identity/src/presentation/graphql/identity-graphql.spec.ts`,
  `domains/identity/src/presentation/grpc/identity-rpc.payloads.spec.ts`,
  `domains/identity/src/application/mappers/mappers.spec.ts`, `apps/monolith/test/tenancy-sessions.e2e-spec.ts`,
  `apps/gateway/test/tenancy-sessions.e2e-spec.ts`, `apps/identity-service/test/app.e2e-spec.ts`

**Interfaces:** Consumes Task 8 (`LoginCommand(email, password, client?, tenantId?)`, `TenantSelection`,
`TenantAccessDeniedException`, `FakeIdentityState.membership()`/`membershipsOf()`/`addMembership()`). Produces
`LoginRequest.tenantId?: string | undefined` (proto `optional string tenant_id = 4`), REST `LoginDto.tenantId?`
(`@IsUUID()`), GraphQL `LoginInput.tenantId: ID` (nullable), `loginRequestSchema.tenantId` (zod, `z.uuid()`), and
`toLoginCommand(request)` passing `request.tenantId` as the fourth argument (I-8, I-9).

**Wave:** 1, Lane S (`b2/schema`), after Task 8. Last task of Lane S: merge `b2/schema` after it.

Edge rules this task fixes (Review Focus 1): a malformed `tenantId` is a 400 (REST and GraphQL validation) or
`INVALID_ARGUMENT` (gRPC, zod) before any handler runs; a well-formed id that is the nil UUID, unknown, or a
tenant the user is not a member of reaches `TenantSelection.forLogin` and fails with the one 403
`TENANT_ACCESS_DENIED`, after the password check (so a wrong password never reveals anything about tenants).
`LocalAuthGuard` passes any `DomainException` thrown by the strategy through unchanged
(`libs/auth/src/guards/auth-errors.ts:16`), so the 403 survives passport.

`identity-graphql.spec.ts`: add `TenantAccessDeniedException` to the `../../domain/identity.errors.js` import
and append inside the top-level `describe`:

```ts
it('login(input: { tenantId }) forwards the tenant; a malformed id is a validation error; 403 keeps its code', async () => {
  authPort.login.mockResolvedValue(makeAuthTokens());
  const tenantId = '0190f3b2-0000-7000-8000-0000000000aa';
  const ok = await gql<{ login: { tenantId: string } }>(
    'mutation ($input: LoginInput!) { login(input: $input) { tenantId } }',
    { input: { email: 'ada@example.com', password: 'pw', tenantId } },
  );
  expect(ok.errors).toBeUndefined();
  expect(authPort.login).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
    expect.objectContaining({ email: 'ada@example.com', password: 'pw', tenantId }),
  );

  authPort.login.mockClear();
  const malformed = await gql(
    'mutation ($input: LoginInput!) { login(input: $input) { tenantId } }',
    {
      input: { email: 'ada@example.com', password: 'pw', tenantId: 'nope' },
    },
  );
  expect(malformed.errors?.[0]?.extensions?.status).toBe(400);
  expect(authPort.login).not.toHaveBeenCalled();

  authPort.login.mockRejectedValue(new TenantAccessDeniedException());
  const denied = await gql('mutation ($input: LoginInput!) { login(input: $input) { tenantId } }', {
    input: { email: 'ada@example.com', password: 'pw', tenantId },
  });
  expect(denied.errors?.[0]?.extensions).toMatchObject({
    code: 'TENANT_ACCESS_DENIED',
    status: 403,
  });
});
```

```ts
describe('loginRequestSchema.tenantId', () => {

    loginRequestSchema.safeParse({ email: 'ada@example.com', password: 'pw', tenantId });

  it('absent, null or "" (proto3 unset optional) → undefined', () => {
```

`local.strategy.ts`: replace the `validate` method with

```ts
  validate(
    request: RequestLike & { body?: unknown },
    email: string,
    password: string,
  ): Promise<AuthTokens> {
    // passport-local hands over only the two credential fields; the tenant comes from the body that
    // LoginRequestGuard has already validated (a UUID or absent).
    const { tenantId } = (request.body ?? {}) as { tenantId?: string };
    return this.auth.login({
      email,
      password,
      client: clientInfoOf(request),
      ...(tenantId === undefined ? {} : { tenantId }),
    });
  }
```

(`RequestLike` in `libs/common/src/context/execution-context.util.ts:7` has no `body`, hence the intersection.)
`login.input.ts`: change the imports to `import { Field, ID, InputType } from '@nestjs/graphql';` and
`import { IsEmail, IsNotEmpty, IsOptional, IsString, IsUUID, MaxLength } from 'class-validator';`, and append:

```ts
  @Field(() => ID, {
    nullable: true,
    description: 'Tenant to open the session in; default: your most recently active workspace',
  })
  @IsOptional()
  @IsUUID()
  tenantId?: string | null;
```

(`| null`: GraphQL delivers an explicit `null` for a nullable argument, and the resolver's `=== null` check
below does not compile against `string | undefined`, TS2367.)

```ts
/** proto3 `optional string` holding a UUID: unset (absent, null or '') → undefined. */
const optionalUuid = z
  .string()
  .nullish()
  .transform((value) => (value === null || value === '' ? undefined : value))
  .pipe(z.uuid().optional());
```

```ts
const membership =
  input.tenantId === undefined
    ? this.state.membershipsOf(entry.user.id)[0]
    : this.state.membership(input.tenantId, entry.user.id);
if (!membership) {
  if (input.tenantId !== undefined) throw new TenantAccessDeniedException();
  throw new InvalidCredentialsException();
}
membership.lastActiveAt = Date.now();
return this.issue(entry.user, membership.tenantId);
```

**Interfaces:** Consumes Tasks 1, 2, 8, 9. Produces I-3 exactly: `AccessTokenClaims` (`tid`, `roles: TenantRole[]`,
`staff: boolean`), `AuthUser` (`tenantId`, `roles: TenantRole[]`, `staff`), `AccessTokenSubject` (`tenantId`,
`roles: readonly TenantRole[]`, `staff?`), `parseAccessTokenClaims`, `toAuthUser(claims, permissions)`,
`isAuthUser`, `TokenService.toAuthUser(claims)` (registry resolves `claims.roles` directly),
`TokenIssuer.issueAccessToken(subject, options?: { ttlSec?: number })`, and in `@platform/auth/testing`
`TEST_TENANT_ID`, `type TestAccessTokenSubject`, `signTestAccessToken(subject: TestAccessTokenSubject, options?)`,
`makeAuthUser(overrides?, rbac?)` (defaults `roles: ['member']`, `tenantId: TEST_TENANT_ID`, `staff: false`).
`GET /v1/auth/me` gains `tenantId`, `tenantRoles`, `staff` (`MeResponse`). `FakeIdentityState.setMembershipRoles()`.
`BILLING_RBAC.grants.admin` and `FILES_RBAC.grants.admin` lose `billing:read-all` / `files:manage` (I-5).
`legacyTenantRoles` deleted. `Role`, `ROLE_VALUES`, `isRole` stay until Task 11 (identity's global roles still use
them).

**Wave:** 2, Lane T (`b2/tokens`), after `b2/platform` (Task 2) and `b2/schema` (Task 9) are merged. Nothing else
runs in wave 2. Do not merge Lane T before Task 12: from this task on every registrant owns a workspace, so
`users:read` (owner ⊇ admin) would let any owner list every user until Task 12 scopes the directory.

Wire rules (Review Focus 2): `tid` is required, a UUID, never `PLATFORM_TENANT_ID`; a token without it (every
pre-B2 token) is 401 `INVALID_TOKEN` with `details.reason`; `roles` must be an array (else 401), its unknown values
are dropped and duplicates removed (`normalizeTenantRoles`); `staff` is `true` only for the JSON boolean `true`;
the issuer writes the `staff` claim only when it is `true` (switch-in tokens, Task 15), so normal tokens have no
`staff` key. Access tokens live 15 minutes, so the cut-over logs nobody out for longer than that: the client's next
refresh gets a `tid` token (refresh tokens did not change).

```ts
import { PLATFORM_TENANT_ID, UnauthenticatedException } from '@platform/common';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { makeAuthUser } from '../testing/auth-test.utils.js';
import { TEST_TENANT_ID } from '../testing/test-tenant.js';
import {
  isAuthUser,
  parseAccessTokenClaims,
  parseRefreshTokenClaims,
  toAuthUser,
} from './token-claims.js';

const access = {
  sub: 'u1',
  email: 'a@b.c',
  tid: TEST_TENANT_ID,
  roles: ['member'],
  typ: 'access',
  jti: 'j1',
  iat: 100,
  exp: 1_000,
  iss: 'nestjs-boilerplate',
  aud: 'nestjs-boilerplate',
};

const rejects = (fn: () => unknown, reason: RegExp): void => {
  try {
    fn();
  } catch (error) {
    expect(error).toBeInstanceOf(UnauthenticatedException);
    expect((error as UnauthenticatedException).code).toBe('INVALID_TOKEN');
    expect(JSON.stringify((error as UnauthenticatedException).details)).toMatch(reason);
    return;
  }
  throw new Error('expected a rejection');
};

describe('parseAccessTokenClaims', () => {
  it('accepts well-formed access claims; staff defaults to false', () => {
    expect(parseAccessTokenClaims(access)).toEqual({ ...access, roles: ['member'], staff: false });
  });

  it('rejects refresh tokens and malformed payloads', () => {
    rejects(() => parseAccessTokenClaims({ ...access, typ: 'refresh' }), /expected a access token/);
    rejects(() => parseAccessTokenClaims({ ...access, typ: undefined }), /expected a access/);
    rejects(() => parseAccessTokenClaims('token'), /not an object/);
    rejects(() => parseAccessTokenClaims({ ...access, sub: '' }), /sub\/jti/);
    rejects(() => parseAccessTokenClaims({ ...access, exp: '1000' }), /iat\/exp/);
    rejects(() => parseAccessTokenClaims({ ...access, aud: undefined }), /iss\/aud/);
    rejects(() => parseAccessTokenClaims({ ...access, email: 1 }), /email/);
  });

  // Review Focus 2: hostile or legacy claims.
  it.each([
    ['a pre-B2 token without tid', { tid: undefined }, /missing or malformed tid/],
    ['a tid that is not a UUID', { tid: 'tenant-1' }, /missing or malformed tid/],
    ['a numeric tid', { tid: 7 }, /missing or malformed tid/],
    ['the platform tenant', { tid: PLATFORM_TENANT_ID }, /platform tenant/],
    ['roles that are not an array', { roles: 'member' }, /missing roles/],
    ['roles that are an object', { roles: { 0: 'owner' } }, /missing roles/],
  ])('rejects %s with 401 INVALID_TOKEN and a reason', (_name, patch, reason) => {
    rejects(() => parseAccessTokenClaims({ ...access, ...patch }), reason);
  });

  it('drops unknown roles, removes duplicates and keeps the canonical order', () => {
    expect(
      parseAccessTokenClaims({
        ...access,
        roles: ['member', 'superhero', 7, 'owner', 'member', 'user', 'admin'],
      }).roles,
    ).toEqual(['owner', 'admin', 'member']);
  });

  it.each([
    [true, true],
    ['true', false],
    [1, false],
    [null, false],
    [undefined, false],
    [{}, false],
  ])('staff %j → %j (only the JSON boolean true counts)', (staff, expected) => {
    expect(parseAccessTokenClaims({ ...access, staff }).staff).toBe(expected);
  });
});

describe('parseRefreshTokenClaims', () => {
  it('accepts refresh claims and rejects access tokens', () => {
    const refresh = {
      sub: 'u1',
      jti: 'session-1',
      typ: 'refresh',
      iat: 1,
      exp: 2,
      iss: 'i',
      aud: 'a',
    };
    expect(parseRefreshTokenClaims({ ...refresh, extra: true })).toEqual(refresh);
    rejects(() => parseRefreshTokenClaims(access), /expected a refresh token/);
  });
});

describe('toAuthUser / isAuthUser', () => {
  it('maps claims and the resolved permissions to the principal', () => {
    const user = toAuthUser(parseAccessTokenClaims({ ...access, roles: ['admin'], staff: true }), [
      'orders:read',
    ]);
    expect(user).toEqual({
      id: 'u1',
      email: 'a@b.c',
      tenantId: TEST_TENANT_ID,
      roles: ['admin'],
      staff: true,
      permissions: ['orders:read'],
      jti: 'j1',
      exp: 1_000,
    });
    expect(isAuthUser(user)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('rejects anything that is not a full AuthUser', () => {
    expect(isAuthUser(makeAuthUser())).toBe(true);
    expect(isAuthUser(undefined)).toBe(false);
    expect(isAuthUser({ id: 'u1' })).toBe(false);
    expect(isAuthUser({ ...makeAuthUser(), roles: ['user'] })).toBe(false);
    expect(isAuthUser({ ...makeAuthUser(), permissions: ['files:*'] })).toBe(false);
    expect(isAuthUser({ ...makeAuthUser(), exp: 'soon' })).toBe(false);
    expect(isAuthUser({ ...makeAuthUser(), tenantId: undefined })).toBe(false);
    expect(isAuthUser({ ...makeAuthUser(), staff: 'false' })).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

Replace `libs/auth/src/guards/rbac.guards.spec.ts` with (Task 13 extends it with the role hierarchy and
`@StaffOnly`):

```ts
import { Reflector } from '@nestjs/core';
import { PermissionDeniedException, UnauthenticatedException } from '@platform/common';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
  RequireAnyPermission,
  RequirePermissions,
} from '../decorators/require-permissions.decorator.js';
import { Roles } from '../decorators/roles.decorator.js';
import { makeAuthUser } from '../testing/auth-test.utils.js';
import { createTestRbacRegistry, TEST_RBAC_FEATURE } from '../testing/rbac-test.utils.js';
import { executionContext, type Transport } from './execution-context.test.js';
import { PermissionsGuard } from './permissions.guard.js';
import { RolesGuard } from './roles.guard.js';

@Roles('admin')
@RequirePermissions('orders:read')
class OrdersController {
  list(): void {
    // inherits the class-level requirements
  }

  @Roles('owner')
  @RequirePermissions('orders:approve', 'alerts:write')
  approve(): void {
    // handler-level metadata overrides the class
  }

  @RequireAnyPermission('files:manage', 'orders:write')
  cleanup(): void {
    // any-of requirement
  }
}

class OpenController {
  ping(): void {
    // no requirements
  }
}

const reflector = new Reflector();
const rolesGuard = new RolesGuard(reflector);
const permissionsGuard = new PermissionsGuard(reflector);

const ctx = (
  handler: () => void,
  user?: unknown,
  type: Transport = 'http',
  cls: abstract new (...args: never[]) => unknown = OrdersController,
) => executionContext(type, { handler, cls }, { headers: {}, user });

const rbac = createTestRbacRegistry(TEST_RBAC_FEATURE);
const member = makeAuthUser({ roles: ['member'] }, rbac);
const admin = makeAuthUser({ roles: ['admin'] }, rbac);
const owner = makeAuthUser({ roles: ['owner'] }, rbac);
const { list, approve, cleanup } = OrdersController.prototype;

describe('RolesGuard', () => {
  it('passes when the user holds a required role (class-level metadata)', () => {
    expect(rolesGuard.canActivate(ctx(list, admin))).toBe(true);
  });

  it('handler metadata overrides class metadata', () => {
    expect(() => rolesGuard.canActivate(ctx(approve, admin))).toThrow(PermissionDeniedException);
    expect(rolesGuard.canActivate(ctx(approve, owner))).toBe(true);
  });

  it('403s with the required roles, 401s without a user', () => {
    try {
      rolesGuard.canActivate(ctx(list, member));
      throw new Error('expected a rejection');
    } catch (error) {
      expect(error).toBeInstanceOf(PermissionDeniedException);
      expect((error as PermissionDeniedException).details).toEqual({ requiredRoles: ['admin'] });
    }
    expect(() => rolesGuard.canActivate(ctx(list, undefined))).toThrow(UnauthenticatedException);
  });

  it('passes without metadata and for rpc handlers', () => {
    expect(
      rolesGuard.canActivate(ctx(OpenController.prototype.ping, undefined, 'http', OpenController)),
    ).toBe(true);
    expect(rolesGuard.canActivate(ctx(list, undefined, 'rpc'))).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe('PermissionsGuard', () => {
  it("'all' mode: every permission is required", () => {
    expect(permissionsGuard.canActivate(ctx(list, admin))).toBe(true);
    expect(() => permissionsGuard.canActivate(ctx(list, member))).toThrow(
      PermissionDeniedException,
    );
    expect(permissionsGuard.canActivate(ctx(approve, admin))).toBe(true);
    expect(() =>
      permissionsGuard.canActivate(ctx(approve, makeAuthUser({ permissions: ['orders:approve'] }))),
    ).toThrow(PermissionDeniedException);
  });

  it("'any' mode: one matching permission is enough", () => {
    const custom = makeAuthUser({ permissions: ['orders:write'] });
    expect(permissionsGuard.canActivate(ctx(cleanup, custom))).toBe(true);
    try {
      permissionsGuard.canActivate(ctx(cleanup, member));
      throw new Error('expected a rejection');
    } catch (error) {
      expect((error as PermissionDeniedException).details).toEqual({
        requiredPermissions: ['files:manage', 'orders:write'],
        mode: 'any',
      });
    }
  });

  it('works for GraphQL and WebSocket requests too', () => {
    expect(permissionsGuard.canActivate(ctx(list, admin, 'graphql'))).toBe(true);
    expect(
      permissionsGuard.canActivate(
        executionContext('ws', { handler: list, cls: OrdersController }, { data: { user: admin } }),
      ),
    ).toBe(true);
  });

  it('passes without metadata / for rpc, 401s without a user', () => {
    expect(
      permissionsGuard.canActivate(
        ctx(OpenController.prototype.ping, undefined, 'http', OpenController),
      ),
    ).toBe(true);
    expect(permissionsGuard.canActivate(ctx(list, undefined, 'rpc'))).toBe(true);
    expect(() => permissionsGuard.canActivate(ctx(list, undefined))).toThrow(
      UnauthenticatedException,
    );
  });
});
```

`libs/auth/src/types/auth-user.ts`: replace the two `import type` lines with
`import type { TenantRole } from '@platform/common';`; replace the `AuthUser`, `AccessTokenClaims` and
`AccessTokenSubject` interfaces with:

```ts
/**
 * The authenticated principal attached to `req.user` (HTTP / GraphQL) and `socket.data.user` (WS).
 * Built from verified access-token claims only — no DB lookup per request.
 */
export interface AuthUser {
  /** The user id (`sub`). */
  id: string;
  email: string;
  /** The active tenant (`tid`): every request runs in exactly one tenant. */
  tenantId: string;
  /** The user's roles in `tenantId` (owner ⊇ admin ⊇ member). */
  roles: TenantRole[];
  /** true only for a platform-staff switch-in token. */
  staff: boolean;
  /** Resolved from `roles` by `RbacRegistry` when the token is verified; never in the token. */
  permissions: string[];
  /** Access-token id (uuidv7) — what logout puts on the denylist. */
  jti: string;
  /** Access-token expiry, epoch seconds. */
  exp: number;
}

/** Registered + private claims of an access token (short-lived, verified at the edge). */
export interface AccessTokenClaims {
  sub: string;
  email: string;
  /** Active tenant: a UUID, never PLATFORM_TENANT_ID. Required. */
  tid: string;
  /** Roles in `tid`; unknown values dropped when parsed. */
  roles: TenantRole[];
  /** On the wire only as `staff: true` (switch-in tokens); anything else parses as false. */
  staff: boolean;
  jti: string;
  typ: 'access';
  iat: number;
  exp: number;
  iss: string;
  aud: string;
}

/** What an access-token issuer (and `signTestAccessToken`) needs to know about the user. */
export interface AccessTokenSubject {
  id: string;
  email: string;
  tenantId: string;
  roles: readonly TenantRole[];
  /** Only a staff switch-in token sets it. */
  staff?: boolean;
}
```

`libs/auth/src/tokens/token-claims.ts`: replace `import { UnauthenticatedException } from '@platform/common';`
with
`import { isPlatformTenantId, isTenantRole, isUuid, normalizeTenantRoles, UnauthenticatedException } from '@platform/common';`,
delete `import { isRole } from '../rbac/role.enum.js';`, and replace `parseAccessTokenClaims`, `toAuthUser` and
`isAuthUser` with:

```ts
/**
 * Validates the shape of verified access-token claims. `tid` is required (a pre-B2 token has none:
 * 401 and the client refreshes). Unknown roles are DROPPED rather than rejected: during a rolling
 * deploy a newer issuer may know roles this service doesn't — least privilege, without locking those
 * users out. `staff` is true only for the JSON boolean `true`.
 */
export function parseAccessTokenClaims(payload: unknown): AccessTokenClaims {
  const base = parseBase(payload, 'access');
  const { email, roles, tid, staff } = base;
  if (!isString(email)) throw invalid('missing email');
  if (!isArray(roles)) throw invalid('missing roles');
  if (isPlatformTenantId(tid)) throw invalid('tid is the platform tenant');
  if (!isString(tid) || !isUuid(tid)) throw invalid('missing or malformed tid');
  return {
    sub: base.sub,
    email,
    tid,
    roles: normalizeTenantRoles(roles),
    staff: staff === true,
    jti: base.jti,
    typ: 'access',
    iat: base.iat,
    exp: base.exp,
    iss: base.iss,
    aud: base.aud,
  };
}

/** Builds `req.user` from verified claims and the permissions their roles resolve to. */
export function toAuthUser(claims: AccessTokenClaims, permissions: readonly string[]): AuthUser {
  return {
    id: claims.sub,
    email: claims.email,
    tenantId: claims.tid,
    roles: [...claims.roles],
    staff: claims.staff,
    permissions: [...permissions],
    jti: claims.jti,
    exp: claims.exp,
  };
}

/** Structural guard for `req.user` / `socket.data.user` (untyped at the transport layer). */
export function isAuthUser(value: unknown): value is AuthUser {
  if (typeof value !== 'object' || value === null) return false;
  const user = value as ClaimsRecord;
  return (
    nonEmpty(user.id) &&
    isString(user.email) &&
    nonEmpty(user.tenantId) &&
    isArray(user.roles) &&
    user.roles.every(isTenantRole) &&
    typeof user.staff === 'boolean' &&
    isArray(user.permissions) &&
    user.permissions.every(isPermissionName) &&
    isString(user.jti) &&
    isInteger(user.exp)
  );
}
```

```ts
import { generateId } from '@platform/common';
import type { RbacRegistry } from '../rbac/rbac.registry.js';
import type { AuthUser } from '../types/auth-user.js';
import { TEST_TENANT_ID } from './test-tenant.js';

/**
 * A valid `AuthUser` for tests: a member of `TEST_TENANT_ID`, token valid for 15 min. Permissions:
 * the given ones, else those `rbac` resolves for the roles (like production), else none.
 */
export function makeAuthUser(overrides: Partial<AuthUser> = {}, rbac?: RbacRegistry): AuthUser {
  const roles = overrides.roles ?? ['member'];
  return {
    id: generateId(),
    email: 'user@example.com',
    tenantId: TEST_TENANT_ID,
    staff: false,
    jti: generateId(),
    exp: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1_000) + 900,
    ...overrides,
    roles,
    permissions: overrides.permissions ?? rbac?.resolve(roles) ?? [],
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Identity issues `tid` tokens; `me` shows the tenant.** Replace
      `domains/identity/src/infrastructure/security/token-issuer.parity.spec.ts` with:

```ts
import { JwtService } from '@nestjs/jwt';
import { signTestAccessToken } from '@platform/auth/testing';
import { authConfig } from '@platform/config';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { refreshTokenConfig } from './refresh-token.config.js';
import { TokenIssuer } from './token-issuer.service.js';

/**
 * Platform and domain specs authenticate with `signTestAccessToken` (`@platform/auth/testing`)
 * instead of identity's issuer, so its tokens must keep matching production issuance. A libs spec
 * cannot import identity: the check lives here. Non-default issuer and audience, so a signer that
 * ignored the config would not match by accident.
 */
const access = authConfig.parse({
  NODE_ENV: 'test',
  JWT_ISSUER: 'parity-issuer',
  JWT_AUDIENCE: 'parity-audience',
  JWT_ACCESS_TTL_SEC: '600',
});
const jwt = new JwtService();
const issuer = new TokenIssuer(jwt, access, refreshTokenConfig.parse({ NODE_ENV: 'test' }));
const subject = {
  id: '0190f3b2-0000-7000-8000-000000000001',
  email: 'a@b.c',
  tenantId: '0190f3b2-0000-7000-8000-0000000000aa',
  roles: ['owner', 'member'] as const,
};

type DecodedClaims = Record<string, unknown> & { iat: number; exp: number };

describe('signTestAccessToken parity with TokenIssuer', () => {
  it('signs the claims production issues: same keys; same sub, iss, aud, typ, email, tid, roles and TTL', async () => {
    const issued = jwt.decode<DecodedClaims>((await issuer.issueAccessToken(subject)).token);
    const signed = jwt.decode<DecodedClaims>(
      (await signTestAccessToken(subject, { config: access })).token,
    );

    expect(Object.keys(signed).sort()).toEqual(Object.keys(issued).sort());
    expect(issued).not.toHaveProperty('staff');
    for (const claim of ['sub', 'iss', 'aud', 'typ', 'email', 'tid', 'roles']) {
      expect(signed[claim], claim).toEqual(issued[claim]);
    }
    expect(issued.exp - issued.iat).toBe(600);
    expect(signed.exp - signed.iat).toBe(issued.exp - issued.iat);
    expect(issued).toMatchObject({ iss: 'parity-issuer', aud: 'parity-audience' });
  });

  it('both write `staff: true` for a staff subject, and the issuer honours a shorter ttlSec', async () => {
    const staffSubject = { ...subject, roles: ['admin'] as const, staff: true };
    const issued = await issuer.issueAccessToken(staffSubject, { ttlSec: 120 });
    const decoded = jwt.decode<DecodedClaims>(issued.token);
    const signed = jwt.decode<DecodedClaims>(
      (await signTestAccessToken(staffSubject, { config: access })).token,
    );
    expect(decoded.staff).toBe(true);
    expect(signed.staff).toBe(true);
    expect(decoded.exp - decoded.iat).toBe(120);
    expect(issued.expiresIn).toBe(120);
  });
});
```

`token-issuer.service.ts`: replace `issueAccessToken` with

```ts
  /**
   * `options.ttlSec` shortens the lifetime (staff switch-in, Task 15); default `JWT_ACCESS_TTL_SEC`.
   * The `staff` claim is written only when true, so normal tokens carry no `staff` key.
   */
  async issueAccessToken(
    subject: AccessTokenSubject,
    options: { ttlSec?: number } = {},
  ): Promise<IssuedAccessToken> {
    const ttlSec = options.ttlSec ?? this.access.accessTtlSec;
    const iat = nowSec();
    const exp = iat + ttlSec;
    const jti = generateId();
    const payload = {
      sub: subject.id,
      email: subject.email,
      tid: subject.tenantId,
      roles: [...subject.roles],
      ...(subject.staff === true ? { staff: true } : {}),
      typ: 'access',
      jti,
      iat,
      exp,
    };
    const token = await this.jwt.signAsync(payload, this.signOptions(this.access.accessSecret));
    return { token, jti, expiresIn: ttlSec, exp };
  }
```

```ts
/** What goes into the access token: the user, the session's tenant and the roles held there. */
export function toAccessTokenSubject(
  user: UserRecord,
  tenantId: string,
  roles: readonly TenantRole[],
): AccessTokenSubject {
  return { id: user.id, email: user.email, tenantId, roles: [...roles] };
}
```

`domains/identity/src/presentation/http/responses/me.response.ts`:

```ts
import type { User } from '@domain/identity-contracts';
import { ApiProperty } from '@nestjs/swagger';
import type { AuthUser } from '@platform/auth';
import { TENANT_ROLES, type TenantRole } from '@platform/common';
import { Exclude, Expose } from 'class-transformer';
import { toUserView } from '../../shared/user-view.js';
import { UserResponse } from './user.response.js';

/** `GET /v1/auth/me`: the profile plus the token's tenant context (spec §5.3). */
@Exclude()
export class MeResponse extends UserResponse {
  @Expose()
  @ApiProperty({ format: 'uuid', description: 'The active tenant (the access token `tid`).' })
  tenantId: string;

  @Expose()
  @ApiProperty({ enum: TENANT_ROLES, enumName: 'TenantRole', isArray: true, example: ['owner'] })
  tenantRoles: TenantRole[];

  @Expose()
  @ApiProperty({ description: 'true only for a platform-staff switch-in token.', example: false })
  staff: boolean;

  /** The profile from identity, the tenant context from the verified token. */
  static forPrincipal(user: User, principal: AuthUser): MeResponse {
    return Object.assign(new MeResponse(), toUserView(user), {
      tenantId: principal.tenantId,
      tenantRoles: [...principal.roles],
      staff: principal.staff,
    });
  }
}
```

```ts
/** A real access token for a user with `roles` in `tenantId`, signed with the app's `auth` config. */
export async function principal(
  app: INestApplication,
  roles: TenantRole[],
  id: string = generateId(),
  tenantId: string = TEST_TENANT_ID,
): Promise<TestPrincipal> {
  const issued = await signTestAccessToken(
    { id, email: 'p@example.com', tenantId, roles },
    { config: app.get<AuthConfig>(authConfig.KEY) },
  );
  return { id, token: issued.token, jti: issued.jti, exp: issued.exp };
}
```

```ts
    method: 'GET',
```

**Interfaces:** Consumes Tasks 4, 10, 11. Produces (I-7, I-8, I-9): `ListUsersCriteria.tenantId: string`
(required; `list` joins `memberships`), `UsersRepository.findByIdInTenant(id, tenantId): Promise<UserRecord | null>`,
`ListUsersQuery(tenantId: string, limit?, cursor?, search?)`, `GetUserByIdQuery(id: string, tenantId?: string)`,
`UsersPort.getUser(id: string, scope?: { tenantId: string }): Promise<User>`, `UsersPort.listUsers(query)` with
`query.tenantId` required by every caller, proto `GetUserRequest.tenant_id = 2` (optional) and
`ListUsersRequest.tenant_id = 4` (optional on the wire, required by `listUsersRequestSchema`).

**Wave:** 2, Lane T, after Task 11. After this task the controller may merge `b2/tokens` (Task 13 may follow in the
same merge).

Rules: `GET /v1/users` and GraphQL `users` list the members of the caller's active tenant only (`users:read` =
admin ⊇ owner). `GET /v1/users/:id` and GraphQL `user(id)`: your own profile always (unscoped, cached); anyone
else only with `users:read` AND a membership in your active tenant, else 404 (not 403: a user of another tenant is
not visible). Scoped reads skip `UserReadCache` (a removed member must disappear at once; the cache key has no
tenant). `GetUsersByIds` (the `users` DataLoader behind `Payment.user` and other cross-domain fields) stays
unscoped here: its callers read ids from rows that RLS scopes (B3), and B2-25 (edge) moves the loader into the
tenant's CLS context.

    `drizzle-users.repository.ts`: import `memberships`from`./identity.schema.js`; replace `list` with

```ts
  async list(criteria: ListUsersCriteria): Promise<CursorPage<UserRecord>> {
    const rows = await this.txHost.tx
      .select(publicColumns)
      .from(users)
      .innerJoin(
        memberships,
        and(eq(memberships.userId, users.id), eq(memberships.tenantId, criteria.tenantId)),
      )
      .where(and(searchFilter(criteria.search), keysetWhere(users.id, criteria.cursor)))
      .orderBy(keysetOrder(users.id))
      .limit(keysetFetchLimit(criteria.limit));
    return keysetPage(rows, criteria.limit);
  }

  async findByIdInTenant(id: string, tenantId: string): Promise<UserRecord | null> {
    const [row] = await this.txHost.tx
      .select(publicColumns)
      .from(users)
      .innerJoin(memberships, and(eq(memberships.userId, users.id), eq(memberships.tenantId, tenantId)))
      .where(eq(users.id, id))
      .limit(1);
    return row ?? null;
  }
```

from `./in-memory-tenancy.js`); add

```ts
  async findByIdInTenant(id: string, tenantId: string): Promise<UserRecord | null> {
    if (!this.store.memberships.has(membershipKey(tenantId, id))) return null;
    return this.findById(id);
  }
```

### Task 13: `@Roles` on the tenant-role hierarchy, `@StaffOnly`, F14 (B2-11 decorator, B2-21)

**Files:**

- Create: `libs/auth/src/decorators/staff-only.decorator.ts`, `libs/auth/src/guards/staff-only.guard.ts`
- Modify: `libs/auth/src/auth.constants.ts`, `libs/auth/src/decorators/roles.decorator.ts`,
  `libs/auth/src/guards/roles.guard.ts`, `libs/auth/src/index.ts`, `libs/auth/README.md`, `README.md`,
  `docs/SECURITY.md` (RBAC decorator table)
- Test: `libs/auth/src/guards/rbac.guards.spec.ts` (extended), `libs/auth/src/guards/staff-only.guard.spec.ts` (new),
  `libs/bootstrap/src/integration.smoke.spec.ts` (two `@Roles` routes through the global guard chain)

**Interfaces:** Consumes Task 1 (`satisfiesTenantRole`), Task 10 (`AuthUser.staff`, `roles: TenantRole[]`). Produces
I-4's Task 13 symbols: `Roles(...roles: TenantRole[])` (ANY listed role, owner ⊇ admin ⊇ member applied),
`STAFF_ONLY_KEY = 'auth:staff-only'`, `StaffOnly(): MethodDecorator & ClassDecorator`, `StaffOnlyGuard`,
`RbacErrorCode = { STAFF_ONLY: 'STAFF_ONLY' }` (403 `PermissionDeniedException`).

**Wave:** 2, Lane T, after Task 12. Merge `b2/tokens` after this task.

F14 resolution: `@Roles()` stays, now meaning tenant roles with the hierarchy (`@Roles('admin')` admits admins and
owners). It is the right tool only when the role itself is the requirement and no permission names it (spec §6:
"owner-only" operations such as deleting a tenant or transferring ownership, B2-12/B2-13); everything else uses
`@RequirePermissions`. `@StaffOnly()` admits only staff switch-in tokens (`req.user.staff === true`); its first
route is `POST /v1/staff/switch-out` (Task 16). Both are documented as such; README no longer advertises a role
matrix (Task 2 already dropped `@Roles()` from the README stack row). No B2 route uses `@Roles()`: the edge plan
guards `PATCH /v1/tenant` with `@RequirePermissions(IdentityPermission.TenantManage)` (spec §5.2), so this task
proves the decorator and the hierarchy with its own usages: the guard spec below and two `@Roles` routes of the
bootstrap smoke app, which run through the real global chain `JwtAuthGuard` → `RolesGuard` → `PermissionsGuard`.

```ts
describe('RolesGuard: tenant-role hierarchy (owner ⊇ admin ⊇ member)', () => {
  @Roles('member')
  class MembersOnly {
    read(): void {
      // any member of the tenant
    }
  }
  const read = MembersOnly.prototype.read;
  const at = (user: unknown) => ctx(read, user, 'http', MembersOnly);

  it('an owner satisfies @Roles("admin"); an admin and an owner satisfy @Roles("member")', () => {
    expect(rolesGuard.canActivate(ctx(list, owner))).toBe(true);
    expect(rolesGuard.canActivate(at(admin))).toBe(true);
    expect(rolesGuard.canActivate(at(owner))).toBe(true);
    expect(rolesGuard.canActivate(at(member))).toBe(true);

  it('no role at all satisfies nothing', () => {
    expect(() => rolesGuard.canActivate(at(makeAuthUser({ roles: [] })))).toThrow(
      PermissionDeniedException,
    );

});
```

`libs/auth/src/guards/staff-only.guard.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { Reflector } from '@nestjs/core';
import { PermissionDeniedException, UnauthenticatedException } from '@platform/common';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { STAFF_ONLY_KEY } from '../auth.constants.js';
import { StaffOnly } from '../decorators/staff-only.decorator.js';
import { makeAuthUser } from '../testing/auth-test.utils.js';
import { executionContext } from './execution-context.test.js';
import { StaffOnlyGuard } from './staff-only.guard.js';

class SupportController {
  @StaffOnly()
  leave(): void {
    // staff switch-in tokens only
  }
}

const guard = new StaffOnlyGuard();
const ctx = (user: unknown, type: 'http' | 'graphql' | 'ws' | 'rpc' = 'http') =>
  executionContext(
    type,
    { handler: SupportController.prototype.leave, cls: SupportController },
    type === 'ws' ? { data: { user } } : { headers: {}, user },
  );

describe('@StaffOnly / StaffOnlyGuard', () => {
  it('writes STAFF_ONLY_KEY metadata and attaches the guard', () => {
    const reflector = new Reflector();
    expect(reflector.get(STAFF_ONLY_KEY, SupportController.prototype.leave)).toBe(true);
    expect(Reflect.getMetadata('__guards__', SupportController.prototype.leave)).toContain(
      StaffOnlyGuard,
    );
  });

  it('admits a staff switch-in principal on HTTP, GraphQL and WebSocket', () => {
    const staff = makeAuthUser({ roles: ['admin'], staff: true });
    for (const type of ['http', 'graphql', 'ws'] as const) {
      expect(guard.canActivate(ctx(staff, type))).toBe(true);
    }
  });

  it('403 STAFF_ONLY for everyone else, owners included; 401 without a user', () => {
    for (const user of [makeAuthUser({ roles: ['owner'] }), makeAuthUser({ roles: ['admin'] })]) {
      try {
        guard.canActivate(ctx(user));
        throw new Error('expected a rejection');
      } catch (error) {
        expect(error).toBeInstanceOf(PermissionDeniedException);
        expect((error as PermissionDeniedException).code).toBe('STAFF_ONLY');
      }
    }
    expect(() => guard.canActivate(ctx(undefined))).toThrow(UnauthenticatedException);
  });

  it('rpc handlers pass (services trust the edge, like RolesGuard)', () => {
    expect(guard.canActivate(ctx(undefined, 'rpc'))).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

  `libs/auth/src/guards/staff-only.guard.ts`:

```ts
import { type CanActivate, type ExecutionContext, Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { getContextType, PermissionDeniedException } from '@platform/common';
import { RbacErrorCode } from '../auth.constants.js';
import { requireAuthUser } from './current-auth-user.js';

/**
 * `@StaffOnly()` enforcement: only a platform-staff switch-in token (`staff: true`, B2-11) passes.
 * An owner is not staff. rpc handlers pass (services trust the edge).
 */
@Injectable()
export class StaffOnlyGuard implements CanActivate {
  canActivate(context: ExecutionContext): boolean {
    if (getContextType(context) === 'rpc') return true;
    if (requireAuthUser(context).staff === true) return true;
    throw new PermissionDeniedException('Platform staff only', { code: RbacErrorCode.STAFF_ONLY });
  }
}
```

`libs/auth/src/decorators/staff-only.decorator.ts`:

```ts
import { applyDecorators, SetMetadata, UseGuards } from '@nestjs/common';
import { STAFF_ONLY_KEY } from '../auth.constants.js';
import { StaffOnlyGuard } from '../guards/staff-only.guard.js';

/**
 * Only a platform-staff switch-in token may call the route (403 `STAFF_ONLY`). Runs after the
 * global guards (authentication first). Class or method.
 */
export function StaffOnly(): MethodDecorator & ClassDecorator {
  return applyDecorators(SetMetadata(STAFF_ONLY_KEY, true), UseGuards(StaffOnlyGuard));
}
```

### Task 14: Switch tenant (REST, GraphQL, gRPC) (B2-10)

**Files:**

- Create: `domains/identity/src/application/commands/switch-tenant/{switch-tenant.command,switch-tenant.handler,switch-tenant.handler.spec}.ts`,

  `domains/identity/src/presentation/graphql/inputs/switch-tenant.input.ts`,
  `apps/monolith/test/tenancy-switch.e2e-spec.ts`, `apps/gateway/test/tenancy-switch.e2e-spec.ts`
- Modify: `identity.proto` + generated, `application/ports/auth.port.ts`, `application/mappers/identity-request.mapper.ts`,
  `infrastructure/adapters/{local/auth-local,grpc/auth-grpc}.adapter.ts`, `presentation/grpc/{auth-grpc.controller,identity-rpc.payloads}.ts`,
  `presentation/http/auth.controller.ts`, `presentation/graphql/auth.resolver.ts`, `domain/identity.errors.ts`,
  `identity.constants.ts`, `identity-core.module.ts`, `src/index.ts`, `testing/fake-identity-ports.ts`, `docs/API.md`
- Test: `identity-http.spec.ts`, `identity-graphql.spec.ts`, `identity-rpc.payloads.spec.ts`, `mappers.spec.ts`,
  `local-adapters.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:** Consumes Tasks 8 (`SessionTokensService.open`, `SessionsRepository.revokeForRotation`,
`TenantAccessDeniedException`), 10 (`AuthUser.staff`), 13. Produces (I-8, I-9, I-10):
`SwitchTenantCommand(userId, tenantId, refreshToken, accessToken: { jti: string; exp: number }, client?)` →
`AuthTokens`; `AuthPort.switchTenant(input: SwitchTenantRequest): Promise<AuthTokens>`; proto
`rpc SwitchTenant(SwitchTenantRequest) returns (AuthTokens)` with `SwitchTenantRequest { user_id = 1; tenant_id = 2;
refresh_token = 3; access_token_jti = 4; access_token_exp = 5; client = 6 }`;
`POST /v1/auth/switch-tenant { tenantId, refreshToken }` → 200 `AuthTokensResponse`; GraphQL
`switchTenant(input: SwitchTenantInput!): AuthPayload!`; `StaffTokenNotAllowedException` (403
`STAFF_TOKEN_NOT_ALLOWED`); `FakeAuthPort.switchTenant`.

**Wave:** 3, Lane F (`b2/flows`), after `b2/tokens` (Task 13) is merged; parallel with Lanes C and O.

Semantics (spec §5.2): the caller presents its access token (bearer) and its refresh token. The target membership is
checked first (403 `TENANT_ACCESS_DENIED` for a non-member, unknown or nil tenant; nothing is rotated, the tokens
keep working). Then, in one transaction, the refresh token's session is rotated into a new session in the target
tenant (same conditional `revokeForRotation` as refresh: a reused refresh token revokes every session, 401
`REFRESH_TOKEN_REUSED`), `last_active_at` is touched, and the old access token is denylisted (it names the old
tenant). A staff switch-in token cannot switch (403 `STAFF_TOKEN_NOT_ALLOWED`): checked at the REST/GraphQL edge from
`req.user.staff`; the `SwitchTenant` RPC trusts its caller (the gateway), like every identity RPC. Switching to the
current tenant is allowed (a rotation).

```ts
import type { AuthTokens, ClientInfo } from '@domain/identity-contracts';
import { Command } from '@nestjs/cqrs';

/**
 * Moves the caller's session to another tenant (spec §5.2): rotates `refreshToken`'s session into
 * `tenantId` and denylists the current access token (`accessToken.jti` until `accessToken.exp`).
 */
export class SwitchTenantCommand extends Command<AuthTokens> {
  constructor(
    readonly userId: string,
    readonly tenantId: string,
    readonly refreshToken: string,
    readonly accessToken: { readonly jti: string; readonly exp: number },
    readonly client?: ClientInfo | undefined,
  ) {
    super();
  }
}
```

`switch-tenant.handler.ts`:

```ts
import type { AuthTokens } from '@domain/identity-contracts';
import { Logger } from '@nestjs/common';
import { CommandHandler, type ICommandHandler } from '@nestjs/cqrs';
import { AccessTokenDenylist, AuthErrorCode, type RefreshTokenClaims } from '@platform/auth';
import {
  type DomainException,
  generateId,
  isPlatformTenantId,
  isUuid,
  sha256Hex,
  UnauthenticatedException,
} from '@platform/common';
import {
  InvalidRefreshTokenException,
  RefreshTokenReuseDetectedException,
  SessionExpiredException,
  TenantAccessDeniedException,
} from '../../../domain/identity.errors.js';
import { classifyRefreshRejection } from '../../../domain/session.js';
import { TokenIssuer } from '../../../infrastructure/security/token-issuer.service.js';
import { MembershipsRepository } from '../../persistence/memberships.repository.js';
import { SessionsRepository } from '../../persistence/sessions.repository.js';
import { TransactionRunner } from '../../persistence/transaction-runner.js';
import { UsersRepository } from '../../persistence/users.repository.js';
import { SessionTokensService } from '../../services/session-tokens.service.js';
import { SwitchTenantCommand } from './switch-tenant.command.js';

/**
 * Switch tenant = a refresh rotation that lands in another tenant:
 * 1. verify the refresh JWT; it must belong to the caller;
 * 2. the target membership must exist (else 403, nothing touched);
 * 3. denylist the current access token (it names the old tenant);
 * 4. in ONE transaction: conditionally revoke the presented session, touch the membership and open
 *    the successor session in the target tenant. A failed rotation is classified like refresh
 *    (reuse → every session revoked).
 */
@CommandHandler(SwitchTenantCommand)
export class SwitchTenantHandler implements ICommandHandler<SwitchTenantCommand> {
  private readonly logger = new Logger(SwitchTenantHandler.name);

  constructor(
    private readonly tokens: TokenIssuer,
    private readonly users: UsersRepository,
    private readonly sessions: SessionsRepository,
    private readonly memberships: MembershipsRepository,
    private readonly sessionTokens: SessionTokensService,
    private readonly transaction: TransactionRunner,
    private readonly denylist: AccessTokenDenylist,
  ) {}

  async execute(command: SwitchTenantCommand): Promise<AuthTokens> {
    const claims = await this.verify(command.refreshToken);
    if (claims.sub !== command.userId) throw new InvalidRefreshTokenException();

    const membership =
      isUuid(command.tenantId) && !isPlatformTenantId(command.tenantId)
        ? await this.memberships.find(command.tenantId, command.userId)
        : null;
    if (membership === null) throw new TenantAccessDeniedException();

    const user = await this.users.findById(command.userId);
    if (!user) throw new InvalidRefreshTokenException();

    await this.denylist.deny(command.accessToken.jti, command.accessToken.exp);

    const refreshTokenHash = sha256Hex(command.refreshToken);
    const now = new Date();
    const successorId = generateId();
    const tokens = await this.transaction.run(async () => {
      const rotated = await this.sessions.revokeForRotation({
        id: claims.jti,
        userId: claims.sub,
        refreshTokenHash,
        replacedById: successorId,
        now,
      });
      if (!rotated) return undefined;
      await this.memberships.touch(membership.tenantId, user.id, now);
      return this.sessionTokens.open({
        user,
        tenantId: membership.tenantId,
        roles: membership.roles,
        client: command.client,
        sessionId: successorId,
      });
    });
    if (tokens) return tokens;
    throw await this.rejection(claims, refreshTokenHash, now);
  }

  private async verify(token: string): Promise<RefreshTokenClaims> {
    try {
      return await this.tokens.verifyRefreshToken(token);
    } catch (error) {
      if (error instanceof UnauthenticatedException && error.code === AuthErrorCode.TOKEN_EXPIRED) {
        throw new SessionExpiredException({ cause: error });
      }
      throw new InvalidRefreshTokenException({ cause: error });
    }
  }

  private async rejection(
    claims: RefreshTokenClaims,
    refreshTokenHash: string,
    now: Date,
  ): Promise<DomainException> {
    const session = await this.sessions.findById(claims.sub, claims.jti);
    switch (classifyRefreshRejection(session, { userId: claims.sub, refreshTokenHash, now })) {
      case 'reused': {
        const revoked = await this.sessions.revokeAllForUser(claims.sub, now);
        this.logger.warn(
          { userId: claims.sub, sessionId: claims.jti, revokedSessions: revoked },
          'Refresh token reuse detected on switch-tenant: all sessions of the user revoked',
        );
        return new RefreshTokenReuseDetectedException();
      }
      case 'expired':
        return new SessionExpiredException();
      case 'invalid':
        return new InvalidRefreshTokenException();
    }
  }
}
```

```ts
describe('POST /v1/auth/switch-tenant', () => {
  const tenantId = '0190f3b2-0000-7000-8000-0000000000a2';
  const refreshToken = 'eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiJ9.eyJzdWIiOiJ1In0.c2ln';

      refreshToken,
      accessTokenJti: user.jti,
      accessTokenExp: String(user.exp),
      client: expect.objectContaining({ ip: expect.any(String) }),
    });
  });

  it.each([
    [{ tenantId: 'x', refreshToken }],
    [{ tenantId, refreshToken: 'not-a-jwt' }],
    [{ tenantId }],
  ])('400 for %j (the port is never called)', async (payload) => {
    const res = await app.inject({
      method: 'POST',
      url: '/v1/auth/switch-tenant',
      headers: bearer(user),
      payload,
    });
    expect(res.statusCode).toBe(400);
    expect(authPort.switchTenant).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('403 STAFF_TOKEN_NOT_ALLOWED for a staff switch-in token (Review Focus 5)', async () => {
    const staff = await principal(app, ['admin'], generateId(), TEST_TENANT_ID, true);
    const res = await app.inject({
      method: 'POST',
      url: '/v1/auth/switch-tenant',
      headers: bearer(staff),
      payload: { tenantId, refreshToken },
    });
    expect(res.statusCode).toBe(403);
    expect(res.json()).toMatchObject({ code: 'STAFF_TOKEN_NOT_ALLOWED' });
    expect(authPort.switchTenant).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Contract and edges.** `identity.proto`: in `service AuthService` add after `Logout`

```proto
  // Moves the caller's session to another tenant: rotates the refresh token into a session there and
  // denylists the current access token. Not a member → PERMISSION_DENIED / TENANT_ACCESS_DENIED.
  rpc SwitchTenant(SwitchTenantRequest) returns (AuthTokens);
```

and the message (after `LogoutRequest`):

```proto
message SwitchTenantRequest {
  string user_id = 1;
  string tenant_id = 2;
  string refresh_token = 3;
  string access_token_jti = 4;
  // Access token `exp` claim (epoch seconds); bounds the denylist entry TTL.
  int64 access_token_exp = 5;
  ClientInfo client = 6;
}
```

`auth.controller.ts`: import `SwitchTenantDto`, `StaffTokenNotAllowedException` and `ApiForbiddenResponse`; add

```ts
  @Post('switch-tenant')
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @ApiBearerAuth()
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: 'Switch the active tenant',
    description:
      'Rotates the refresh token into a session in `tenantId` and revokes the current access token. ' +
      'Returns the new token pair.',
  })
  @ApiOkResponse({ type: AuthTokensResponse })
  @ApiBadRequestResponse({ type: ProblemResponse })
  @ApiUnauthorizedResponse({ type: ProblemResponse, description: '`INVALID_REFRESH_TOKEN`, `SESSION_EXPIRED`, `REFRESH_TOKEN_REUSED`' })
  @ApiForbiddenResponse({ type: ProblemResponse, description: '`TENANT_ACCESS_DENIED`, `STAFF_TOKEN_NOT_ALLOWED`' })
  async switchTenant(
    @Body() body: SwitchTenantDto,
    @CurrentUser() user: AuthUser,
    @Req() request: RequestLike,
  ): Promise<AuthTokensResponse> {
    if (user.staff) throw new StaffTokenNotAllowedException();
    const tokens = await this.auth.switchTenant({
      userId: user.id,
      tenantId: body.tenantId,
      refreshToken: body.refreshToken,
      accessTokenJti: user.jti,
      accessTokenExp: String(user.exp),
      client: clientInfoOf(request),
    });
    return AuthTokensResponse.from(tokens);
  }
```

`fake-identity-ports.ts` `FakeAuthPort`: add `SwitchTenantRequest` to the contracts import,
`TenantAccessDeniedException` is already imported (Task 9), and

```ts
  async switchTenant(input: SwitchTenantRequest): Promise<AuthTokens> {
    this.state.calls.push('AuthService.SwitchTenant');
    const claims = await this.tokens.verifyRefreshToken(input.refreshToken).catch(() => {
      throw new InvalidRefreshTokenException();
    });
    const session = this.state.sessions.get(claims.jti);
    if (claims.sub !== input.userId || session?.userId !== input.userId) throw new InvalidRefreshTokenException();
    const membership = this.state.membership(input.tenantId, input.userId);
    if (!membership) throw new TenantAccessDeniedException();
    const entry = this.state.users.get(input.userId);
    if (!entry) throw new InvalidRefreshTokenException();
    await this.denylist.deny(input.accessTokenJti, Number(input.accessTokenExp));
    this.state.sessions.delete(claims.jti);
    membership.lastActiveAt = Date.now();
    return this.issue(entry.user, input.tenantId);
  }
```
