- [ ] **Step 9: Write the e2e specs (both topologies).** `apps/monolith/test/tenancy-switch.e2e-spec.ts`:

```ts
/*
 * Switch tenant through the monolith's real handlers over the in-memory identity store (spec §5.2).
 */
await vi.hoisted(async () => {
  const { MONOLITH_E2E_ENV } = await import('./support/e2e-env.js');
  Object.assign(process.env, MONOLITH_E2E_ENV);
});

import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { bearer, graphql } from './support/http.js';
import { createMonolithE2eApp, type MonolithE2e } from './support/monolith-e2e-app.js';

interface AuthTokensBody {
  accessToken: string;
  refreshToken: string;
  tenantId: string;
  tenantRoles: string[];
  user: { id: string };
}
const PASSWORD = 'correct horse battery staple';

describe('switch tenant (monolith)', () => {
  let e2e: MonolithE2e;
  const inject = (...args: Parameters<MonolithE2e['app']['inject']>) => e2e.app.inject(...args);
  const register = async (email: string): Promise<AuthTokensBody> => {
    const res = await inject({
      method: 'POST',
      url: '/v1/auth/register',
      payload: { email, password: PASSWORD, displayName: 'X' },
    });
    expect(res.statusCode, res.body).toBe(201);
    return res.json<AuthTokensBody>();
  };
  const join = (tenantId: string, userId: string, roles: ('owner' | 'admin' | 'member')[]) =>
    e2e.identity.store.memberships.set(`${tenantId}:${userId}`, {
      tenantId,
      userId,
      roles,
      lastActiveAt: null,
      createdAt: new Date(),
    });

  beforeAll(async () => {
    e2e = await createMonolithE2eApp();
  });

  afterAll(async () => {
    await e2e?.app.close();
  });

  it('REST: switches into a membership; the old access token is revoked, the old refresh token is spent', async () => {
    const owner = await register('owner.switch@example.com');
    const guest = await register('guest.switch@example.com');
    join(owner.tenantId, guest.user.id, ['admin']);

    const res = await inject({
      method: 'POST',
      url: '/v1/auth/switch-tenant',
      headers: bearer(guest.accessToken),
      payload: { tenantId: owner.tenantId, refreshToken: guest.refreshToken },
    });
    expect(res.statusCode, res.body).toBe(200);
    const switched = res.json<AuthTokensBody>();
    expect(switched).toMatchObject({ tenantId: owner.tenantId, tenantRoles: ['admin'] });

    const me = await inject({
      method: 'GET',
      url: '/v1/auth/me',
      headers: bearer(switched.accessToken),
    });
    expect(me.json()).toMatchObject({ tenantId: owner.tenantId, tenantRoles: ['admin'] });
    const old = await inject({
      method: 'GET',
      url: '/v1/auth/me',
      headers: bearer(guest.accessToken),
    });
    expect(old.statusCode).toBe(401);
    expect(old.json<{ code: string }>().code).toBe('TOKEN_REVOKED');

    const refreshed = await inject({
      method: 'POST',
      url: '/v1/auth/refresh',
      payload: { refreshToken: switched.refreshToken },
    });
    expect(refreshed.json<AuthTokensBody>().tenantId).toBe(owner.tenantId);

    // The pre-switch refresh token was rotated away: presenting it again is reuse (every session revoked).
    const reused = await inject({
      method: 'POST',
      url: '/v1/auth/refresh',
      payload: { refreshToken: guest.refreshToken },
    });
    expect(reused.statusCode).toBe(401);
    expect(reused.json<{ code: string }>().code).toBe('REFRESH_TOKEN_REUSED');
  });

  it.each([
    ['a tenant the caller is not a member of', 'foreign'],
    ['the platform tenant', '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000'],
    ['an unknown tenant', '0190f3b2-0000-7000-8000-00000000dead'],
  ])(
    'switch to %s → 403 TENANT_ACCESS_DENIED and both tokens keep working (Review Focus 1)',
    async (_name, which) => {
      const victim = await register(`victim.sw.${which.slice(0, 6)}@example.com`);
      const caller = await register(`caller.sw.${which.slice(0, 6)}@example.com`);
      const res = await inject({
        method: 'POST',
        url: '/v1/auth/switch-tenant',
        headers: bearer(caller.accessToken),
        payload: {
          tenantId: which === 'foreign' ? victim.tenantId : which,
          refreshToken: caller.refreshToken,
        },
      });
      expect(res.statusCode).toBe(403);
      expect(res.json<{ code: string }>().code).toBe('TENANT_ACCESS_DENIED');
      expect(
        (await inject({ method: 'GET', url: '/v1/auth/me', headers: bearer(caller.accessToken) }))
          .statusCode,
      ).toBe(200);
      const refreshed = await inject({
        method: 'POST',
        url: '/v1/auth/refresh',
        payload: { refreshToken: caller.refreshToken },
      });
      expect(refreshed.statusCode).toBe(200);
    },
  );

  it('GraphQL switchTenant returns the AuthPayload of the target tenant', async () => {
    const a = await register('a.gql.switch@example.com');
    const b = await register('b.gql.switch@example.com');
    join(b.tenantId, a.user.id, ['member']);
    const res = await inject(
      graphql(
        'mutation ($input: SwitchTenantInput!) { switchTenant(input: $input) { tenantId tenantRoles } }',
        { input: { tenantId: b.tenantId, refreshToken: a.refreshToken } },
        bearer(a.accessToken),
      ),
    );
    expect(res.json()).toEqual({
      data: { switchTenant: { tenantId: b.tenantId, tenantRoles: ['MEMBER'] } },
    });
  });
});
```

### Task 15: Staff switch-in, audited and short-lived (B2-11)

**Files:**

- Create: `domains/identity/src/application/commands/staff-switch-in/{staff-switch-in.command,staff-switch-in.handler,staff-switch-in.handler.spec}.ts`,
  `domains/identity/src/infrastructure/security/{staff-switch-in.config,staff-switch-in.config.spec}.ts`,
  `domains/identity/src/presentation/http/staff.controller.ts`,
  `domains/identity/src/presentation/http/dto/staff-switch-in.dto.ts`,
  `domains/identity/src/presentation/http/responses/staff-access-token.response.ts`,
  `domains/identity/src/presentation/http/staff-http.spec.ts`, `apps/monolith/test/staff-switch.e2e-spec.ts`,
  `apps/gateway/test/staff-switch.e2e-spec.ts`
- Modify: `identity.proto` + generated, `application/ports/auth.port.ts`, `application/mappers/identity-request.mapper.ts`,
  `infrastructure/adapters/{local/auth-local,grpc/auth-grpc}.adapter.ts`, `infrastructure/adapters/grpc/grpc-contract.normalizer.ts`,
  `presentation/grpc/{auth-grpc.controller,identity-rpc.payloads}.ts`, `domain/identity.errors.ts`,
  `identity.constants.ts`, `identity-core.module.ts`, `identity-api.module.ts`, `src/index.ts`,
  `testing/fake-identity-ports.ts`; root `.env.example`, `docs/CONFIGURATION.md`, `docs/API.md`, `docs/SECURITY.md`,
  `apps/{monolith,identity-service}/test/env-example.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:** Consumes Tasks 3 (`TenantsRepository.findById`), 6 (`TenantAuditLog`, `AUDIT_EVENT_TYPES.STAFF_SWITCH_IN`),
10 (`TokenIssuer.issueAccessToken(subject, { ttlSec })`), 11 (`UsersRepository.isPlatformStaff`). Produces (I-8, I-9, I-10): `StaffSwitchInCommand(staffUserId, tenantId, reason,
client?)` → `StaffAccessToken`; `AuthPort.staffSwitchIn(input: StaffSwitchInRequest): Promise<StaffAccessToken>`;
`staffSwitchInConfig` (`STAFF_SWITCH_IN_TTL_SEC`, int 60..3600, default 900) and `type StaffSwitchInConfig`;
`IDENTITY_LIMITS.SWITCH_IN_REASON_MAX_LENGTH = 1000`; `NotPlatformStaffException` (403 `STAFF_ONLY`); proto
`rpc StaffSwitchIn`, `StaffSwitchInRequest { staff_user_id = 1; tenant_id = 2; reason = 3; client = 4 }`,
`StaffAccessToken { access_token = 1; expires_in = 2; token_type = 3; tenant_id = 4; audit_event_id = 5 }`;
`POST /v1/staff/tenants/:tenantId/switch-in { reason }` → 201; `StaffController` in both
`IdentityApiModule.forLocal()` and `.forRemote()`; `FakeIdentityState.grantStaff()`, `.auditEvents`.

**Wave:** 3, Lane F, after Task 14.

Rules (spec §5.2, §6; Review Focus 5): the caller authenticates with its own normal token; the handler re-reads
`users.is_platform_staff` (a flag revoked after login is honoured at once) → else 403 `STAFF_ONLY`. The reason is trimmed, 1..1000
characters (400 otherwise). The tenant must exist: unknown ids and the nil UUID (never a tenant, CHECK
`tenants_id_not_platform`) are 404 `NOT_FOUND`; a malformed id is 400. The result is an access token only:
`roles: ['admin']`, `staff: true`, `tid` = the tenant, lifetime `STAFF_SWITCH_IN_TTL_SEC`; no refresh token, no
session row. An `audit_events` row `staff.switch_in` (in the target tenant: the tenant sees who entered) with
`{ reason, accessTokenJti, expiresAt, ip, userAgent }` is written before the token is returned, plus the
`IdentityAudit` log line.

```ts
import { RbacErrorCode } from '@platform/auth';
import {
  DomainValidationException,
  EntityNotFoundException,
  PLATFORM_TENANT_ID,
} from '@platform/common';
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { makeUserRecord } from '../../../../test/fixtures.js';
import { mockOf } from '../../../../test/mocks.js';
import { NotPlatformStaffException } from '../../../domain/identity.errors.js';
import type { TokenIssuer } from '../../../infrastructure/security/token-issuer.service.js';
import { AUDIT_EVENT_TYPES } from '../../persistence/audit-events.repository.js';
import type { TenantsRepository } from '../../persistence/tenants.repository.js';
import type { UsersRepository } from '../../persistence/users.repository.js';
import type { TenantAuditLog } from '../../services/tenant-audit-log.service.js';
import { StaffSwitchInCommand } from './staff-switch-in.command.js';
import { StaffSwitchInHandler } from './staff-switch-in.handler.js';

const TENANT = '0190f3b2-0000-7000-8000-0000000000aa';

describe('StaffSwitchInHandler', () => {
  const staff = makeUserRecord({ isPlatformStaff: true });
  const log: string[] = [];
  let users: ReturnType<typeof mockOf<UsersRepository>>;
  let tenants: ReturnType<typeof mockOf<TenantsRepository>>;
  let issuer: ReturnType<typeof mockOf<TokenIssuer>>;
  let audit: ReturnType<typeof mockOf<TenantAuditLog>>;
  let handler: StaffSwitchInHandler;

  beforeEach(() => {
    log.length = 0;
    users = mockOf<UsersRepository>({
      isPlatformStaff: async () => true,
      findById: async () => staff,
    });
    tenants = mockOf<TenantsRepository>({
      findById: async (id) =>
        id === TENANT
          ? { id: TENANT, slug: 'acme', name: 'Acme', personal: false, createdAt: new Date() }
          : null,
    });
    issuer = mockOf<TokenIssuer>({
      issueAccessToken: async () => {
        log.push('sign');
        return { token: 'staff-jwt', jti: 'staff-jti', expiresIn: 300, exp: 1_900_000_000 };
      },
    });
    audit = mockOf<TenantAuditLog>({
      record: async () => {
        log.push('audit');
        return 'audit-1';
      },
    });
    handler = new StaffSwitchInHandler(users, tenants, issuer, audit, { ttlSec: 300 });
  });

  it('issues an admin + staff access token in the tenant, for the configured TTL, and audits it', async () => {
    const result = await handler.execute(
      new StaffSwitchInCommand(staff.id, TENANT, '  ticket #42: billing bug  ', {
        ip: '10.0.0.7',
        userAgent: 'curl',
      }),
    );
    expect(result).toEqual({
      accessToken: 'staff-jwt',
      expiresIn: 300,
      tokenType: 'Bearer',
      tenantId: TENANT,
      auditEventId: 'audit-1',
    });
    expect(issuer.issueAccessToken).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      { id: staff.id, email: staff.email, tenantId: TENANT, roles: ['admin'], staff: true },
      { ttlSec: 300 },
    );
    expect(audit.record).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
      tenantId: TENANT,
      actorId: staff.id,
      type: AUDIT_EVENT_TYPES.STAFF_SWITCH_IN,
      data: {
        reason: 'ticket #42: billing bug',
        accessTokenJti: 'staff-jti',
        expiresAt: new Date(1_900_000_000_000).toISOString(),
        ip: '10.0.0.7',
        userAgent: 'curl',
      },
    });
    expect(log).toEqual(['sign', 'audit']);
  });

  it('403 STAFF_ONLY when the flag is not (or no longer) set: a fresh read, never the token', async () => {
    users.isPlatformStaff.mockResolvedValue(false);
    const error = await handler
      .execute(new StaffSwitchInCommand(staff.id, TENANT, 'x'))
      .catch((e: unknown) => e);
    expect(error).toBeInstanceOf(NotPlatformStaffException);
    expect((error as NotPlatformStaffException).code).toBe(RbacErrorCode.STAFF_ONLY);
    expect(log).toEqual([]);
  });

  it.each([
    ['an unknown tenant', '0190f3b2-0000-7000-8000-00000000dead'],
    ['the platform tenant (nil UUID)', PLATFORM_TENANT_ID],
  ])('404 for %s, nothing signed or audited', async (_name, tenantId) => {
    await expect(
      handler.execute(new StaffSwitchInCommand(staff.id, tenantId, 'x')),
    ).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(EntityNotFoundException);
    expect(log).toEqual([]);
  });

  it.each([['   '], ['x'.repeat(1_001)]])(
    'rejects the reason %j (1..1000 characters after trimming)',
    async (reason) => {
      await expect(
        handler.execute(new StaffSwitchInCommand(staff.id, TENANT, reason)),
      ).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(DomainValidationException);
      expect(log).toEqual([]);
    },
  );
});
```

```ts
import type { ConfigType } from '@nestjs/config';
import { defineConfigNamespace, zInt } from '@platform/config';
import { z } from 'zod';

/** Lifetime of a staff switch-in access token (spec §5.2). Loaded by IdentityCoreModule only. */
export const staffSwitchInEnvSchema = z
  .object({
    STAFF_SWITCH_IN_TTL_SEC: zInt(900, { min: 60, max: 3_600 }),
  })
  .transform((env) => ({ ttlSec: env.STAFF_SWITCH_IN_TTL_SEC }));

export const staffSwitchInConfig = defineConfigNamespace('staffSwitchIn', staffSwitchInEnvSchema);
export type StaffSwitchInConfig = ConfigType<typeof staffSwitchInConfig>;
```

```ts
/** 403 — switch-in by a user whose `is_platform_staff` flag is not set (read fresh, never from the token). */
export class NotPlatformStaffException extends PermissionDeniedException {
  constructor() {
    super('Platform staff only', { code: IdentityErrorCode.STAFF_ONLY });
  }
}
```

`staff-switch-in.handler.ts`:

```ts
import type { StaffAccessToken } from '@domain/identity-contracts';
import { Inject } from '@nestjs/common';
import { CommandHandler, type ICommandHandler } from '@nestjs/cqrs';
import {
  DomainValidationException,
  EntityNotFoundException,
  isPlatformTenantId,
  isUuid,
} from '@platform/common';
import { NotPlatformStaffException } from '../../../domain/identity.errors.js';
import { BEARER_TOKEN_TYPE, IDENTITY_LIMITS } from '../../../identity.constants.js';
import {
  type StaffSwitchInConfig,
  staffSwitchInConfig,
} from '../../../infrastructure/security/staff-switch-in.config.js';
import { TokenIssuer } from '../../../infrastructure/security/token-issuer.service.js';
import { toSessionClient } from '../../mappers/client-info.mapper.js';
import { AUDIT_EVENT_TYPES } from '../../persistence/audit-events.repository.js';
import { TenantsRepository } from '../../persistence/tenants.repository.js';
import { UsersRepository } from '../../persistence/users.repository.js';
import { TenantAuditLog } from '../../services/tenant-audit-log.service.js';
import { StaffSwitchInCommand } from './staff-switch-in.command.js';

/**
 * Staff switch-in: fresh staff check → reason → tenant exists → sign `{ roles: ['admin'], staff: true }`
 * for `STAFF_SWITCH_IN_TTL_SEC` → `staff.switch_in` audit row in the tenant. No refresh token, no
 * session: the token simply expires (or `switch-out` denylists it).
 */
@CommandHandler(StaffSwitchInCommand)
export class StaffSwitchInHandler implements ICommandHandler<StaffSwitchInCommand> {
  constructor(
    private readonly users: UsersRepository,
    private readonly tenants: TenantsRepository,
    private readonly tokens: TokenIssuer,
    private readonly audit: TenantAuditLog,
    @Inject(staffSwitchInConfig.KEY) private readonly config: StaffSwitchInConfig,
  ) {}

  async execute(command: StaffSwitchInCommand): Promise<StaffAccessToken> {
    if (!(await this.users.isPlatformStaff(command.staffUserId)))
      throw new NotPlatformStaffException();

    const reason = command.reason.trim();
    if (reason.length === 0 || reason.length > IDENTITY_LIMITS.SWITCH_IN_REASON_MAX_LENGTH) {
      throw new DomainValidationException('Invalid switch-in reason', {
        issues: [
          {
            path: 'reason',
            message: `Must be 1-${IDENTITY_LIMITS.SWITCH_IN_REASON_MAX_LENGTH} characters`,
          },
        ],
      });
    }

    const tenant =
      isUuid(command.tenantId) && !isPlatformTenantId(command.tenantId)
        ? await this.tenants.findById(command.tenantId)
        : null;
    if (tenant === null) throw new EntityNotFoundException('Tenant', command.tenantId);

    const staff = await this.users.findById(command.staffUserId);
    if (staff === null) throw new NotPlatformStaffException();

    const access = await this.tokens.issueAccessToken(
      { id: staff.id, email: staff.email, tenantId: tenant.id, roles: ['admin'], staff: true },
      { ttlSec: this.config.ttlSec },
    );
    const client = toSessionClient(command.client);
    const auditEventId = await this.audit.record({
      tenantId: tenant.id,
      actorId: staff.id,
      type: AUDIT_EVENT_TYPES.STAFF_SWITCH_IN,
      data: {
        reason,
        accessTokenJti: access.jti,
        expiresAt: new Date(access.exp * 1_000).toISOString(),
        ip: client.ip,
        userAgent: client.userAgent,
      },
    });
    return {
      accessToken: access.token,
      expiresIn: access.expiresIn,
      tokenType: BEARER_TOKEN_TYPE,
      tenantId: tenant.id,
      auditEventId,
    };
  }
}
```

(`toSessionClient` returns `{ userAgent: string | null; ip: string | null }`, so the spec's `ip`/`userAgent`
values pass through trimmed.) `identity-core.module.ts`: `imports` gains
`ConfigModule.forFeature(staffSwitchInConfig),`; `IDENTITY_COMMAND_HANDLERS` gains `StaffSwitchInHandler`.
`src/index.ts` (root): `StaffSwitchInCommand`, `NotPlatformStaffException` (errors export),
`export { type StaffSwitchInConfig, staffSwitchInConfig } from './infrastructure/security/staff-switch-in.config.js';`.

- [ ] **Step 5: Contract.** `identity.proto`: `service AuthService` gains

```proto
  // Platform staff enters a tenant for support: an access token only (admin + staff, short TTL), audited
  // in the tenant. Not staff → PERMISSION_DENIED / STAFF_ONLY; unknown tenant → NOT_FOUND.
  rpc StaffSwitchIn(StaffSwitchInRequest) returns (StaffAccessToken);
```

and the messages

```proto
message StaffSwitchInRequest {
  string staff_user_id = 1;
  string tenant_id = 2;
  // Why staff enters (audited), 1..1000 characters.
  string reason = 3;
  ClientInfo client = 4;
}

message StaffAccessToken {
  string access_token = 1;
  // Lifetime in seconds (STAFF_SWITCH_IN_TTL_SEC).
  int32 expires_in = 2;
  // Always "Bearer".
  string token_type = 3;
  string tenant_id = 4;
  // The identity.audit_events row recording the switch-in.
  string audit_event_id = 5;
}
```

`bun run proto:gen`. `grpc-contract.normalizer.ts`: add

```ts
/** proto-loader leaves absent scalars undefined on the client side: fill the contract defaults. */
export function normalizeStaffAccessToken(token: Partial<StaffAccessToken>): StaffAccessToken {
  return {
    accessToken: token.accessToken ?? '',
    expiresIn: token.expiresIn ?? 0,
    tokenType: token.tokenType ?? 'Bearer',
    tenantId: token.tenantId ?? '',
    auditEventId: token.auditEventId ?? '',
  };
}
```

```ts
}) satisfies z.ZodType<StaffSwitchInRequest>;
```

```ts
import { Test } from '@nestjs/testing';
import { TEST_TENANT_ID } from '@platform/auth/testing';
import { EntityNotFoundException, generateId, provideCommonEnhancers } from '@platform/common';
import { createFastifyTestApp, createMock } from '@platform/testing';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
  AUTH_TEST_IMPORTS,
  bearer,
  principal,
  type TestPrincipal,
} from '../../../test/auth-test.module.js';
import { AuthPort } from '../../application/ports/auth.port.js';
import { NotPlatformStaffException } from '../../domain/identity.errors.js';
import { StaffController } from './staff.controller.js';

type App = Awaited<ReturnType<typeof createFastifyTestApp>>;
const authPort = createMock<AuthPort>();
const TENANT = '0190f3b2-0000-7000-8000-0000000000aa';
const url = (tenantId: string) => `/v1/staff/tenants/${tenantId}/switch-in`;

describe('staff REST API (Fastify, real JWT guards, fake port)', () => {
  let app: App;
  let caller: TestPrincipal;
  let switchedIn: TestPrincipal;

  beforeAll(async () => {
    const builder = Test.createTestingModule({
      imports: AUTH_TEST_IMPORTS,
      controllers: [StaffController],
      providers: [{ provide: AuthPort, useValue: authPort }, ...provideCommonEnhancers()],
    });
    app = await createFastifyTestApp(builder, undefined, { appOptions: { logger: false } });
    caller = await principal(app, ['member']);
    switchedIn = await principal(app, ['admin'], generateId(), TENANT, true);
  });

  afterAll(async () => {
    await app.close();
  });

  beforeEach(() => {
    authPort.staffSwitchIn.mockReset();
  });

  it('201: caller, tenant, trimmed reason and client reach the port; the body is the staff token', async () => {
    authPort.staffSwitchIn.mockResolvedValue({
      accessToken: 'staff-jwt',
      expiresIn: 900,
      tokenType: 'Bearer',
      tenantId: TENANT,
      auditEventId: 'audit-1',
    });
    const res = await app.inject({
      method: 'POST',
      url: url(TENANT),
      headers: bearer(caller),
      payload: { reason: '  ticket #42  ' },
    });
    expect(res.statusCode, res.body).toBe(201);
    expect(res.json()).toEqual({
      accessToken: 'staff-jwt',
      expiresIn: 900,
      tokenType: 'Bearer',
      tenantId: TENANT,
      auditEventId: 'audit-1',
    });
    expect(res.json()).not.toHaveProperty('refreshToken');
    expect(authPort.staffSwitchIn).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
      staffUserId: caller.id,
      tenantId: TENANT,
      reason: 'ticket #42',
      client: expect.objectContaining({ ip: expect.any(String) }),
    });
  });

  it.each([
    ['a blank reason', TENANT, { reason: '   ' }],
    ['a 1 001-character reason', TENANT, { reason: 'x'.repeat(1_001) }],
    ['no reason', TENANT, {}],
    ['a malformed tenant id', 'not-a-uuid', { reason: 'r' }],
  ])('400 for %s (Review Focus 5), the port is never called', async (_name, tenantId, payload) => {
    const res = await app.inject({
      method: 'POST',
      url: url(tenantId),
      headers: bearer(caller),
      payload,
    });
    expect(res.statusCode).toBe(400);
    expect(authPort.staffSwitchIn).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('403 STAFF_ONLY (not staff, or flag revoked since login) and 404 (unknown or nil tenant) from the port', async () => {
    authPort.staffSwitchIn.mockRejectedValueOnce(new NotPlatformStaffException());
    const denied = await app.inject({
      method: 'POST',
      url: url(TENANT),
      headers: bearer(caller),
      payload: { reason: 'r' },
    });
    expect(denied.statusCode).toBe(403);
    expect(denied.json()).toMatchObject({ code: 'STAFF_ONLY' });

    authPort.staffSwitchIn.mockRejectedValueOnce(
      new EntityNotFoundException('Tenant', TEST_TENANT_ID),
    );
    const missing = await app.inject({
      method: 'POST',
      url: url('00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000'),
      headers: bearer(caller),
      payload: { reason: 'r' },
    });
    expect(missing.statusCode).toBe(404);
  });

  it('401 anonymous', async () => {
    const anonymous = await app.inject({
      method: 'POST',
      url: url(TENANT),
      payload: { reason: 'r' },
    });
    expect(anonymous.statusCode).toBe(401);
    expect(authPort.staffSwitchIn).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});
```

```ts
import { ApiProperty } from '@nestjs/swagger';
import { IsNotEmpty, IsString, MaxLength } from 'class-validator';
import { IDENTITY_LIMITS } from '../../../identity.constants.js';
import { TrimString } from '../../shared/transforms.js';

export class StaffSwitchInDto {
  @ApiProperty({
    description: 'Why you enter this tenant (audited, visible to the tenant).',
    maxLength: IDENTITY_LIMITS.SWITCH_IN_REASON_MAX_LENGTH,
    example: 'Support ticket #42: invoice totals are wrong',
  })
  @TrimString()
  @IsString()
  @IsNotEmpty()
  @MaxLength(IDENTITY_LIMITS.SWITCH_IN_REASON_MAX_LENGTH)

}
```

`presentation/http/responses/staff-access-token.response.ts`:

```ts
import type { StaffAccessToken } from '@domain/identity-contracts';
import { ApiProperty } from '@nestjs/swagger';
import { Exclude, Expose } from 'class-transformer';

/** A staff switch-in token: access only, no refresh token. */
@Exclude()
export class StaffAccessTokenResponse {
  @Expose()
  @ApiProperty({ description: 'Access JWT: roles ["admin"], staff: true, tid = tenantId.' })
  accessToken: string;

  @Expose()
  @ApiProperty({ example: 900, description: 'Seconds (STAFF_SWITCH_IN_TTL_SEC).' })
  expiresIn: number;

  @Expose()
  @ApiProperty({ example: 'Bearer' })
  tokenType: string;

  @Expose()
  @ApiProperty({ format: 'uuid' })
  tenantId: string;

  @Expose()
  @ApiProperty({ format: 'uuid', description: 'The audit_events row recording this switch-in.' })
  auditEventId: string;

  static from(token: StaffAccessToken): StaffAccessTokenResponse {
    return Object.assign(new StaffAccessTokenResponse(), token);
  }
}
```

`presentation/http/staff.controller.ts`:

```ts
import {
  Body,
  ClassSerializerInterceptor,
  Controller,
  HttpCode,
  HttpStatus,
  Param,
  ParseUUIDPipe,
  Post,
  Req,
  UseInterceptors,
} from '@nestjs/common';
import {
  ApiBadRequestResponse,
  ApiBearerAuth,
  ApiCreatedResponse,
  ApiForbiddenResponse,
  ApiNotFoundResponse,
  ApiOperation,
  ApiParam,
  ApiTags,
  ApiUnauthorizedResponse,
} from '@nestjs/swagger';
import { type AuthUser, CurrentUser } from '@platform/auth';
import type { RequestLike } from '@platform/common';
import { AuthPort } from '../../application/ports/auth.port.js';
import { clientInfoOf } from '../shared/client-info.util.js';
import { StaffSwitchInDto } from './dto/staff-switch-in.dto.js';
import { ProblemResponse } from './responses/problem.response.js';
import { StaffAccessTokenResponse } from './responses/staff-access-token.response.js';

/** Platform-staff support access (spec §5.2): switch-in here, switch-out in Task 16. */
@ApiTags('staff')
@ApiBearerAuth()
@ApiUnauthorizedResponse({ type: ProblemResponse })
@Controller({ path: 'staff', version: '1' })
@UseInterceptors(ClassSerializerInterceptor)
export class StaffController {
  constructor(private readonly auth: AuthPort) {}

  @Post('tenants/:tenantId/switch-in')
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.CREATED)
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: 'Enter a tenant as platform staff',
    description:
      'Needs `users.is_platform_staff` (re-read on every call). Returns a short-lived access token ' +
      '(admin + staff, `STAFF_SWITCH_IN_TTL_SEC`), no refresh token. Audited in the tenant.',
  })
  @ApiParam({ name: 'tenantId', format: 'uuid' })
  @ApiCreatedResponse({ type: StaffAccessTokenResponse })
  @ApiBadRequestResponse({ type: ProblemResponse })
  @ApiForbiddenResponse({ type: ProblemResponse, description: '`STAFF_ONLY`' })
  @ApiNotFoundResponse({ type: ProblemResponse })
  async switchIn(
    @Param('tenantId', new ParseUUIDPipe()) tenantId: string,
    @Body() body: StaffSwitchInDto,
    @CurrentUser() user: AuthUser,
    @Req() request: RequestLike,
  ): Promise<StaffAccessTokenResponse> {
    const token = await this.auth.staffSwitchIn({
      staffUserId: user.id,
      tenantId,
      reason: body.reason,
      client: clientInfoOf(request),
    });
    return StaffAccessTokenResponse.from(token);
  }
}
```

`identity-api.module.ts`: `const CONTROLLERS = [AuthController, UsersController, StaffController];` (both
`forLocal()` and `forRemote()` use `CONTROLLERS`). `fake-identity-ports.ts`: `FakeIdentityState` gains

```ts
  /** Users holding is_platform_staff. */
  readonly staff = new Set<string>();
  readonly auditEvents: { id: string; tenantId: string; actorId: string | null; type: string; data: Record<string, unknown> }[] = [];

  grantStaff(userId: string): void {
    this.staff.add(userId);
  }
```

and `FakeAuthPort` gains (imports: `StaffAccessToken`, `StaffSwitchInRequest`, `NotPlatformStaffException`,
`AUDIT_EVENT_TYPES`, `EntityNotFoundException` already imported)

```ts
  async staffSwitchIn(input: StaffSwitchInRequest): Promise<StaffAccessToken> {
    this.state.calls.push('AuthService.StaffSwitchIn');
    if (!this.state.staff.has(input.staffUserId)) throw new NotPlatformStaffException();
    if (!this.state.tenants.has(input.tenantId)) throw new EntityNotFoundException('Tenant', input.tenantId);
    const entry = this.state.users.get(input.staffUserId);
    if (!entry) throw new NotPlatformStaffException();
    const access = await this.tokens.issueAccessToken(
      { id: entry.user.id, email: entry.user.email, tenantId: input.tenantId, roles: ['admin'], staff: true },
      { ttlSec: 900 },
    );
    const auditEventId = generateId();
    this.state.auditEvents.push({
      id: auditEventId,
      tenantId: input.tenantId,
      actorId: input.staffUserId,
      type: AUDIT_EVENT_TYPES.STAFF_SWITCH_IN,
      data: { reason: input.reason.trim(), accessTokenJti: access.jti },
    });
    return { accessToken: access.token, expiresIn: access.expiresIn, tokenType: 'Bearer', tenantId: input.tenantId, auditEventId };
  }
```

```markdown
### `staffSwitchIn` — staff switch-in tokens (identity)

[`staff-switch-in.config.ts`](../domains/identity/src/infrastructure/security/staff-switch-in.config.ts) ·
monolith, identity-service (loaded by `IdentityCoreModule`)

| Variable                  | Default | Description                                                                                     |

| `STAFF_SWITCH_IN_TTL_SEC` | `900`   | Lifetime of a staff switch-in access token, 60..3600 s. No refresh token is ever issued for it. |
```

In the same file, the "Which app loads which namespace" table gains, after the `refreshToken` row,
``| `staffSwitchIn` | ✓ | | ✓ | | | `IdentityCoreModule` (`@domain/identity`) |`` (Prettier realigns it), and the
intro's "identity's `password` and `refreshToken`" becomes "identity's `password`, `refreshToken` and
`staffSwitchIn`" (conventions §2.10: every new namespace gets a row).
`docs/API.md`: REST row `| \`POST\` | \`/v1/staff/tenants/{tenantId}/switch-in\` | Bearer (platform staff) | class-validator | 201 | Enter a tenant for support (\`{ reason }\`, 1..1000 chars). Returns \`{ accessToken, expiresIn, tokenType, tenantId, auditEventId }\` (admin + staff, no refresh token). 403 \`STAFF_ONLY\`; 404 unknown tenant |`;
  error-code row `| \`STAFF_ONLY\` | 403 | auth / identity (a \`@StaffOnly()\` route without a switch-in token; switch-in without the staff flag) |`.
  `docs/SECURITY.md`: new subsection under "Authorisation (RBAC)":

```markdown
### Platform staff switch-in

Staff are users with `identity.users.is_platform_staff` (granted only by `staff:grant`, see "The first admin").
`POST /v1/staff/tenants/{tenantId}/switch-in { reason }` re-reads the flag, then issues an access token for that
tenant with `roles: ["admin"]`, `staff: true` and a lifetime of `STAFF_SWITCH_IN_TTL_SEC` (default 900 s). There is
no refresh token and no session. Every switch-in writes a `staff.switch_in` row to the tenant's `audit_events`
(reason, token id, expiry, client) and an `IdentityAudit` log line. Staff never get `tenant:manage` (an owner
grant). A switch-in token cannot switch tenants (`STAFF_TOKEN_NOT_ALLOWED`); the flag is re-read on every
switch-in, so a revocation stops new switch-ins at once.
```

      `apps/monolith/test/staff-switch.e2e-spec.ts`:

```ts
/*
 * Platform-staff switch-in through the monolith's real handlers over the in-memory identity store
 * (spec §5.2, §13: staff switch-in and its audit row).
 */
await vi.hoisted(async () => {
  const { MONOLITH_E2E_ENV } = await import('./support/e2e-env.js');
  Object.assign(process.env, MONOLITH_E2E_ENV);
});

import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { bearer } from './support/http.js';
import { createMonolithE2eApp, type MonolithE2e } from './support/monolith-e2e-app.js';

interface AuthTokensBody {
  accessToken: string;
  refreshToken: string;
  tenantId: string;
  user: { id: string };
}
const PASSWORD = 'correct horse battery staple';

describe('staff switch-in (monolith)', () => {
  let e2e: MonolithE2e;
  const inject = (...args: Parameters<MonolithE2e['app']['inject']>) => e2e.app.inject(...args);
  const register = async (email: string): Promise<AuthTokensBody> => {
    const res = await inject({
      method: 'POST',
      url: '/v1/auth/register',
      payload: { email, password: PASSWORD, displayName: 'X' },
    });
    expect(res.statusCode, res.body).toBe(201);
    return res.json<AuthTokensBody>();
  };
  const setStaff = (userId: string, isPlatformStaff: boolean) => {
    const row = e2e.identity.store.users.get(userId);
    if (row === undefined) throw new Error(`user ${userId} missing`);
    e2e.identity.store.users.set(userId, { ...row, isPlatformStaff });
  };

  beforeAll(async () => {
    e2e = await createMonolithE2eApp();
  });

  afterAll(async () => {
    await e2e?.app.close();
  });

  it('a staff user enters a tenant: admin + staff token, no refresh token, a staff.switch_in audit row', async () => {
    const owner = await register('owner.staff@example.com');
    const support = await register('support.staff@example.com');
    setStaff(support.user.id, true);

    const res = await inject({
      method: 'POST',
      url: `/v1/staff/tenants/${owner.tenantId}/switch-in`,
      headers: bearer(support.accessToken),
      payload: { reason: 'ticket #42' },
    });
    expect(res.statusCode, res.body).toBe(201);
    const body = res.json<{
      accessToken: string;
      expiresIn: number;
      tenantId: string;
      auditEventId: string;
    }>();
    expect(body).toMatchObject({ tenantId: owner.tenantId, expiresIn: 900 });
    expect(body).not.toHaveProperty('refreshToken');

    const me = await inject({
      method: 'GET',
      url: '/v1/auth/me',
      headers: bearer(body.accessToken),
    });
    expect(me.json()).toMatchObject({
      id: support.user.id,
      tenantId: owner.tenantId,
      tenantRoles: ['admin'],
      staff: true,
    });
    expect(e2e.identity.store.auditEvents).toContainEqual(
      expect.objectContaining({
        id: body.auditEventId,
        tenantId: owner.tenantId,
        actorId: support.user.id,
        type: 'staff.switch_in',
        data: expect.objectContaining({ reason: 'ticket #42' }),
      }),
    );

    // A switch-in token is no refresh token (typ: access, other secret) (Review Focus 5).
    const asRefresh = await inject({
      method: 'POST',
      url: '/v1/auth/refresh',
      payload: { refreshToken: body.accessToken },
    });
    expect(asRefresh.statusCode).toBe(401);
    expect(asRefresh.json<{ code: string }>().code).toBe('INVALID_REFRESH_TOKEN');

    // A switch-in token cannot switch tenant (Review Focus 5).
    const sw = await inject({
      method: 'POST',
      url: '/v1/auth/switch-tenant',
      headers: bearer(body.accessToken),
      payload: { tenantId: support.tenantId, refreshToken: support.refreshToken },
    });
    expect(sw.statusCode).toBe(403);
    expect(sw.json<{ code: string }>().code).toBe('STAFF_TOKEN_NOT_ALLOWED');
  });

  it('403 STAFF_ONLY for a non-staff user and for a flag revoked after login; 404 for the nil tenant', async () => {
    const victim = await register('victim.staff@example.com');
    const nobody = await register('nobody.staff@example.com');
    const denied = await inject({
      method: 'POST',
      url: `/v1/staff/tenants/${victim.tenantId}/switch-in`,
      headers: bearer(nobody.accessToken),
      payload: { reason: 'r' },
    });
    expect(denied.statusCode).toBe(403);
    expect(denied.json<{ code: string }>().code).toBe('STAFF_ONLY');

    const exStaff = await register('exstaff.staff@example.com');
    const switchIn = () =>
      inject({
        method: 'POST',
        url: `/v1/staff/tenants/${victim.tenantId}/switch-in`,
        headers: bearer(exStaff.accessToken),
        payload: { reason: 'r' },
      });
    setStaff(exStaff.user.id, true);
    expect((await switchIn()).statusCode).toBe(201);
    setStaff(exStaff.user.id, false); // revoked; the same login token is still valid
    const revoked = await switchIn();
    expect(revoked.statusCode).toBe(403);
    expect(revoked.json<{ code: string }>().code).toBe('STAFF_ONLY');

    setStaff(exStaff.user.id, true);
    const nil = await inject({
      method: 'POST',
      url: '/v1/staff/tenants/00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000/switch-in',
      headers: bearer(exStaff.accessToken),
      payload: { reason: 'r' },
    });
    expect(nil.statusCode).toBe(404);
  });
});
```

**Interfaces:** Consumes Tasks 6, 13 (`@StaffOnly()`), 15. Produces (I-8, I-9): `StaffSwitchOutCommand(staffUserId,
tenantId, accessToken: { jti: string; exp: number })` → `void`; `AuthPort.staffSwitchOut(input:
StaffSwitchOutRequest): Promise<void>`; proto `rpc StaffSwitchOut(StaffSwitchOutRequest) returns
(google.protobuf.Empty)`, `StaffSwitchOutRequest { staff_user_id = 1; tenant_id = 2; access_token_jti = 3;
access_token_exp = 4 }`; `POST /v1/staff/switch-out` → 204 (`@StaffOnly()`); `FakeAuthPort.staffSwitchOut`.

**Wave:** 3, Lane F, after Task 15. Last task of Lane F: merge `b2/flows` after it.

Rules: only a switch-in token may call it (403 `STAFF_ONLY` for a normal token, Review Focus 5); it denylists the
switch-in token (`jti` until `exp`) and writes `staff.switch_out` (`{ accessTokenJti }`) into the tenant's
`audit_events`. The staff user then continues with their own normal token. Idempotent for the denylist; each call
records one row (a second call with the same, now denylisted token is already rejected by `JwtAuthGuard` with 401
`TOKEN_REVOKED`).

```ts
import type { AccessTokenDenylist } from '@platform/auth';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { mockOf } from '../../../../test/mocks.js';
import { AUDIT_EVENT_TYPES } from '../../persistence/audit-events.repository.js';
import type { TenantAuditLog } from '../../services/tenant-audit-log.service.js';
import { StaffSwitchOutCommand } from './staff-switch-out.command.js';
import { StaffSwitchOutHandler } from './staff-switch-out.handler.js';

describe('StaffSwitchOutHandler', () => {
  it('denylists the switch-in token, then records staff.switch_out in the tenant', async () => {
    const log: string[] = [];
    const denylist = mockOf<AccessTokenDenylist>({
      deny: async () => {
        log.push('deny');
      },
    });
    const audit = mockOf<TenantAuditLog>({
      record: async () => {
        log.push('audit');
        return 'audit-2';
      },
    });
    const handler = new StaffSwitchOutHandler(denylist, audit);
    const tenantId = '0190f3b2-0000-7000-8000-0000000000aa';
    const staffUserId = '0190f3b2-0000-7000-8000-000000000001';

    await handler.execute(
      new StaffSwitchOutCommand(staffUserId, tenantId, { jti: 'staff-jti', exp: 1_900_000_000 }),
    );

    expect(denylist.deny).toHaveBeenCalledWith('staff-jti', 1_900_000_000);
    expect(audit.record).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
      tenantId,
      actorId: staffUserId,
      type: AUDIT_EVENT_TYPES.STAFF_SWITCH_OUT,
      data: { accessTokenJti: 'staff-jti' },
    });
    expect(log).toEqual(['deny', 'audit']);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { Command } from '@nestjs/cqrs';

/** Platform staff leaves `tenantId`: the switch-in token is revoked and the exit audited. */
export class StaffSwitchOutCommand extends Command<void> {
  constructor(
    readonly staffUserId: string,
    readonly tenantId: string,
    readonly accessToken: { readonly jti: string; readonly exp: number },
  ) {
    super();
  }
}
```

`staff-switch-out.handler.ts`:

```ts
import { CommandHandler, type ICommandHandler } from '@nestjs/cqrs';
import { AccessTokenDenylist } from '@platform/auth';
import { AUDIT_EVENT_TYPES } from '../../persistence/audit-events.repository.js';
import { TenantAuditLog } from '../../services/tenant-audit-log.service.js';
import { StaffSwitchOutCommand } from './staff-switch-out.command.js';

/**
 * Revokes a staff switch-in token before its expiry (Redis denylist, like logout) and writes the
 * `staff.switch_out` row next to the `staff.switch_in` one. The edge guarantees a switch-in token
 * (`@StaffOnly()`); the RPC trusts its caller.
 */
@CommandHandler(StaffSwitchOutCommand)
export class StaffSwitchOutHandler implements ICommandHandler<StaffSwitchOutCommand> {
  constructor(
    private readonly denylist: AccessTokenDenylist,
    private readonly audit: TenantAuditLog,
  ) {}

  async execute(command: StaffSwitchOutCommand): Promise<void> {
    await this.denylist.deny(command.accessToken.jti, command.accessToken.exp);
    await this.audit.record({
      tenantId: command.tenantId,
      actorId: command.staffUserId,
      type: AUDIT_EVENT_TYPES.STAFF_SWITCH_OUT,
      data: { accessTokenJti: command.accessToken.jti },
    });
  }
}
```

`identity-core.module.ts`: `IDENTITY_COMMAND_HANDLERS` gains `StaffSwitchOutHandler`; `src/index.ts`:
`export { StaffSwitchOutCommand } from './application/commands/staff-switch-out/staff-switch-out.command.js';`.

```ts
it('switch-out revokes the switch-in token and records staff.switch_out; a normal token gets 403 STAFF_ONLY', async () => {
  const owner = await register('owner.out@example.com');
  const support = await register('support.out@example.com');
  setStaff(support.user.id, true);
  const switched = await inject({
    method: 'POST',
    url: `/v1/staff/tenants/${owner.tenantId}/switch-in`,
    headers: bearer(support.accessToken),
    payload: { reason: 'ticket #9' },
  });
  const staffToken = switched.json<{ accessToken: string }>().accessToken;

  const normal = await inject({
    method: 'POST',
    url: '/v1/staff/switch-out',
    headers: bearer(support.accessToken),
  });
  expect(normal.statusCode).toBe(403);
  expect(normal.json<{ code: string }>().code).toBe('STAFF_ONLY');

  const out = await inject({
    method: 'POST',
    url: '/v1/staff/switch-out',
    headers: bearer(staffToken),
  });
  expect(out.statusCode, out.body).toBe(204);
  const after = await inject({ method: 'GET', url: '/v1/auth/me', headers: bearer(staffToken) });
  expect(after.statusCode).toBe(401);
  expect(after.json<{ code: string }>().code).toBe('TOKEN_REVOKED');
  expect(
    e2e.identity.store.auditEvents
      .filter((event) => event.tenantId === owner.tenantId)
      .map((event) => event.type),
  ).toEqual(['staff.switch_in', 'staff.switch_out']);
  // The staff user's own token still works.
  expect(
    (await inject({ method: 'GET', url: '/v1/auth/me', headers: bearer(support.accessToken) }))
      .statusCode,
  ).toBe(200);
});
```

### Task 17: The principal in CLS at every edge (B2-22, part 2)

**Files:**

- Create: `libs/auth/src/context/principal-context.ts`, `libs/auth/src/context/principal-context.interceptor.ts`,
  `libs/auth/src/context/principal-context.spec.ts`, `apps/monolith/test/principal.e2e-spec.ts`
- Modify: `libs/auth/package.json` (+ `bun.lock`), `libs/auth/src/guards/jwt-auth.guard.ts` (+ spec),
  `libs/auth/src/auth.module.ts` (+ spec), `libs/auth/src/index.ts`, `libs/auth/README.md`,
  `libs/observability/src/context/request-context.service.ts`, `libs/observability/src/context/request-context.spec.ts`,
  `libs/bootstrap/src/integration.smoke.spec.ts`, `domains/identity/src/application/services/session-tokens.service.ts`
  (+ spec), `domains/identity/src/application/commands/login/login.handler.ts` (+ spec)

**Interfaces:** Consumes I-1 (`createPrincipal`, `getPrincipal`, `setPrincipal`, `runWithPrincipal`), Task 10
(`AuthUser.tenantId`, `staff`). Produces I-2's core writers: `principalOf(user: AuthUser): Principal` and
`PrincipalContextInterceptor` (`@platform/auth` root), the internal `writePrincipal(user: AuthUser): void`,
`JwtAuthGuard` writing after authentication, `AuthModule.forRootAsync({ globalGuards: true })` registering
`PrincipalContextInterceptor` as an `APP_INTERCEPTOR`, `RequestContextService.userId` reading the principal first,
`SessionTokensService.open()` setting `{ userId, tenantId, roles, staff: false }`, and `LoginHandler` running
tenant selection inside `runWithPrincipal({ userId, tenantId: null })`.

**Wave:** 3, Lane C (`b2/context`), after `b2/tokens` (Task 13) is merged; parallel with Lanes F and O.

Writer rules (I-2): write only inside an active CLS context, never open one at the edge; the interceptor writes only
when `getPrincipal()` is still `undefined`; never replace a principal with one of another user: a writer that finds
another user's principal throws 401 `INVALID_PRINCIPAL` (reason `principal mismatch`) instead of keeping it, so
authorization (`req.user`) and the CLS principal (audit `actorId`, B3's RLS) can never name different users (fail
closed; no legitimate flow reaches it: the guard writes once per request, later GraphQL root fields skip it, and the
session writers run in `@Public()` flows or as the same user). A token whose `sub`
is not a UUID cannot become a principal (`createPrincipal` throws 401 `INVALID_PRINCIPAL`): identity always issues
uuidv7 subjects, so only hand-made test tokens hit this. Interceptor order: `ObservabilityModule` (its
`RequestContextInterceptor` opens the CLS context for Socket.IO messages and graphql-ws operations) is imported
before `AuthModule` in every edge app (`apps/monolith/src/app.module.ts`, `apps/gateway/src/app.module.ts`), so it
is the outer interceptor and `PrincipalContextInterceptor` runs inside the context it opened; the e2e test below
pins that for GraphQL over HTTP and REST, and the unit spec for `ws`.

```ts
import type { CallHandler } from '@nestjs/common';
import { getPrincipal, PLATFORM_TENANT_ID, UnauthenticatedException } from '@platform/common';
import { ClsServiceManager } from 'nestjs-cls';
import { lastValueFrom, of } from 'rxjs';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { executionContext } from '../guards/execution-context.test.js';
import { makeAuthUser } from '../testing/auth-test.utils.js';
import { principalOf, writePrincipal } from './principal-context.js';
import { PrincipalContextInterceptor } from './principal-context.interceptor.js';

const cls = ClsServiceManager.getClsService();
const inContext = <T>(fn: () => T): T => cls.run(fn);

describe('principalOf / writePrincipal', () => {
  it('maps an AuthUser to a frozen principal of its tenant', () => {
    const user = makeAuthUser({ roles: ['admin'], staff: true });
    expect(principalOf(user)).toEqual({
      userId: user.id,
      tenantId: user.tenantId,
      roles: ['admin'],
      staff: true,
    });
    expect(Object.isFrozen(principalOf(user))).toBe(true);
  });

  it('is a no-op outside a CLS context (never opens one)', () => {
    expect(() => writePrincipal(makeAuthUser())).not.toThrow();
    expect(getPrincipal()).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('writes inside a context; the same user may move tenant; another user is refused (fail closed)', () => {
    inContext(() => {
      const ada = makeAuthUser();
      writePrincipal(ada);
      expect(getPrincipal()).toMatchObject({ userId: ada.id, tenantId: ada.tenantId });
      const moved = { ...ada, tenantId: '0190f3b2-0000-7000-8000-0000000000a2' };
      writePrincipal(moved);
      expect(getPrincipal()?.tenantId).toBe(moved.tenantId);
      expect(() => writePrincipal(makeAuthUser())).toThrow(UnauthenticatedException);
      expect(getPrincipal()?.userId).toBe(ada.id);
    });
  });

  it('refuses a user that cannot be a principal (platform tenant, non-UUID id): 401 INVALID_PRINCIPAL', () => {
    inContext(() => {
      expect(() => writePrincipal(makeAuthUser({ tenantId: PLATFORM_TENANT_ID }))).toThrow(
        UnauthenticatedException,
      );
      expect(() => writePrincipal(makeAuthUser({ id: 'u-1' }))).toThrow(UnauthenticatedException);
    });
  });
});

describe('PrincipalContextInterceptor', () => {
  const interceptor = new PrincipalContextInterceptor();
  const next = (probe: () => unknown): CallHandler => ({ handle: () => of(probe()) });

  it('writes socket.data.user inside the context RequestContextInterceptor opened (ws)', async () => {
    const user = makeAuthUser();
    const seen = await inContext(() =>
      lastValueFrom(
        interceptor.intercept(
          executionContext('ws', { handler: () => undefined, cls: Object }, { data: { user } }),
          next(() => getPrincipal()),
        ),
      ),
    );
    expect(seen).toMatchObject({ userId: user.id, tenantId: user.tenantId });
  });

  it('keeps a principal the guard already wrote; skips rpc; passes through without a user', async () => {
    const first = makeAuthUser();
    await inContext(async () => {
      writePrincipal(first);
      const other = makeAuthUser();
      const seen = await lastValueFrom(
        interceptor.intercept(
          executionContext(
            'http',
            { handler: () => undefined, cls: Object },
            { headers: {}, user: other },
          ),
          next(() => getPrincipal()?.userId),
        ),
      );
      expect(seen).toBe(first.id);
    });
    const rpc = await inContext(() =>
      lastValueFrom(
        interceptor.intercept(
          executionContext(
            'rpc',
            { handler: () => undefined, cls: Object },
            { headers: {}, user: first },
          ),
          next(() => getPrincipal()),
        ),
      ),
    );
    expect(rpc).toBeUndefined();
    const anonymous = await inContext(() =>
      lastValueFrom(
        interceptor.intercept(
          executionContext('http', { handler: () => undefined, cls: Object }, { headers: {} }),
          next(() => getPrincipal()),
        ),
      ),
    );
    expect(anonymous).toBeUndefined();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement.** `libs/auth/src/context/principal-context.ts`:

```ts
import {
  createPrincipal,
  getPrincipal,
  type Principal,
  setPrincipal,
  TenancyErrorCode,
  UnauthenticatedException,
} from '@platform/common';
import { ClsServiceManager } from 'nestjs-cls';
import type { AuthUser } from '../types/auth-user.js';

/** The request principal (spec §7) of an authenticated `AuthUser`. @throws 401 INVALID_PRINCIPAL */
export function principalOf(user: AuthUser): Principal {
  return createPrincipal({
    userId: user.id,
    tenantId: user.tenantId,
    roles: user.roles,
    staff: user.staff,
  });
}

/**
 * Edge writer of `CLS_PRINCIPAL` (I-2): only inside an active CLS context (never opens one: that
 * would hide the request id), and never over a principal of ANOTHER user: that throws (fail closed),
 * since `req.user` and the principal must name the same user. The same user may move tenant
 * (switch-tenant re-authenticates the request with the new token's tenant).
 * @throws UnauthenticatedException INVALID_PRINCIPAL (another user's principal, or an invalid one)
 */
export function writePrincipal(user: AuthUser): void {
  if (!ClsServiceManager.getClsService().isActive()) return;
  const current = getPrincipal();
  if (current !== undefined && current.userId !== user.id) {
    throw new UnauthenticatedException('Principal mismatch', {
      code: TenancyErrorCode.INVALID_PRINCIPAL,
      details: { reason: 'principal mismatch' },
    });
  }
  setPrincipal(principalOf(user));
}
```

`libs/auth/src/context/principal-context.interceptor.ts`:

```ts
import {
  type CallHandler,
  type ExecutionContext,
  Injectable,
  type NestInterceptor,
} from '@nestjs/common';
import { getContextType, getPrincipal, getRequest, type RequestLike } from '@platform/common';
import type { Observable } from 'rxjs';
import { isAuthUser } from '../tokens/token-claims.js';
import { writePrincipal } from './principal-context.js';

/**
 * Writes the principal for Socket.IO messages and graphql-ws operations, whose CLS context
 * `RequestContextInterceptor` (@platform/observability) opens only after the guards ran. Registered
 * by `AuthModule` (global guards on); apps import ObservabilityModule first, so this runs inside that
 * context. Idempotent: an HTTP request already got its principal from `JwtAuthGuard`.
 */
@Injectable()
export class PrincipalContextInterceptor implements NestInterceptor {
  intercept(context: ExecutionContext, next: CallHandler): Observable<unknown> {
    if (getContextType(context) !== 'rpc' && getPrincipal() === undefined) {
      const user = getRequest<RequestLike>(context)?.user;
      if (isAuthUser(user)) writePrincipal(user);
    }
    return next.handle();
  }
}
```

```ts
return allowed;
```

```ts
  /**
   * Authenticated user of the current request: the CLS principal (B2, spec §7), else the legacy
   * `CLS_USER_ID` the gRPC metadata interceptor still sets until B2-23 retires it.
   */
  get userId(): string | undefined {
    if (!this.cls.isActive()) return undefined;
    return getPrincipal()?.userId ?? this.cls.get<string | undefined>(CLS_USER_ID);
  }
```

```ts
// I-2: once a session is opened, the unit of work runs as that user in that tenant. Another
// user's principal is never replaced: fail closed (the caller's transaction rolls back).
if (ClsServiceManager.getClsService().isActive()) {
  const current = getPrincipal();
  if (current !== undefined && current.userId !== input.user.id) {
    throw new UnauthenticatedException('Principal mismatch', {
      code: TenancyErrorCode.INVALID_PRINCIPAL,
      details: { reason: 'principal mismatch' },
    });
  }
  setPrincipal(
    createPrincipal({ userId: input.user.id, tenantId: input.tenantId, roles: input.roles }),
  );
}
```

      `expect(response.json()).toMatchObject({ principal: { userId: claims.sub, tenantId: TEST_TENANT_ID, roles: ['member'], staff: false } });`.
      `apps/monolith/test/principal.e2e-spec.ts`:

```ts
/*
 * The request principal reaches the handlers of the real monolith (spec §7): REST and GraphQL over
 * HTTP (JwtAuthGuard writes it in the ClsMiddleware context).
 */
await vi.hoisted(async () => {
  const { MONOLITH_E2E_ENV } = await import('./support/e2e-env.js');
  Object.assign(process.env, MONOLITH_E2E_ENV);
});

import { UsersPort } from '@domain/identity';
import type { ApplicationConfig } from '@nestjs/core';
import { getPrincipal, type Principal } from '@platform/common';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { bearer, graphql } from './support/http.js';
import { createMonolithE2eApp, type MonolithE2e } from './support/monolith-e2e-app.js';

describe('principal in CLS (monolith)', () => {
  let e2e: MonolithE2e;
  let tokens: { accessToken: string; tenantId: string; user: { id: string } };
  const seen: (Principal | undefined)[] = [];

  beforeAll(async () => {
    e2e = await createMonolithE2eApp();
    const ports = e2e.app.get(UsersPort);
    const getUser = ports.getUser.bind(ports);
    vi.spyOn(ports, 'getUser').mockImplementation(
      async (...args: Parameters<UsersPort['getUser']>) => {
        seen.push(getPrincipal());
        return getUser(...args);
      },
    );
    const listUsers = ports.listUsers.bind(ports);
    vi.spyOn(ports, 'listUsers').mockImplementation(
      async (...args: Parameters<UsersPort['listUsers']>) => {
        seen.push(getPrincipal());
        return listUsers(...args);
      },
    );
    const res = await e2e.app.inject({
      method: 'POST',
      url: '/v1/auth/register',
      payload: {
        email: 'cls.principal@example.com',
        password: 'correct horse battery staple',
        displayName: 'Cls',
      },
    });
    tokens = res.json();
  });

  afterAll(async () => {
    await e2e?.app.close();
  });

  it('REST: GET /v1/auth/me runs with { userId, tenantId, roles, staff } of the token', async () => {
    seen.length = 0;
    const res = await e2e.app.inject({
      method: 'GET',
      url: '/v1/auth/me',
      headers: bearer(tokens.accessToken),
    });
    expect(res.statusCode, res.body).toBe(200);
    expect(seen).toEqual([
      { userId: tokens.user.id, tenantId: tokens.tenantId, roles: ['owner'], staff: false },
    ]);
  });

  it('GraphQL over HTTP: two root fields of one operation see the same principal', async () => {
    seen.length = 0;
    const res = await e2e.app.inject(
      graphql(
        '{ a: users(limit: 1) { items { id } } b: users(limit: 2) { items { id } } }',
        undefined,
        bearer(tokens.accessToken),
      ),
    );
    expect(res.json()).not.toHaveProperty('errors');
    expect(seen).toEqual([
      { userId: tokens.user.id, tenantId: tokens.tenantId, roles: ['owner'], staff: false },
      { userId: tokens.user.id, tenantId: tokens.tenantId, roles: ['owner'], staff: false },
    ]);
  });

  it('Socket.IO / graphql-ws: PrincipalContextInterceptor runs inside the context RequestContextInterceptor opens', () => {
    // Global interceptors nest in registration order (module scan order): ObservabilityModule is
    // imported before AuthModule, so RequestContextInterceptor must come first. Reversed, ws
    // messages and graphql-ws operations would silently run without a principal.
    const config = Reflect.get(e2e.app, 'config') as ApplicationConfig;
    const order = config.getGlobalInterceptors().map((interceptor) => interceptor.constructor.name);
    const outer = order.indexOf('RequestContextInterceptor');
    expect(outer).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
    expect(order.indexOf('PrincipalContextInterceptor')).toBeGreaterThan(outer);
  });
});
```

(`users` is uncached and needs `users:read`, which the registrant holds as the owner of its workspace; the
second root field skips passport, so this pins the "first field's write stands" rule of I-2. The last test pins
the interceptor order that Socket.IO and graphql-ws depend on: `@nestjs/core` `scanner.js`
`applyApplicationProviders` pushes default-scoped `APP_INTERCEPTOR` instances in module-scan order and
`NestApplication` keeps them on its `config` (`nest-application.js:46`). No core spec drives a real Socket.IO or
graphql-ws client; B2-28 (edge) adds those e2e checks.)
Run (sandbox off): `npx vitest run --project bootstrap --project monolith:e2e --project gateway:e2e` → PASS.

```ts
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { JwtService } from '@nestjs/jwt';
import { authConfig } from '@platform/config';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { refreshTokenConfig } from './refresh-token.config.js';
import { TokenIssuer } from './token-issuer.service.js';

/**
 * AUDIT-F26: docs/SECURITY.md must name every claim identity actually issues, and must not present
 * code constants as environment variables.
 */
const ROOT = join(import.meta.dirname, '..', '..', '..', '..', '..');
const security = readFileSync(join(ROOT, 'docs', 'SECURITY.md'), 'utf8');
const envExample = readFileSync(join(ROOT, '.env.example'), 'utf8');
const configuration = readFileSync(join(ROOT, 'docs', 'CONFIGURATION.md'), 'utf8');
const tableRow = (label: string): string => {
  const row = security.split('\n').find((line) => line.startsWith(`| ${label} `));
  if (row === undefined) throw new Error(`SECURITY.md has no token row "${label}"`);
  return row;
};

const jwt = new JwtService();
const issuer = new TokenIssuer(
  jwt,
  authConfig.parse({ NODE_ENV: 'test' }),
  refreshTokenConfig.parse({ NODE_ENV: 'test' }),
);
const subject = {
  id: '0190f3b2-0000-7000-8000-000000000001',
  email: 'a@b.c',
  tenantId: '0190f3b2-0000-7000-8000-0000000000aa',
  roles: ['admin'] as const,
  staff: true,
};

describe('docs/SECURITY.md token table (AUDIT-F26)', () => {
  it('the Access row names every claim of an access token (a staff one: the largest claim set)', async () => {
    const claims = jwt.decode<Record<string, unknown>>(
      (await issuer.issueAccessToken(subject)).token,
    );
    const row = tableRow('Access');
    for (const claim of Object.keys(claims)) expect(row, claim).toContain(`\`${claim}\``);
  });

  it('the Refresh row names every claim of a refresh token', async () => {
    const issued = await issuer.issueRefreshToken({
      userId: subject.id,
      sessionId: '0190f3b2-0000-7000-8000-000000000002',
    });
    const row = tableRow('Refresh');
    for (const claim of Object.keys(jwt.decode<Record<string, unknown>>(issued.token))) {
      expect(row, claim).toContain(`\`${claim}\``);
    }
  });

  it.each(['JWT_CLOCK_TOLERANCE_SEC', 'STRIPE_WEBHOOK_TOLERANCE_SEC'])(
    '%s is a code constant: never in .env.example or CONFIGURATION.md, and called a constant in SECURITY.md',
    (name) => {
      expect(envExample).not.toContain(name);
      expect(configuration).not.toContain(name);
      const mentions = security.split('\n').filter((line) => line.includes(name));
      expect(mentions.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      for (const line of mentions) expect(line).toMatch(/code constant/);
    },
  );
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Rewrite the docs.** `docs/SECURITY.md`, section "Tokens": replace the table and the bullet about
      `iss`/`aud` with

```markdown
| Token   | Algorithm / secret                       | TTL (env)                                                                       | Claims                                                                                                                                                      | Stored server-side                                                   |
| ------- | ---------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Access  | HS256, `JWT_ACCESS_SECRET` (≥ 32 chars)  | 900 s (`JWT_ACCESS_TTL_SEC`); staff switch-in 900 s (`STAFF_SWITCH_IN_TTL_SEC`) | `sub` (user id), `email`, `tid` (active tenant), `roles` (tenant roles), `staff` (switch-in tokens only), `jti` (uuidv7), `typ`, `iss`, `aud`, `iat`, `exp` | no (verified locally); `jti` denylisted on logout                    |
| Refresh | HS256, `JWT_REFRESH_SECRET` (≥ 32 chars) | 604800 s (`JWT_REFRESH_TTL_SEC`)                                                | `sub`, `jti` = session id, `typ`, `iss`, `aud`, `iat`, `exp`                                                                                                | `sessions` row (with its tenant) holding `sha256(refreshToken)` only |

- `iss`/`aud` default to `nestjs-boilerplate` (`JWT_ISSUER`, `JWT_AUDIENCE`). Verification allows 5 s of
  clock skew: the code constant `JWT_CLOCK_TOLERANCE_SEC` (`libs/auth/src/auth.constants.ts`), not an
  environment variable.
- `tid` is required: a token without it (issued before B2) is 401 `INVALID_TOKEN`; the client refreshes.
  `roles` are the roles held in `tid` (owner ⊇ admin ⊇ member); unknown values are dropped. `staff` is
  written only on staff switch-in tokens and counts only as the JSON boolean `true`. Permissions are never in
  the token: each service resolves them from `roles` at verification.
- The refresh token carries no tenant: its session row does, so a refresh stays in the session's tenant.
```

In "Stripe webhook" replace `stale (> 300 s, \`STRIPE_WEBHOOK_TOLERANCE_SEC\`)`with
 `stale (> 300 s, the code constant \`STRIPE_WEBHOOK_TOLERANCE_SEC\` in \`domains/billing/src/infrastructure/stripe/stripe.constants.ts\`)`   and keep the rest of that line. (If either replacement leaves a line naming the constant without the words
  "code constant", reword that line so it has them: the spec checks every mention.)
  `docs/API.md`, "Authentication and authorization": replace the paragraph "Roles only group permissions. … Role
changes take effect on the user's next token refresh." and the role × permission table with

```markdown
Every access token belongs to one tenant (`tid`) and carries your roles there (`owner` ⊇ `admin` ⊇ `member`); the
API acts inside that tenant only. Pick the tenant at login (`tenantId`) or switch with
`POST /v1/auth/switch-tenant`. Endpoints check permissions (`@RequirePermissions` needs all of the listed
permissions, `@RequireAnyPermission` at least one); each domain declares its permissions and the roles that get
them by default. Role changes take effect on your next token refresh.

| Permission            | `member` | `admin` | `owner` |
| --------------------- | :------: | :-----: | :-----: |
| `users:read`          |          |   yes   |   yes   |
| `members:manage`      |          |   yes   |   yes   |
| `tenant:manage`       |          |         |   yes   |
| `notifications:read`  |   yes    |   yes   |   yes   |
| `notifications:write` |          |   yes   |   yes   |
| `billing:checkout`    |   yes    |   yes   |   yes   |
| `billing:read-all`    |          |         |         |
| `files:read`          |   yes    |   yes   |   yes   |
| `files:write`         |   yes    |   yes   |   yes   |
| `files:manage`        |          |         |         |

`billing:read-all` and `files:manage` are declared but granted to nobody until payments and files are scoped to the
tenant. Platform staff enter a tenant with a short-lived switch-in token (`admin` + `staff`); see
[SECURITY.md](SECURITY.md#platform-staff-switch-in).
```

postgres.js. A revocation stops new switch-ins at once (the handler re-reads the flag, Task 15); a switch-in token
issued before it stays valid until it expires (at most `STAFF_SWITCH_IN_TTL_SEC`) or is switched out (Task 16).

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { runStaffGrantCli, type StaffGrantDeps, type StaffStore } from './staff-grant.js';

const ID = '0190f3b2-0000-7000-8000-000000000001';

function harness(env: Record<string, string | undefined>, found: string | null = ID) {
  const out: string[] = [];
  const err: string[] = [];
  const store: StaffStore = {
    setPlatformStaff: vi.fn(async () => found),
    close: vi.fn(async () => undefined),
  };
  const connect = vi.fn((_url: string) => store);
  const deps: StaffGrantDeps = {
    env,
    connect,
    out: (line) => out.push(line),
    err: (line) => err.push(line),
  };
  return { deps, store, connect, out, err };
}

describe('staff:grant', () => {
  it('grants the flag to the normalised email over DATABASE_URL and exits 0', async () => {
    const h = harness({ DATABASE_URL: 'postgres://app@db/app' });
    await expect(runStaffGrantCli(['  Ada@Example.COM '], h.deps)).resolves.toBe(0);
    expect(h.connect).toHaveBeenCalledWith('postgres://app@db/app');
    expect(h.store.setPlatformStaff).toHaveBeenCalledWith('ada@example.com', true);
    expect(h.out).toEqual([`Granted platform staff to ada@example.com (${ID}).`]);
    expect(h.store.close).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('prefers DATABASE_MIGRATION_URL (the owner role, B3-3) and --revoke clears the flag', async () => {
    const h = harness({
      DATABASE_URL: 'postgres://app@db/app',
      DATABASE_MIGRATION_URL: 'postgres://owner@db/app',
    });
    await expect(runStaffGrantCli(['ada@example.com', '--revoke'], h.deps)).resolves.toBe(0);
    expect(h.connect).toHaveBeenCalledWith('postgres://owner@db/app');
    expect(h.store.setPlatformStaff).toHaveBeenCalledWith('ada@example.com', false);
    expect(h.out).toEqual([`Revoked platform staff from ada@example.com (${ID}).`]);
  });

  it('exit 1 for an unknown email; the connection is still closed', async () => {
    const h = harness({ DATABASE_URL: 'postgres://app@db/app' }, null);
    await expect(runStaffGrantCli(['nobody@example.com'], h.deps)).resolves.toBe(1);
    expect(h.err).toEqual(['No user with email nobody@example.com.']);
    expect(h.store.close).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it.each([
    [[], /Usage: staff:grant <email> \[--revoke\]/],
    [['not-an-email'], /Usage/],
    [['a@b.c', 'd@e.f'], /Usage/],
    [['a@b.c', '--force'], /Usage/],
  ])('exit 2 for arguments %j, nothing connected', async (args, message) => {
    const h = harness({ DATABASE_URL: 'postgres://app@db/app' });
    await expect(runStaffGrantCli(args, h.deps)).resolves.toBe(2);
    expect(h.err.join('\n')).toMatch(message);
    expect(h.connect).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('exit 2 without any database URL', async () => {
    const h = harness({});
    await expect(runStaffGrantCli(['ada@example.com'], h.deps)).resolves.toBe(2);
    expect(h.err).toEqual(['Set DATABASE_MIGRATION_URL or DATABASE_URL.']);
  });

  it('exit 1 and the error message (never the URL) when the database fails', async () => {
    const h = harness({ DATABASE_URL: 'postgres://app:secret@db/app' });
    vi.mocked(h.store.setPlatformStaff).mockRejectedValue(new Error('connect ECONNREFUSED'));
    await expect(runStaffGrantCli(['ada@example.com'], h.deps)).resolves.toBe(1);
    expect(h.err).toEqual(['staff:grant failed: connect ECONNREFUSED']);
    expect(h.err.join('\n')).not.toContain('secret');
    expect(h.store.close).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement.** `domains/identity/src/cli/staff-grant.ts`:

```ts
import { normalizeEmail } from '../domain/email.js';
import { createPostgresStaffStore } from './postgres-staff-store.js';

/** The one write the CLI needs: `users.is_platform_staff` by email. */
export interface StaffStore {
  /** @returns the user id, or null when no user has that (normalised) email */
  setPlatformStaff(email: string, staff: boolean): Promise<string | null>;
  close(): Promise<void>;
}

export interface StaffGrantDeps {
  readonly env: Readonly<Record<string, string | undefined>>;
  connect(url: string): StaffStore;
  out(line: string): void;
  err(line: string): void;
}

const USAGE = 'Usage: staff:grant <email> [--revoke]';
const looksLikeEmail = (value: string): boolean => /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+$/.test(value);

const defaultDeps = (): StaffGrantDeps => ({
  env: process.env,
  connect: createPostgresStaffStore,
  out: (line) => console.info(line),
  err: (line) => console.error(line),
});

/**
 * `bun run --filter @domain/identity staff:grant <email> [--revoke]` (spec §5.2, §9 method 7): the only
 * way to grant or revoke platform staff. Uses DATABASE_MIGRATION_URL (B3-3's owner role) when set,
 * else DATABASE_URL. Exit codes: 0 done, 1 unknown email or database failure, 2 usage/configuration.
 */
export async function runStaffGrantCli(
  args: readonly string[] = process.argv.slice(2),
  deps: StaffGrantDeps = defaultDeps(),
): Promise<number> {
  const flags = args.filter((arg) => arg.startsWith('--'));
  const positional = args.filter((arg) => !arg.startsWith('--'));
  const email = positional.length === 1 ? normalizeEmail(positional[0] ?? '') : '';
  if (!looksLikeEmail(email) || flags.some((flag) => flag !== '--revoke')) {
    deps.err(USAGE);
    return 2;
  }
  const staff = !flags.includes('--revoke');
  const url = deps.env.DATABASE_MIGRATION_URL || deps.env.DATABASE_URL;
  if (!url) {
    deps.err('Set DATABASE_MIGRATION_URL or DATABASE_URL.');
    return 2;
  }

  const store = deps.connect(url);
  try {
    const id = await store.setPlatformStaff(email, staff);
    if (id === null) {
      deps.err(`No user with email ${email}.`);
      return 1;
    }
    deps.out(
      staff
        ? `Granted platform staff to ${email} (${id}).`
        : `Revoked platform staff from ${email} (${id}).`,
    );
    return 0;
  } catch (error) {
    deps.err(`staff:grant failed: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`);
    return 1;
  } finally {
    await store.close();
  }
}
```

`domains/identity/src/cli/postgres-staff-store.ts`:

```ts
import postgres from 'postgres';
import type { StaffStore } from './staff-grant.js';

/** postgres.js over one connection; `email` is already normalised (users.email stores that form). */
export function createPostgresStaffStore(url: string): StaffStore {
  const sql = postgres(url, { max: 1, onnotice: () => undefined });
  return {
    async setPlatformStaff(email, staff) {
      const rows = await sql<{ id: string }[]>`
        update identity.users
           set is_platform_staff = ${staff}, updated_at = now()
         where email = ${email}
     returning id`;
      return rows[0]?.id ?? null;
    },
    close: () => sql.end({ timeout: 5 }),
  };
}
```

`domains/identity/src/cli/staff-grant.main.ts`:

```ts
process.exitCode = await runStaffGrantCli();
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Integration (real Postgres).** `domains/identity/src/cli/staff-grant.int-spec.ts`:

```ts
import { sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { type IdentityDatabase, startIdentityDatabase } from '../../test/identity-database.js';
import { createPostgresStaffStore } from './postgres-staff-store.js';
import { runStaffGrantCli } from './staff-grant.js';

describe('staff:grant against Postgres', () => {
  let database: IdentityDatabase;
  let url: string;

  beforeAll(async () => {
    database = await startIdentityDatabase([]);
    url = process.env.DATABASE_URL ?? '';
    await database.db.execute(
      sql`insert into identity.users (email, password_hash, display_name) values ('ops@example.com', 'x', 'Ops')`,
    );
  }, 180_000);

  afterAll(async () => {
    await database?.close();
  });

  const flag = async (): Promise<boolean | undefined> => {
    const [row] = await database.db.execute<{ is_platform_staff: boolean }>(
      sql`select is_platform_staff from identity.users where email = 'ops@example.com'`,
    );
    return row?.is_platform_staff;
  };

  it('grants and revokes is_platform_staff by email; unknown emails exit 1', async () => {
    const deps = {
      env: { DATABASE_URL: url },
      connect: createPostgresStaffStore,
      out: () => undefined,
      err: () => undefined,
    };
    await expect(runStaffGrantCli(['OPS@example.com'], deps)).resolves.toBe(0);
    await expect(flag()).resolves.toBe(true);
    await expect(runStaffGrantCli(['ops@example.com', '--revoke'], deps)).resolves.toBe(0);
    await expect(flag()).resolves.toBe(false);
    await expect(runStaffGrantCli(['ghost@example.com'], deps)).resolves.toBe(1);
  });
});
```

Smoke the script once by hand against the dev database (optional, needs `bun run dev` infra):
`bun run --filter @domain/identity staff:grant you@example.com` → `Granted platform staff to …` or exit 1.

- [ ] **Step 7: Docs.** `docs/SECURITY.md`: replace the whole section "The first admin" (heading, text, SQL block,

````markdown
### The first admin (platform staff)

There is no seed account. Every registration creates the user's own workspace, where they are `owner`. Platform
staff (support access to other tenants, see [Platform staff switch-in](#platform-staff-switch-in)) is a flag only
this command sets, from a shell with database access:

```bash
bun run --filter @domain/identity staff:grant you@example.com           # grant
bun run --filter @domain/identity staff:grant you@example.com --revoke  # revoke
```

It connects with `DATABASE_MIGRATION_URL` when set, else `DATABASE_URL`, and exits 1 when no user has that email.
No API can grant staff. A revocation takes effect at the next switch-in (the flag is re-read every time).
````
