## Self-review

**Coverage.** Every inventory item in scope maps to a task (table above): B2-1 (3), B2-2 (4), B2-3 (5), B2-4 +
F16 (6), B2-5 (8 sessions, 11 users), B2-7 (7), B2-8 (8, 9), B2-9 (8), B2-10 (14), B2-11 (13, 15, 16), B2-15 + FU-7
(19), B2-18 (10, completed by 12), B2-19 + F26 (18), B2-20 (2; 10 withholds the cross-tenant grants; 11 removes
`users:manage-roles`; the `libs/auth/` exemption of `scripts/libs-vocabulary.spec.ts` goes in 2), B2-21 + F14 (2
drops `@Roles()` from the README stack row, 13 gives it tenant-role semantics, documents when to use it and proves
it with its own routes, since no B2 route uses it), B2-22 (1 kernel, 17 writers). Edge items (B2-6, B2-12..B2-14,
B2-16, B2-17, B2-23..B2-29) are out of scope.

**Names and signatures checked across tasks.**

- `SessionTokensService.open(input: OpenSessionInput)` (8) is called with `{ user, tenantId, roles, client,
sessionId? }` by register (8), login (8, wrapped in `runWithPrincipal` by 17), refresh (8) and switch (14); 10
  makes it sign `toAccessTokenSubject(user, tenantId, roles)`; 17 sets the principal at its end.
- `TokenIssuer.issueAccessToken(subject, options?: { ttlSec?: number })` (10) is used by `SessionTokensService`,
  `FakeAuthPort.issue` (10), `StaffSwitchInHandler` (15, `{ ttlSec }`, `staff: true`) and the parity spec (10); the
  `staff` claim is written only when true in both `TokenIssuer` and `signTestAccessToken`.
- `principal(app, roles, id?, tenantId?, staff?)` in `domains/identity/test/auth-test.module.ts`: 10 adds
  `tenantId`, 14 adds `staff` (used by 14, 15, 16 specs).
- `MembershipsRepository.find/touch` (4) are used by `TenantSelection` (8), refresh (8) and `SwitchTenantHandler`
  (14); `FakeIdentityState.membership/membershipsOf/addMembership` (8), `setMembershipRoles` (10), `grantStaff` and
  `auditEvents` (15) are used by the gateway e2e specs of 9, 10, 12, 14, 15, 16.
- `UsersRepository`: `isPlatformStaff` (11) is used by `StaffSwitchInHandler` (15); `findByIdInTenant` and
  `ListUsersCriteria.tenantId` (12) by the query handlers; `UsersPort.getUser(id, scope?)` (12) by the controller,
  the resolver and `UserReadCache.getScopedUser`.
- Error codes: `TENANT_ACCESS_DENIED`, `MEMBERSHIP_REVOKED` (8), `GLOBAL_ROLES_REMOVED` (11),
  `STAFF_TOKEN_NOT_ALLOWED` (14), `STAFF_ONLY` (`RbacErrorCode`, 13; `IdentityErrorCode` + `NotPlatformStaffException`,
  15), `NOT_FOUND` entity `Tenant` (15) match I-10.
- Proto field numbers match I-9: `AuthTokens` 6-7 (8), `LoginRequest` 4 (9), `GetUserRequest` 2 and
  `ListUsersRequest` 4 (12), `SwitchTenantRequest` 1-6 (14), `StaffSwitchInRequest` 1-4 and `StaffAccessToken` 1-5
  (15), `StaffSwitchOutRequest` 1-4 (16); `User.roles` and `UpdateUserRoles` are retired with comments only (11).
- Migrations: `0005` (8), `0006_platform_staff`, `0007_drop_user_roles` (11); next free identity index `0008`.

**Decisions taken here (none changes an Interface).**

1. `staff:grant` connects with `DATABASE_MIGRATION_URL` when set, else `DATABASE_URL` (inventory B2-15's open
   question; B3-3 introduces the first key). It is the only writer of `is_platform_staff`.
2. Staff switch-in to the nil UUID answers 404 like any unknown tenant (no tenant can have that id); a malformed id
   is 400 at the edge. A switch-in token may call switch-in again (the flag is re-read; I-10 is unchanged); it may
   not switch tenants (14).
3. Scoped user reads (`GET /v1/users/:id` for someone else, GraphQL `user(id)`) bypass `UserReadCache`; own-profile
   reads stay cached. `GetUsersByIds` stays unscoped until B2-25 (edge).
4. While `billing:read-all` and `files:manage` are withheld (10 → B2-26 / B4-3), the billing/files specs that showed
   admins reading everything now pin the 403, policy unit specs pass the permission explicitly, and both
   `payment-user.e2e-spec.ts` files query the caller's own payments (the fakes return the rows regardless).
5. The `staff` claim is omitted (not `false`) on normal tokens, in the issuer and the test signer alike.
6. Task 17 adds `nestjs-cls` to `libs/auth` (catalog 7.0.1) so `writePrincipal` can check for an active context
   without opening one.
7. A CLS writer that finds another user's principal throws 401 `INVALID_PRINCIPAL` (reason `principal mismatch`)
   instead of silently keeping it (Task 17: `writePrincipal`, `SessionTokensService.open()`). I-2's rule ("never
   replace") is unchanged; it is now enforced fail-closed so `req.user` and the principal never differ.
8. `@Roles()` has no production route in B2 (`PATCH /v1/tenant` uses `tenant:manage`, spec §5.2); Task 13 proves it
   through two routes of the bootstrap smoke app.
9. Socket.IO and graphql-ws principals are covered by `principal-context.spec.ts` (a hand-opened context) and the
   interceptor-order pin in `apps/monolith/test/principal.e2e-spec.ts`; no core spec drives a real Socket.IO or
   graphql-ws client (edge B2-28 adds those e2e checks).

**Risks for the executor.** Line numbers are hints from `9600510`; G, C wave 1, S0 and E land first and move
code (e.g. `@AuthThrottle()` becomes `@RateLimit(...)`): match edits on the quoted text, never on line numbers.
Task 10 edits about forty spec files by rule; its Step 6 table lists every file whose semantics change, and
`rg -n 'Role\.(User|Admin|Moderator)|legacyTenantRoles'` must end with only identity's global-role files (removed
in 11).

  role owns the table until B3. B3's policies (spec §8.2: SELECT and INSERT only) add the database side.

  without a tenant, non-canonical role order); it now mirrors `createPrincipal`, with tests.

fixed in place in Tasks 10-19, Spec coverage and Self-review; the Interfaces and Lane sections are
untouched (see "Notes for the controller").

### Executive summary (review, part 2)

- Interfaces I-1..I-12: unchanged. The edge plan's helpers keep their names and shapes (list below).
- Security, fixed: Task 17's CLS writers silently kept ANOTHER user's principal while `req.user`
  named the new one (authz and the future RLS/audit identity could differ). `writePrincipal` and
  `SessionTokensService.open()` now throw 401 `INVALID_PRINCIPAL` (`principal mismatch`); `open()`
  checks before writing the session row.
- Coverage, fixed: Socket.IO and graphql-ws get their principal only if `RequestContextInterceptor`
  wraps `PrincipalContextInterceptor`; a monolith e2e now pins the global interceptor order (Nest
  pushes `APP_INTERCEPTOR` instances in module-scan order, `scanner.js:355-374`). Real ws clients
  stay with edge B2-28.
- Task 13: the gate no longer waits for B2-12 (it uses `tenant:manage`, spec §5.2). `@Roles` and the
  owner ⊇ admin ⊇ member hierarchy are proven by the guard spec and two smoke-app routes through the
  real global guards.
- Task 11 executability: two int-spec tests read the dropped `roles` column / `user_role` type; a
  handler spec overrides `roles` on `UserRecord` (tsc); the `rg` gate hit `libs/transport` and two
  READMEs; `docs/SECURITY.md` was edited but not staged. All fixed.
- drizzle-kit 0.31.11 on a scratch copy: `0006` is exactly `ADD COLUMN "is_platform_staff" boolean
DEFAULT false NOT NULL`; `0007` emits `DROP COLUMN "roles"` then `DROP TYPE
"identity"."user_role"` with no prompt; a further run is a no-op. `bun run db:check` exists
  (`drizzle-kit check` per domain).
- Task 10: Step 6 rows aligned with part 1 (Task 2 already rewrote the moderator principals).
- Task 12: two missing imports (`graphql` in the monolith e2e, `TEST_TENANT_ID` in the GraphQL
  spec); Task 14: the switch e2e now proves the pre-switch refresh token is spent (401
  `REFRESH_TOKEN_REUSED`); Task 15: `docs/CONFIGURATION.md` namespace table row, and the port doc's
  400 → 422 for `DomainValidationException` (`domain.exception.ts:82`); Task 11/18: the API.md
  permission table hand-off; Task 19: Biome (not Prettier) formats `package.json`, a concrete README
  section, precise revocation wording.
- Verified with no change: claims parsing (tid required, nil UUID refused before the UUID check,
  staff only for the JSON `true`, issuer and test signer omit `staff` on normal tokens);
  switch-tenant (membership checked before any rotation, access token denylisted before the rotating
  transaction, reuse revokes every session, staff tokens refused at REST and GraphQL); switch-in
  (flag re-read first so non-staff cannot probe tenants, nil UUID passes `ParseUUIDPipe` and answers
  404, no refresh token, audit row before return); switch-out behind `@StaffOnly`; `UpdateUserRoles`
  kept and answering `FAILED_PRECONDITION` via `UsersServiceControllerMethods`; the CLI prefers
  `DATABASE_MIGRATION_URL`, is the only writer (`drizzle-users.repository.ts` updates only
  `password_hash`/`roles`), and `bun run --filter … staff:grant <email> --revoke` forwards both
  arguments and the exit code (bun 1.4.4, checked); `GLOBAL_ROLES_REMOVED` keeps 422 across gRPC.
- Commit messages of Tasks 10-19: all ten pass the repo's commitlint with no warning.
- Blockers: none. Must precede B2: unchanged from part 1.

### Interface changes (part 2)

None. I-1..I-12 are unchanged. Task 17 now enforces I-2's existing rule ("a writer never replaces a
principal with one of another user") by throwing 401 `INVALID_PRINCIPAL` instead of ignoring the
write; no signature, export or error code changes (`INVALID_PRINCIPAL` is I-10's). I-8's heading
says "root exports" while listing `runStaffGrantCli`; Task 19 keeps it internal to `src/cli` (I-11
does not export it), which is what the export map says.

### Helper names the edge plan depends on (as they stand)

- `FakeIdentityState` (`domains/identity/src/testing/fake-identity-ports.ts`, Task 8): `tenants:
Map<string, { id: string; name: string; personal: boolean }>`, `memberships: { tenantId: string;
userId: string; roles: TenantRole[]; lastActiveAt: number }[]`, `sessions: Map<string, { userId:
string; tenantId: string }>`, `membership(tenantId, userId)` (the mutable entry or `undefined`),
  `membershipsOf(userId)` (most recent first), `addMembership(tenantId, userId, roles:
TenantRole[]): void` (creates a non-personal tenant if missing), `removeMembership(tenantId,
userId): void`; Task 10: `setMembershipRoles(tenantId, userId, roles: TenantRole[]): void`; Task
  15: `staff: Set<string>`, `grantStaff(userId: string): void`, `auditEvents: { id: string;
tenantId: string; actorId: string | null; type: string; data: Record<string, unknown> }[]` (Task
  16 appends `staff.switch_out` rows); `calls: string[]` gains
  `AuthService.SwitchTenant|StaffSwitchIn|StaffSwitchOut`.
- In-memory persistence (`createInMemoryIdentityPersistence()`, Task 7): `store.users` values carry
  `isPlatformStaff: boolean` (Task 11; set it to grant staff in monolith e2e), `store.tenants`,
  `store.memberships` keyed by `membershipKey(tenantId, userId)` = `` `${tenantId}:${userId}` ``,
  `store.auditEvents: AuditEventRecord[]`; `InMemoryUsersRepository.isPlatformStaff(id):
Promise<boolean>` (Task 11) and `findByIdInTenant(id, tenantId)` (Task 12).
- `principal(app, roles: TenantRole[], id?, tenantId = TEST_TENANT_ID, staff = false)` in
  `domains/identity/test/auth-test.module.ts` (Tasks 10, 14).

  drop "13 (`@Roles`)" (B2-12 uses `IdentityPermission.TenantManage`; Task 13 is still a wave
  dependency, not an API one).
- Docs merge order inside wave 3: Tasks 18 (Lane C) and 19 (Lane O) link to
  `SECURITY.md#platform-staff-switch-in`, which Task 15 (Lane F) writes; if Lane C or O merges first
  the anchor dangles until Lane F lands (harmless; no link checker runs in `bun run check`).
- `libs/auth/package.json` (Task 17) and `domains/identity/package.json` (Task 19) both change
  `bun.lock`; per the ruling, a conflict is resolved by re-running `bun install`.
